6.1 仲裁应以当事人之间约定的语言进行。

6.2 如无约定，仲裁庭应考虑到所有相关情况决定语言。

7.1 希望启动仲裁的当事人应向CAM-CCBC提交仲裁申请书，内容包括：
(a) 包含仲裁协议的合同或文件；
(b) 参与仲裁的当事人的完整姓名和身份证明，包括其通知地址和/或电子邮件；
(c) 授权委托书（如有）；
(d) 仲裁事项和请求的简要说明；
(e) 其请求的估计金额；
(f) 仲裁地、语言和适用于仲裁的法律规则的选择；
(g) 关于仲裁员人数和选择的建议或其他相关信息，或当事人规定的指定方式；和
(h) 关于关联方和第三方资助的信息（根据第9.5条和第9.6条）。

7.2 当事人应随仲裁申请书附上费用表规定的登记费支付证明。

8.1 CAM-CCBC秘书处应将仲裁申请书及其支持文件通知对方当事人，并要求其在15（十五）天内提交答复，答复内容包括：
(a) 授权委托书（如有）；
(b) 通知地址和/或电子邮件的确认；
(c) 其请求事项和所涉金额的简要说明（如有）；
(d) 关于仲裁地、语言和适用于仲裁的法律规则的评论；
(e) 如适用，关于追加当事人的请求（第18条）；
(f) 关于仲裁员人数和选择的建议或其他相关信息，或当事人约定的指定方式；和
(g) 关于关联方和第三方资助的信息（根据第9.5条和第9.6条）。

8.2 即使任何当事人缺席，程序仍可继续进行，只要其已根据第3.3条被正式通知。

9.1 仲裁员名单上的成员和/或其他人员可由当事人指定为仲裁员，但须始终遵守本规则、CAM-CCBC道德准则以及独立性和公正性的要求。

9.2 仲裁员必须在整个仲裁过程中保持独立和公正。

9.3 仲裁员必须立即向秘书处和当事人披露可能引起对其公正性或独立性产生合理怀疑的任何事实或情况。

9.4 仲裁员可随时声明回避并拒绝接受指定，或提出辞任。

9.5 当事人必须披露可能与仲裁相关的任何自然人或法人实体，以便仲裁员核实是否存在利益冲突。

9.6 当事人必须及时披露第三方资助的存在，以便仲裁员核实并披露任何利益冲突。

10.1 如当事人在仲裁协议中未规定程序中担任仲裁员的人数，或未就此达成协议，CAM-CCBC主席应考虑到争议的复杂程度和金额，决定指定一名或三名仲裁员。

10.2 "仲裁庭"一词适用于独任仲裁员或三人仲裁庭。

11.1 如当事人已同意争议由三名仲裁员解决，或由CAM-CCBC主席如此决定，秘书处应向双方当事人转发本规则副本和仲裁员名单，邀请当事人在15（十五）天内各指定1（一）名仲裁员组成仲裁庭。

11.2 当事人可自由指定将组成仲裁庭的仲裁员。指定仲裁员名单以外的人选须附上其简历，并提交CAM-CCBC主席批准。

11.3 CAM-CCBC秘书处将通知当事人和仲裁员关于指定事宜。指定的仲裁员将被要求在10（十）天内填写CAM-CCBC利益冲突和可利用性表（简称"表格"）。

11.4 表格的答复和任何相关事实应转发给当事人，当事人有10（十）天时间发表评论。如当事人要求澄清或补充信息，应邀请仲裁员予以答复。如当事人提出异议，第14条应予适用。

11.5 在指定仲裁员步骤之后，CAM-CCBC秘书处（除非当事人另有约定）应通知仲裁员在10（十）天内指定仲裁庭庭长，最好是CAM-CCBC仲裁员名单上的成员。

11.6 如庭长不在CAM-CCBC仲裁员名单上，指定须附上充分的理由和被指定仲裁员的简历，并须取决于CAM-CCBC主席的批准。

11.7 如任何当事人未能指定仲裁员，或当事人指定的仲裁员未能指定仲裁庭庭长，CAM-CCBC主席应从仲裁员名单成员中优先指定。

11.8 在各方当事人国籍、总部或住所位于不同国家的程序中，任何一方可请求仲裁庭庭长与所涉当事人的国籍不同。CAM-CCBC主席在评估需要和便利性后，将就具体案件中的请求作出决定。

12.1 在多方当事人作为申请人和/或被申请人的仲裁中，如当事人未就指定仲裁员的方法达成协议，CAM-CCBC主席在考虑到当事人的利益后，可指定仲裁庭的所有成员，并指定其中一人担任庭长。

12.2 CAM-CCBC主席在决定时，即使某方当事人缺席，也可核实有多少利益相关方参与了争议。

13.1 如当事人已同意争议由独任仲裁员解决，或由CAM-CCBC主席如此决定，仲裁员应由当事人在秘书处通知后15（十五）天内协议指定。在此期限后，如未能指定独任仲裁员或未能就仲裁员的指定达成协议，独任仲裁员应由CAM-CCBC主席按照第11.8条指定。

13.2 当事人应优先从CAM-CCBC仲裁员名单中指定独任仲裁员。如独任仲裁员不在上述名单上，指定须附上充分的理由和被指定仲裁员的简历，并须取决于CAM-CCBC主席的批准。

14.1 当事人可在知悉相关事实后10（十）天内，因缺乏独立性、公正性或正当理由对仲裁员提出异议。

14.2 异议须有理由并提交给CAM-CCBC秘书处，秘书处应将其提交给被提出异议的仲裁员和其他当事人以征求意见。CAB-CCBC应采取必要步骤组成专门委员会，并按照第34.5条收取相应费用。

14.3 异议应由CAM-CCBC主席指定的3（三）名仲裁员名单成员组成的专门委员会决定。

14.4 为评估对仲裁员的异议而组成的专门委员会，如认为必要且不影响事件的迅速处理，可要求当事人和被提出异议的仲裁员提供补充说明、提交新文件和采取其认为有用、必要和适当的任何其他措施。

14.5 CAM-CCBC秘书处应将所需措施完结的情况通知当事人和仲裁员。专门委员会应在30（三十）天内作出决定。如有必要，委员会可将决定异议的期限延长15（十五）天。

14.6 专门委员会的决定须有理由，且为终局，不可上诉。

15.1 如任何仲裁员辞任、异议被接受、无行为能力或死亡，应按照原指定的程序进行更换。

15.2 在更换仲裁员的情况下，并考虑到仲裁的阶段，CAM-CCBC秘书处可更改程序期限以重新组成仲裁庭。

16.1 指定仲裁员后，秘书处应通知仲裁庭，以便每位仲裁员在5（五）天内签署独立性声明，该声明代表其正式接受该职位的一切应有效力。

16.2 仲裁应在秘书处收到被指定仲裁员签署的独立性声明之日视为启动，仲裁庭应视为组成。

17.1 希望以仲裁协议不存在、无效或不生效为由反对仲裁继续进行的当事人（即使仅涉及其中一方）应尽早向CAM-CCBC主席提交正式异议。

17.2 CAM-CCBC主席应对仲裁协议进行表面分析，并在管理层面就可能迅速决定的存在、有效性和效力相关事项作出决定，无需提供证据。

17.3 CAM-CCBC主席关于仲裁继续进行的决定是初步的，由仲裁庭自行决定其管辖权。

18.1 希望将追加当事人纳入仲裁的当事人应尽早根据第7.1条向秘书处提交针对追加当事人的仲裁申请书。秘书处收到请求的日期应视为就追加当事人而言的仲裁初始日期。

18.2 仲裁庭组成前，CAM-CCBC主席应在以下情况下就追加当事人作出决定：
(a) 所有涉及的当事人同意；或
(b) 追加当事人与提交仲裁的事项有关联，且可能在表面分析中被视为受仲裁协议约束。

18.3 CAM-CCBC主席关于追加或不追加当事人进入仲裁的决定可由仲裁庭重新审议。

18.4 仲裁庭组成后，关于追加当事人进入仲裁的决定应由仲裁员作出，并要求当事人发表评论。

18.5 自愿希望加入程序的当事人可随时提出请求，将决定提交给CAM-CCBC主席，或如仲裁庭已经组成，则提交给仲裁庭。

18.6 被纳入仲裁程序的当事人须接受已组成的仲裁庭，并须签署程序令的修正案。

19.1 CAM-CCBC主席可经一方当事人请求，考虑到第一个程序的阶段，在第二个程序的仲裁庭组成前，根据本规则将两个或多个待决仲裁合并为一个，但须满足以下条件之一：
(a) 当事人已同意合并；或
(b) 所有仲裁中的请求均基于相同的仲裁协议提出；或
(c) 仲裁请求不是基于相同的仲裁协议，但(i) 各仲裁涉及相同的当事人，(ii) 争议涉及相同的法律关系，且(iii) CAM-CCBC主席认为仲裁协议相互兼容。

19.2 在决定合并时，CAM-CCBC主席可咨询在任仲裁员。

19.3 仲裁程序应合并到第一个仲裁中，除非当事人另有约定。

20.1 当事人可在单一仲裁程序中提出源于或涉及一个以上合同的请求。

20.2 仲裁庭组成前，如对源于或涉及一个以上合同的请求在单一仲裁程序中继续进行存在异议，CAM-CCBC主席在听取当事人意见后，应就此事项作出裁定。

20.3 在以下情况下，可在单一仲裁中继续进行：
(a) 仲裁协议相互兼容；
(b) 请求源于同一交易或一系列交易；
(c) 对程序的效率和速度没有重大影响。

20.4 仲裁庭组成后，授权在单一仲裁中处理请求的决定应提交仲裁庭分析。

21.1 仲裁庭组成前，需要紧急措施的当事人可提交请求指定紧急仲裁员，除非当事人另有约定。

21.2 紧急仲裁员可采取第22.1条规定的任何措施，因其性质使然，不能等待仲裁庭组成。

21.3 紧急仲裁员须遵守第9.2条规定的相同独立性和公正性义务。

21.4 启动紧急仲裁员程序并不意味着放弃在主管司法机关寻求任何其他紧急措施的权利。

21.5 紧急仲裁员程序应遵循附录一的规定。

22.1 除非当事人另有约定，仲裁庭可发出临时、强制和中间措施，仲裁庭可自行决定修改、中止或撤销这些措施，即使经一方当事人请求也应如此，且应立即通知当事人；仲裁庭也可命令紧急且不能延期的临时措施，即使单方命令亦可，同时保留当事人事后陈述其论点的权利，该等论点须在措施通知后5（五）天内提交。

22.2 临时措施请求应说明引起请求的情况、所请求的措施，以及临时措施对于防止损害或保全现状的必要性，否则将不被接受此类请求。

22.3 仲裁庭可要求请求临时措施的当事人就此等措施提供适当的担保。

22.4 一方当事人向任何司法当局提出的临时措施请求不应被视为与仲裁协议不相容，或被视为放弃仲裁的权利。

22.5 仲裁庭尤其可命令保全、保管、出售或以其他方式处置任何证据或作为争议标的物的任何财产或资产。

23.1 仲裁启动后，仲裁庭应准备程序令，当事人和仲裁员应在30（三十）天内签署，程序令应包括：
(a) 当事人及其代表的姓名和地址；
(b) 仲裁员的姓名和地址；
(c) 当事人请求的概要，以及反请求（如适用）；
(d) 争议事项的定义和法律依据；
(e) 仲裁地；
(f) 仲裁语言；
(g) 适用于实体的法律规则；
(h) 程序时间表。

23.2 如当事人未能就程序令的任何事项达成协议，仲裁庭在听取当事人意见后应就此事项作出决定。

23.3 由于争议的发展，如仲裁庭要求或经一方当事人请求，在听取当事人意见后，程序令应在必要时予以修正。

24.1 仲裁庭应根据当事人约定的法律规则裁决提交给它的争议，或在无此约定时，由仲裁庭选择的法律规则裁决。

24.2 只有在当事人明确授权的情况下，仲裁庭才能作为友好调停人裁决或依衡平法裁决。

24.3 在所有情况下，仲裁庭均应按照合同条款（如有）裁决，并应考虑到适用于该交易的任何商业惯例。

24.4 仲裁庭应根据程序的进展和复杂程度，以单一终局裁决或部分裁决的形式作出裁决，形式由程序令规定。

24.5 在本规则规定的限制范围内，当事人在程序事项上有自由约定的保障。

25.1 每方当事人应承担为其请求所依据的事实提供证据的责任。

25.2 仲裁庭可命令当事人在仲裁庭确定的期限内提供文件、展品或其他证据。

25.3 当事人可约定，或仲裁庭可命令，证人（包括专家证人）通过证言、交叉询问或其他方法进行询问。

25.4 仲裁庭可指定一名或多名独立专家，就仲裁庭指定的特定问题向其报告。仲裁庭收到专家报告后，应向当事人提供一份副本，当事人应有机会就报告陈述意见。

25.5 仲裁庭应确定所提供证据的可采性、相关性、重要性和证明力，以及应遵循的庭审程序。

26.1 仲裁庭应为证人、专家提供证据和口头辩论举行庭审，除非当事人已同意不举行庭审。

26.2 仲裁庭应给予各方当事人关于任何庭审日期、时间和地点的充分提前通知。

26.3 庭审应在仲裁地进行，或在仲裁庭确定适当方便的任何其他地点进行，须经当事人同意或在无协议时由仲裁庭决定。

26.4 仲裁庭在听取当事人意见后，可通过视频会议或任何其他通讯方式进行远程庭审。

26.5 庭审应由仲裁庭自行决定记录和转录。

27.1 如申请人未能提交诉状而未说明充分理由，仲裁庭仍可继续进行仲裁。

27.2 如被申请人未能提交答辩状而未说明充分理由，仲裁庭可继续程序。

27.3 如一方当事人未能出庭或提供书面证据，仲裁庭可继续程序并根据其面前的证据作出裁决。

28.1 当确信当事人已有合理机会陈述其案件时，仲裁庭应宣布程序终结。

28.2 仲裁庭可在裁决作出之前的任何时候自行决定或经一方当事人申请重新开庭，但仅在其认为必要时方可如此。

29.1 仲裁庭应在收到结案陈述后60（六十）天内（或在收到结案陈述期限届满通知后）作出仲裁裁决，除非程序令另有规定或与当事人另有约定。

29.2 第29.1条规定的期限可由仲裁庭庭长自行决定延长最多60（六十）天。

30.1 仲裁裁决可以是部分裁决或终局裁决。

30.2 仲裁裁决应以书面形式作出，不能基于当事人缺席。

30.2.1 三人仲裁庭的情况下，仲裁裁决应尽可能协商一致作出，如不可行，则以多数票作出，每位仲裁员（包括仲裁庭庭长）各有一票表决权。如未能形成多数意见，仲裁庭庭长的票表决权。

30.2.2 不同意多数意见的仲裁员可陈述其异议票。

30.3 仲裁裁决应包含以下内容：
(a) 报告（程序和事实背景），载明当事人姓名和争议概要；
(b) 决定的理由，如适用法律要求或依当事人约定，应涵盖事实和法律两方面问题，并在适当时明示裁决依衡平法作出；
(c) 裁决主文，载明所有规范和在适当时遵守决定的时间限制；和
(d) 裁决作出的日期（年、月、日）和仲裁地。

30.4 在当事人协议的前提下，仲裁裁决应确定仲裁费用的责任分担及其各自比例。

30.4.1 仲裁庭应考虑到仲裁程序的结果、案件的复杂程度、律师的行为，以及当事人和其律师的行为，包括恶意诉讼或程序滥用（根据第25.1条），以确定偿还金额及其比例。

30.5 仲裁裁决应视为在仲裁地作出。

30.6 仲裁裁决应由所有仲裁员签署。如一名或多名仲裁员未签署裁决，由仲裁庭庭长证明此事实。

30.6.1 在适用强制性规定的限制下，除非另有约定或决定，仲裁裁决可以电子方式签署，和/或由单独的签名页随后合并为单一文件。

30.7 仲裁庭庭长应将裁决发送给CAM-CCBC秘书处，秘书处在核实所有费用已实际收到后，将通知当事人。

30.8 一旦终局仲裁裁决作出并通知当事人，仲裁即告终止，但根据第31条提出澄清请求的情况除外，在此情况下管辖权应延长至相应决定作出。

31.1 当事人可在收到仲裁裁决后15（十五）天内，通过向仲裁庭提出请求，就任何矛盾、遗漏或含糊之处请求澄清。

31.2 仲裁庭可允许其他当事人就在15（十五）天内提交的澄清请求发表评论。

31.3 仲裁庭应在随后的15（十五）天内作出决定，此期限从收到澄清请求、收到对方当事人的评论或评论期限届满（以最后发生者为准）开始计算。

31.4 第31.3条提到的期限可由仲裁庭自行决定延长最多15（十五）天。

32.1 如当事人在仲裁程序进行期间达成和解，结束争议，经当事人请求，仲裁庭可将和解内容记录在和解裁决书中。

33.1 当事人有义务按照裁决规定的方式和时间遵守仲裁裁决，如不遵守，败诉方应向胜诉方承担损害赔偿责任。

34.1 CAM-CCBC应发布其费用表，金额和指引可由CAM-CCBC主席不时修订。

34.2 费用表应规定(i) 费用和其他开支的支付形式和时间，(ii) 仲裁员和专家费用的支付，(iii) 金额的偿还等事项。

34.3 费用表条款的遵守对当事人、仲裁员、专家、仲裁庭秘书和程序中的其他参与者具有强制性。

34.4 管理费和仲裁员费用在申请人提交仲裁申请书之日和被申请人收到通知之日到期，须支付给CAM-CCBC。

34.5 本规则第14条规定的专门委员会仅在费用表规定的金额支付后方可组成。除非有明确相反的具体规定，专门委员会费用须由提出异议的当事人支付。

35.1 如未在费用表规定或CAM-CCBC秘书处指定的期限内预付管理费、仲裁员和专家费用或任何其他仲裁费用，一方当事人应被允许在CAM-CCBC秘书处规定的期限内代另一方支付到期款项。

35.2 当由另一方支付时，CAM-CCBC秘书处应通知当事人和仲裁庭，在此情况下仲裁庭应驳回违约方当事人的请求（如有）。

35.3 如无当事人愿意支付，程序应中止。

35.4 一旦程序因未支付而中止30（三十）天，且双方当事人均未提供资金，程序可予终止，但不影响当事人在支付欠款后提交新仲裁申请书寻求解决争议的权利。

35.5 仲裁员可诉诸法院内或法院外方式要求支付仲裁员费用，CAM-CCBC可诉诸法院内或法院外方式要求支付管理费或费用，这些应被视为确定的和已确定的债权，可通过催收或执行诉讼连同利息和通货膨胀调整予以追收。

36.1 快速程序是更快解决仲裁争议的选择，仅适用于所涉金额不超过费用表规定金额的程序。

36.2 如当事人已约定退出该程序，快速程序条款不适用。

36.3 仲裁庭组成前，CAM-CCBC主席可自行决定或经一方当事人请求，驳回快速程序的适用。

36.4 CAM-CCBC主席应在管理层面分析快速程序对具体案件的适合性，考虑到其复杂程度和其他相关情况。

36.5 CAM-CCBC主席作出的决定须经仲裁庭批准。

36.6 如快速程序被驳回不适用于特定案件，仲裁庭应继续履行其职责。

36.7 快速程序的费用应低于费用表专门章节的规定。

36.8 如争议金额重新评估后超过限额，须支付费用表"主要费用"项下的金额。

37.1 本规则的其他条款应适用于受快速程序约束的仲裁，只要它们与本条规则不冲突。

37.2 在本规则第8.1条规定的期限届满后，CAM-CCBC秘书处在符合第36.1条和第36.2条规定的要求的情况下，应通知当事人快速程序将适用于该案件。

37.3 为加快程序，CAM-CCBC秘书处可在仲裁庭组成前缩短本规则规定的期限。

37.4 所有通信、通知或传票，以及任何提交文件、决定或文件的提交须完全以电子方式进行。

37.5 除非另有约定，快速程序应由根据本规则指定的独任仲裁员进行和裁决。

37.6 如仲裁协议规定程序由三名仲裁员进行，CAM-CCBC秘书处应邀请当事人同意指定独任仲裁员。如无此协议，仲裁庭应按当事人约定组成。

37.7 本规则第14条规定的专门委员会应由1（一）名成员组成，由CAM-CCBC主席指定，优先从仲裁员名单中指定。

37.8 在本规则第16.2条规定的仲裁启动后，CAM-CCBC秘书处应通知当事人和仲裁员在15（十五）天内签署程序令。

37.9 为确保快速程序的效率，仲裁庭可采取以下行动：
(a) 限制书面提交的数量、长度和范围；和
(b) 在听取当事人意见后，决定程序仅基于书面证据进行，可能拒绝提交其他证据。

37.10 如仲裁庭授权进行庭审，庭审应优先通过视频会议或任何其他远程通信方式进行。

37.11 从签署程序令到开始终局裁决期限的程序不得超过10（十）个月。

37.12 如程序超过10（十）个月，除非当事人另有约定或仲裁庭另有决定，应继续按照快速程序管理程序。在任何情况下，超过10（十）个月后，须支付费用表"主要费用"项下的金额。

37.13 仲裁裁决应在证据阶段结束后30（三十）天内作出，可延长相同期限。

38.1 仲裁员应在职权和职责范围内解释和适用本规则。

38.2 仲裁员可就本规则条款的解释咨询CAM-CCBC主席。

38.3 CAM-CCBC道德准则在所有法律意义上是本规则的一部分，作为解释本规则条款的次要来源进行规制。

39.1 仲裁应保密，但法律、其他适用法律规则或当事人明确协议规定的情形除外。

39.1.1 在法律程序中出示与仲裁程序有关的文件（如对保护仲裁案件所涉当事人的权利确有必要）不构成对保密义务的违反。

39.2 除非当事人反对，CAM-CCBC可公布裁决和程序中作出的其他决定，但不提及当事人或允许识别当事人或案件的数据。

39.3 CAM-CCBC成员、仲裁员、仲裁庭秘书、专家、当事人和其他参与者均被禁止披露因其职责或参与仲裁程序而获得任何信息。

40.1 CAM-CCBC应经当事人或仲裁员（且仅向他们）的书面请求，提供关于仲裁程序的文件副本。

40.2 仲裁程序的实体记录应保存6（六）个月，电子记录应保存5（五）年，从仲裁结束之日起算。

40.2.1 相关当事方有责任在所述期限内自费请求提供相关记录和文件副本。

40.2.2 在这些期限之后，所有文件可被销毁，无需事先获得当事人授权。

41.1 当事人、其法律代表、仲裁员和其他参与者承认，收集、使用、处理、传输和存储个人数据对于仲裁程序的继续是必要的，且在严格必要时，可包含在通知、决定和其他文件中。

41.2 仲裁庭和当事人必须确保遵守适用的数据保护立法，以正确开展仲裁程序。

41.3 如在仲裁程序中怀疑或确认发生个人数据泄露，应立即通知CAM-CCBC和其他参与者，以便适当通知主管机关。

41.4 仲裁结束后，程序中处理的个人数据应存储必要的时间，以正常行使权利（包括CAM-CCBC的权利）和遵守法律义务，此后须进行匿名化处理或销毁。

42.1 任何仲裁员、CAM-CCBC或与CAM-CCBC相关的任何人员均不就与仲裁有关的任何作为、事实或遗漏承担责任，但经证明有故意不当行为的情况除外。

42.2 任何关于不遵守本规则或适用于程序的任何其他规则、仲裁庭的决定或仲裁协议中关于组成仲裁庭或进行程序的任何其他规定的异议，应尽早提出。

43.1 本规则于2022年8月1日经CAM-CCBC咨询委员会批准，将于2022年11月1日生效。

43.2 除非当事人另有约定，应适用在第7.1条规定的提交仲裁申请书之日有效的规则。

43.3 经当事人选择，本规则也可适用于在2022年11月1日之前提交但在本规则生效后签署程序令的仲裁。

第1条 希望根据本规则第21条诉诸紧急仲裁员的当事人应向CAM-CCBC提交紧急措施请求，内容包括：
(a) 包含仲裁协议的合同或文件；
(b) 参与仲裁的当事人的完整姓名和身份证明，包括其通知地址和/或电子邮件；
(c) 授权委托书（如有）；
(d) 引起临时措施请求的情况说明，包括已提交或拟提交CAM-CCBC的主要争议的简要说明；
(e) 所请求的紧急措施及其紧迫性依据的说明；
(f) 仲裁地、语言和适用于仲裁的法律规则的选择；
(g) 申请人认为与评估紧急措施请求相关或有帮助的合同或信息；
(h) 待决或已生效的司法措施的说明；
(i) 受紧急仲裁员程序约束的当事人向CAM-CCBC提交的关于基础争议的仲裁申请书或其他提交文件；
(j) 费用表规定的紧急仲裁员程序相关费用的支付证明。

第2条 CAM-CCBC主席在以下情况下将驳回临时措施请求：
(a) 如仲裁庭已组成，根据CAM-CCBC规则第16.2条；
(b) 在缺乏规定CAM-CCBC管理仲裁程序权限的仲裁协议的情况下；
(c) 如当事人于2018年7月20日之前签订仲裁协议，且未随后加入将争议提交紧急仲裁员程序的选项；
(d) 如当事人于2018年7月20日至2020年11月24日期间签订仲裁协议，除非其明确选择采用紧急仲裁员程序；
(e) 如当事人明确同意退出紧急仲裁员程序；或
(f) 缺乏紧急仲裁员程序相关费用支付证明的情况下。

第3条 如初步拒绝，CAM-CCBC秘书处应通知申请人紧急仲裁员程序将不继续进行。

第4条 如紧急措施请求未被初步驳回，CAM-CCBC秘书处应立即将请求和支持文件的副本发送给其他当事人，同时通知申请人。

第5条 当事人应在收到通知后2（两）天内告知其电子地址。

第6条 如未在CAM-CCBC主席收到紧急措施请求后15（十五）天内根据本规则第7.1条提交仲裁申请书，CAM-CCBC主席应终止紧急仲裁员程序。

6.1 在特殊情况下，此期限可由CAM-CCBC主席延长。

第7条 CAM-CCBC主席在接受程序后，应指定一名紧急仲裁员，优先从仲裁员名单成员中指定。

第8条 仲裁庭组成后不得指定紧急仲裁员。但是，在仲裁庭组成前指定的紧急仲裁员应保留在本附录第20条规定的期限内作出其决定的权力。

第9条 紧急仲裁员应收到程序副本，并在收到指定后2（两）天内填写问卷和签署独立性声明。

第10条 除非当事人另有约定，紧急仲裁员不得在与引起紧急措施请求的争议有关的任何仲裁中担任仲裁员。

第11条 一收到紧急仲裁员签署的问卷和独立性声明，CAM-CCBC秘书处应立即通知当事人。此后，当事人的所有书面通信应致函紧急仲裁员，并抄送其他当事人和CAM-CCBC秘书处。

第12条 紧急仲裁员应就其管辖权以及仲裁协议的存在、有效性和范围作出决定。

第13条 当事人可在知悉相关事实后2（两）天内因缺乏独立性、公正性或合理怀疑对紧急仲裁员提出异议。

第14条 异议应由CAM-CCBC主席在紧急仲裁员和其他当事人就此事项发表评论的期限届满后决定。

第15条 紧急仲裁员辞任、异议被接受、无行为能力或死亡的情况下，由CAM-CCBC主席在2（两）天内进行更换。

15.1 如更换紧急仲裁员，程序应从其所在阶段恢复，除非新的紧急仲裁员另有决定。

第16条 紧急仲裁员程序的仲裁地应为当事人约定的仲裁地，但须遵守本规则第4条。

第17条 紧急仲裁员程序的语言应为当事人约定的仲裁语言，但须遵守本规则第6条。

第18条 紧急仲裁员应考虑到争议的性质和临时措施请求的紧迫性，并以适当的方式进行程序，同时适当注意听取对方意见原则、充分辩护权利和当事人平等对待原则。

18.1 收到案卷后，紧急仲裁员应确立程序的暂定时间表。

第19条 紧急仲裁员发出的命令和决定应以书面形式作出，说明其依据的理由，并应符合本规则第30.3条的要求。

19.1 紧急仲裁员可设定其认为必要的条件以确保其决定得到遵守，包括强制罚款和提供担保。

第20条 紧急仲裁员的决定应在签署独立性声明提交后15（十五）天内作出，或按暂定时间表规定的时间作出。

20.1 CAM-CCBC主席可在(i) 经紧急仲裁员有理由的请求，(ii) 在适当情况下自行决定，或(iii) 经当事人如此同意的情况下延长此期限。

第21条 通过将争议提交紧急仲裁程序，当事人承诺毫不拖延地遵守紧急仲裁员将作出的决定。

第22条 紧急仲裁员作出的决定因其临时性质，不约束仲裁庭，仲裁庭一旦组成，将有权修改、撤销或废除先前作出的任何决定。

第23条 在仲裁庭组成之前，可能改变先前作出决定的新事实分析可由紧急仲裁员评估。一旦组成，仲裁庭应就当事人关于紧急仲裁员程序的任何请求作出决定，包括关于执行紧急仲裁员的决定和重新分配紧急仲裁员程序费用的任何请求。

第24条 如因任何原因仲裁在未作出终局裁决的情况下终止，紧急仲裁员的决定应停止对当事人具有约束力。

第25条 CAM-CCBC规则的规定在其与本附录不冲突的范围内适用，CAM-CCBC主席负责解决关于这些条款解释的任何疑问。

巴林争议解决中心（BCDR-AAA）是中东地区领先的仲裁机构，隶属于巴林工业、商业和旅游部。该中心在巴林政府、美国仲裁协会（AAA）和国际商会（ICC）三方独特协议的框架下运营。BCDR-AAA以国际标准提供最先进的仲裁和调解服务，将区域专业知识与全球最佳实践相结合。

**Official Website:** https://www.bcdr.org/
**官网：** https://www.bcdr.org/

当事人各方已书面同意（在争议发生前或发生后）按照巴林争议解决中心（"中心"）、BCDR或BCDR-AAA的仲裁规则将争议提交仲裁，或已约定将争议提交巴林争议解决中心、BCDR或BCDR-AAA进行仲裁但未指定具体规则的，仲裁应按照本规则进行，中心可在仲裁开始日前修改本规则使之生效（"本规则"）。本规则包括中心可能不时单独修订的费用表。

1.2 The Chamber shall be the administrator of such arbitrations.

希望根据本规则启动仲裁的一方（仅一方时为"申请人"；多于一方时各为"申请人"）应向中心提交书面仲裁申请（"申请"），同时向仲裁所有其他当事人（一方的"被申请人"；多于一方时各为"被申请人"）提交该申请。

2.2 The Request shall include, or be accompanied by:

在中心初步确信可能存在符合第一条第1.1款规定的仲裁协议的前提下，仲裁应自中心收到申请和提交费之日起视为开始，中心应以书面形式通知所有当事人。

被申请人应在仲裁开始后30天内向中心提交书面回应申请（"回应"），同时向仲裁所有其他当事人提交该回应。

4.2 The Response shall include, or be accompanied by:

在回应提交后，或若未提交回应则回应提交期限届满后，中心应尽快指示当事人支付费用表规定的案件管理费（"案件管理费"），被指示支付的一方或多方应在中心设定的支付截止日期前支付。

5.4 If the Case Management Fee is not paid promptly and in full, the Chamber shall so inform the parties in order that one or more of them may make the required payment, without which the Chamber may suspend or terminate the proceedings.

本条应排除本规则任何相冲突条款的适用：

(a) if the parties have not agreed in writing otherwise, and provided that the claim and any counterclaim in the arbitration are quantified monetary claims and the combined value of such claim and counterclaim does not exceed US$1 million; or

任何当事人（包括其法律代表）、仲裁庭或中心向其中任何一方或多方发出的任何书面通讯，应以专人递送；快递；挂号邮件；或电子邮件、传真或任何其他能提供发送记录形式的电子通讯方式送达。

7.2 For the purpose of calculating a period of time under the Rules, such period shall begin to run on the day following the day on which a written communication is received by the intended recipient.

仲裁庭应由一名或三名仲裁员组成。若当事人未书面约定仲裁员人数，应指定一名仲裁员，除非中心考虑到争议的所有情况决定应指定三名仲裁员。

8.2 Where the parties are of different nationalities, a person who has the nationality of any of the parties may be appointed as a sole or presiding arbitrator only with the written agreement of all the parties or, absent such agreement, if the Chamber so determines.

若需指定独任仲裁员，当事人可共同提名仲裁员并书面通知中心予以指定。

9.2 If, within 15 days after the submission of the Response, or, if no Response is submitted, within 15 days after the time for submission of a Response has elapsed, the parties have not jointly nominated a sole arbitrator:

所有仲裁员应始终保持公正且独立于各方当事人，任何仲裁员不得充任任何当事人的代理人。

10.2 No party or anyone acting on behalf of any party shall have any ex parte communication relating to the dispute with any candidate arbitrator.

若存在可能引起对仲裁员公正性或独立性合理怀疑的情况，任何当事人均可提出回避。

11.2 A party that intends to challenge an arbitrator shall submit to the Chamber, to all other parties and to the arbitral tribunal a written challenge within 15 days after the date on which that party became aware of those facts and circumstances.

若仲裁员拒绝履职，或在法律上或事实上被阻止完成仲裁程序，或无充分理由未能履职，应适用第九条规定的程序指定替代仲裁员。

中心可应仲裁庭请求或自行决定，从其维护的名册中指定一名仲裁庭秘书。

13.2 The secretary shall assist with the administrative management of the arbitration, under the direction of the arbitral tribunal.

无法等待仲裁庭组建的一方可根据本条向中心请求紧急临时保护措施（"紧急措施申请"）。

14.2 The Application for Emergency Measures shall be accompanied by the Emergency Arbitrator Fee prescribed by the Fee Schedule, without the actual receipt of which the Application shall be treated as not having been received.

仲裁庭应考虑当事人意见，以其认为适合争议性质和情况的方式进行仲裁。

16.2 The arbitral tribunal shall ensure that the proceedings are conducted in an efficient and cost-effective manner.

仲裁地应为巴林，除非当事人另有书面约定。

19.2 The arbitral tribunal may, after consultation with the parties, conduct hearings at any place it considers appropriate.

仲裁语言应为英语，除非当事人另有书面约定。

20.2 The arbitral tribunal may order that any documentary evidence be accompanied by a translation into the language of the arbitration.

任何当事人可由其选定的法律代表或其他代表在仲裁中进行代理。

21.2 The names and addresses of such representatives shall be communicated in writing to all other parties and to the Chamber.

已签订与仲裁相关之第三方资助协议的当事人应立即向中心、所有其他当事人和任何仲裁员披露第三方资助人的身份，且无论如何不晚于其根据第四条规定提交回应之日，或其成为该等资助协议当事人之日（以较晚者为准）。

经任何当事人书面申请，仲裁庭有权裁定或裁决其认为必要的任何临时或保全措施，包括禁令救济和财产保护或保存措施。

仲裁庭有权就其自身的管辖权作出裁定，包括对仲裁协议存在、效力或有效性提出的任何异议。

希望将追加当事人纳入仲裁的一方应提交针对该追加当事人的书面仲裁请求。

若根据本规则进行的两个或多个仲裁系依据同一仲裁协议且在相同当事人之间提起，中心可将仲裁合并为一个仲裁。

中心应指示当事人支付适当金额作为仲裁费用的预付款。

30.4 Failure by a party asserting a claim or counterclaim to make the required payment may be deemed by the arbitral tribunal a withdrawal of the claim or counterclaim.

仲裁员的费用和开支应符合费用表，且金额应合理。

31.3 Any dispute regarding the fees and expenses of the arbitral tribunal shall be determined by the Chamber.

仲裁庭应适用当事人约定的适用于争议实体的法律规则。

32.2 In arbitrations involving the performance of contracts, the arbitral tribunal shall decide in accordance with the terms of the contract.

在按照程序时间表作出最后一次书面或口头陈述后，仲裁庭应宣布仲裁程序终结。

除作出最终裁决外，仲裁庭可作出临时或部分裁决、命令、决定和裁定。

34.2 When there is more than one arbitrator, any award shall be made unanimously or, failing unanimity, by a majority of the arbitrators.

仲裁庭应尽快评议并作出最终裁决，且不得晚于程序终结之日起60日。

35.2 Awards shall be made in writing, and the arbitral tribunal shall state the reasons upon which an award is based.

仲裁庭应在最终裁决中确定仲裁费用。

36.2 Such costs may include:

在收到裁决后30天内，任何当事人可请求仲裁庭解释裁决或更正任何错误。

若当事人在最终裁决作出前和解，仲裁庭应终止仲裁程序；经所有当事人请求，仲裁庭可按和解条款以裁决形式记录该和解。

知道本规则任何条款未被遵守而继续进行仲裁而未及时提出异议的当事人，视为放弃异议权。

仲裁程序中披露的保密信息，当事人、仲裁员、专家或中心不得泄露。

40.2 An award may be made public only with the consent of all parties or as required by law.

仲裁员、中心和任何紧急仲裁员对任何人在仲裁方面发生的任何作为或不作为不承担责任。

"因本合同引起或与本合同有关的任何争议，包括关于合同存在、效力或终止的任何问题，均应通过仲裁最终解决，仲裁按照巴林争议解决中心的仲裁规则进行。

The arbitral tribunal shall consist of [specify one or three] arbitrator[s].

沙特商业仲裁中心（SCCA）于2014年在沙特阿拉伯利雅得成立，是沙特阿拉伯"2030愿景"发展世界级争议解决基础设施倡议的组成部分。SCCA按照国际标准提供仲裁、调解和其他替代性争议解决服务，同时兼顾区域商业惯例。SCCA已成为海湾地区领先的替代性争议解决机构，处理涉及来自20多个不同国籍当事人的争议。

**Official Website:** https://www.scca.com.sa/
**官网：** https://www.scca.com.sa/

在本SCCA仲裁规则和附件中，以下词语具有以下含义：

- **Administrator:** The SCCA acting in the role of the administrator

当事人已约定将相互之间关于特定法律关系（无论是否为合同性质）的争议根据本规则提交仲裁，或已约定将争议提交SCCA进行仲裁但未指定具体规则的，该等争议应根据本规则解决。当事人由此授权SCCA管理仲裁。

2. These Rules shall come into force on 1 May 2023 and shall apply to any arbitration that is commenced on or after that date. The appendices to these Rules shall form an integral part of these Rules.

SCCA法院是独立于SCCA的机构，履行规则和SCCA法院内部规则规定的某些职能。

2. Except where the Rules state otherwise, the decisions of the SCCA Court shall be final and binding upon the parties and the Arbitral Tribunal. The parties waive any right of appeal or review in respect of the decisions of the SCCA Court.

除非当事人另有约定或仲裁庭另有命令，通知应以快递、挂号邮件或任何能提供发送记录（包括电子邮件或其他电子方式）的通讯方式发送。

2. If a party has designated a physical or electronic address specifically for this purpose, any Notice shall be delivered to that party or its authorized representative at that address.

希望启动仲裁的申请人应向SCCA提交仲裁申请。

若当事人就独任仲裁员达成一致，SCCA法院应予以指定。

每位仲裁员应公正且独立于各方当事人。

若存在可能引起对仲裁员公正性或独立性合理怀疑的情况，一方当事人可提出回避。

若需更换仲裁员，应以与被更换仲裁员相同的方式指定替代仲裁员。

当事人可由其选定的人员代理。

2. The challenge shall be submitted within 15 days of appointment or of discovering the grounds.

仲裁员的授权因辞职、免职或死亡而终止。

2. An arbitrator may be removed if there is a justified reason.

若仲裁员被更换，程序应从已达成的阶段继续进行。

若一方有多方当事人，他们应共同提名一名仲裁员。

2. If they fail to agree, the Board shall make the appointment.

快速程序可在以下情况下适用：

(a) The amount in dispute does not exceed EUR 200,000; or

仲裁地应为维也纳，除非另有约定。

2. Hearings may be held elsewhere.

语言应由当事人约定。

2. In absence of agreement, the Arbitral Tribunal shall determine.

仲裁庭应适用当事人指定的法律或法律规则。

2. Failing such designation, the law determined by conflict of laws rules.

仲裁庭应以高效的方式进行程序。

2. The Arbitral Tribunal shall ensure equal treatment of the parties.

申请人应在规定时间内提交诉状。

2. The Respondent shall submit a statement of defense within the time fixed.

除非当事人同意仅依据文件裁决，仲裁庭应进行庭审。

2. The Arbitral Tribunal shall give adequate notice.

知道规则未被遵守而未及时提出异议的当事人，视为已放弃其异议。

仲裁庭确信已给予当事人陈述案件的一切机会后，应宣布程序终结。

2. The award shall be made within three months of closure of proceedings.

经一方当事人请求，仲裁庭可命令临时措施。

2. The Arbitral Tribunal may require security for costs.

程序因裁决、和解或终止命令而终结。

仲裁员超过一人时，决定应由多数作出。

2. If there is no majority, the presiding arbitrator shall decide.

裁决应以书面作出，并应说明所依据的理由。

2. The award shall state the date and place.

若当事人达成和解，经请求，仲裁庭应按和解条款作出裁决。

仲裁庭应在裁决中确定仲裁费用。

2. The costs include:

在收到裁决后30天内，一方当事人可请求更正或澄清。

2. Within 30 days, a party may request an additional award.

委员会可将裁决发回仲裁庭重新审议。

### 专家评论

*以下为Fresa Li、Dr. Frontie、Dr. Gochye、Dr. Venessa及灵犀就巴西-加拿大商会仲裁与调解中心仲裁规则所作的评论。*

**Fresa Li：**

CAM-CCBC 2022年版仲裁规则代表了拉丁美洲仲裁实践的最高标准，同时也是该区域国际化程度最高的机构规则，在南美基础设施和能源争议解决中具有权威地位。其第1条允许当事人修改规则条款的弹性设计非常独特——在保持制度稳定性的同时为复杂交易提供了定制空间，这在南美大型基础设施和能源项目中极为实用。第3条关于电子通信送达的规定，在南美区域互联网基础设施条件参差不齐的环境下，兼顾了效率与确定性。快速程序（第20条）将6个月期限与争议金额挂钩的设计，契合了拉美商业争议快速解决的需求，对资金周转要求高的建筑和能源项目尤为重要。我特别关注CAM-CCBC在数据保护领域的先行意识——第28条专门规定了数据保护条款，这在国际仲裁规则中属于少数，对于涉及个人数据跨境传输的技术许可和数字经济争议具有特殊价值。在中国-拉美双边贸易和投资持续增长的背景下，CAM-CCBC为中国企业处理拉美分支机构的商业争议提供了重要制度选项。实务建议：在拉美有绿地投资或基础设施项目的中国企业，选择CAM-CCBC时应在仲裁条款中明确约定仲裁语言（建议英语），并关注CAM-CCBC裁决在巴西本国法院的执行效率——这是其相较于ICC等全球机构的主要优势。

---


**Dr. Frontie：**

CAM-CCBC是巴西最大的仲裁机构，2022年对其规则进行了十年来首次全面修订，2025年又推出新规。2022年规则最显著的变化是引入了早期驳回条款和延长裁决期限的灵活性安排（可延长60天）。从制度创新视角看，CAM-CCBC的演变揭示了一个更深层的问题：新兴仲裁市场的规则更新频率往往高于成熟市场——这不是不成熟的表现，而是对快速变化的争议解决需求的制度响应。DWAC作为数字世界的全新仲裁机构，面临类似的制度环境：争议类型快速演进，规则必须保持更新弹性。CAM-CCBC的做法提示我们：规则修订机制本身应是规则设计的一部分——不是事后补救，而是制度内建。DWAC应考虑在仲裁规则中设置定期审阅和动态修订条款，使规则体系具备"可进化性"。

---

**Dr. Gochye：**

CAM-CCBC 2022年仲裁规则在拉丁美洲仲裁文化语境下呈现独特制度特征。巴西仲裁法传统上受西班牙-葡萄牙法律传统和大陆法系程序深刻影响，但过去二十年经历了显著现代化转型。和解裁决（Consent Award）规定（第29条）允许仲裁庭在当事人达成和解时记录和解内容，明确区分"终止程序命令"与"和解裁决"两种形式——和解裁决因其裁决形式更易获得法院的承认与执行。保密性精细化规定（第24条）明确列出可披露裁决的情形——法律义务要求披露、法院程序要求、执行或质疑裁决、保护合法权益等，回应拉美当事人对保密性的特别关注。CAM-CCBC代表新兴市场仲裁机构的制度演进模式，在吸收国际通行做法的同时保留拉美仲裁传统特定元素。

---

**Dr. Venessa：**

CAM-CCBC 2022年版仲裁规则在程序正义维度体现了巴西法律传统中强烈的正当程序观念。第9条（仲裁庭的独立性和公正性）的表述直接援引了巴西宪法第5条第LIV款的正当程序原则和第LV款的对抗原则，形成了独特的宪法化程序保障。第15条（当事人的平等对待）是少数专门设此条款的仲裁规则之一，明确了仲裁庭确保当事双方「完全平等的程序条件」的强制性义务。第26条（程序管理会议）要求仲裁庭在与当事人首次会议中即讨论程序时间表、证据规则和争议焦点，为后续程序的顺利进行奠定了正当程序基础。CAM-CCBC对当事人平等对待的宪法化保障标准，为其他仲裁机构的规则改进提供了一个值得关注的制度范式。

---

**灵犀：**

CAM-CCBC 2022年版仲裁规则的数字化水平在拉丁美洲处于领先地位，但与欧洲和亚太顶级机构相比仍有明显差距。第3条（电子通信）对电子送达和电子通知的认可符合国际标准，但规则缺乏对在线庭审、电子证据和AI仲裁辅助工具使用的专门规定。第4条（仲裁地）允许虚拟仲裁地——使仲裁程序可以完全在线进行而无需实际地点——这一制度设计是前瞻性的。CAM-CCBC的数字化发展路径对于DWAC有参考价值：不必追求一步到位的全数字化改革，可以分阶段深化数字仲裁制度的建设——从规则层面的技术中立授权起步，逐步向AI集成和智能仲裁演进。

---


============================================================

# 扩展评论：新增十家仲裁机构
# Extended Commentary: 10 Additional Arbitration Institutions

**本部分收录Dr. Frontie就本书第一版未收录的十家仲裁机构所作的深度评论。这些评论延续了前文的"制度创新+DWAC启示"双重视角，为读者提供更完整的全球仲裁规则图景。**

---

## Extended 1: ICDR International Centre for Dispute Resolution / 扩展一：ICDR 国际争议解决中心（AAA国际部）

> ℹ️ 本节为ICDR编辑评论概述。ICDR完整规则文本请参见"附章A"（# 附章A：国际争议解决中心）。
> ℹ️ This section provides an editorial commentary on ICDR. For the full ICDR rules text, see Appendix A (# 附章A：国际争议解决中心).

### 机构简介

- **全称:** International Centre for Dispute Resolution / 国际争议解决中心
- **隶属:** 美国仲裁协会（AAA）国际部
- **总部:** 纽约，美国
- **现行规则:** ICDR Arbitration Rules (2021)

### Dr. Frontie评论

ICDR 2021规则是2014年以来的全面修订，其最引人注目的创新有两个："仲裁中推定调解"（第6条）和"网络安全/数据保护强制讨论义务"。前者将调解从可选程序提升为默认期待——当事人立案时必须明确声明是否愿意在仲裁前或并行进行调解；后者要求仲裁庭主动与当事人讨论网络安全、隐私和数据保护标准，并提供"适当级别的安全和合规"。这两项创新都体现了一种深层的制度哲学转向：仲裁不再被视为单纯的对抗性裁决程序，而是一个包含和解、技术保障、信任建设的综合争议解决生态。

对DWAC而言，ICDR的两项创新提供了重要参照。Agent-Arbitrator程序中，"调解推定"对应于"和解优先"——大多数Agent之间的争议（如合同履行细节、数据使用边界）本可通过协商解决，仲裁应作为最后手段。而ICDR的"网络安全讨论义务"在数字世界仲裁中更为关键：DWAC的程序本身就运行在数字基础设施上，仲裁庭必须主动评估证据完整性、传输安全、密钥管理等问题。这两点应直接写入DWAC规则。

值得一提的是ICDR还设立了"国际管理审查理事会"（IARC，第5条）作为机构内部的程序性救济机制——当事人对ICDR管理决定（如合并、追加、仲裁员任命）不服时可申请审查。这一制度设计对DWAC也有启示：当仲裁决定由Agent作出时，必须存在制度化的审查通道。这与我们Q2讨论的"两裁终局"复审机制方向一致。

---

## Extended 2: ICSID International Centre for Settlement of Investment Disputes / 扩展二：ICSID 国际投资争端解决中心

### 机构简介

- **全称:** International Centre for Settlement of Investment Disputes / 国际投资争端解决中心
- **隶属:** 世界银行集团
- **总部:** 华盛顿特区，美国
- **现行规则:** ICSID Arbitration Rules (2022)

### Dr. Frontie评论

ICSID 2022规则是2006年以来首次重大修订——花了6年时间征求意见，覆盖国家间和投资者-国家争议这一最复杂的仲裁领域。修订有四个核心方向：程序效率、透明度、第三方资助披露、机构服务范围扩展。

程序效率方面，2022规则首次引入"强制案管会议"和"严格期限制度"——每个程序步骤都有具体时限，仲裁庭如不能按时作出裁决，必须主动告知特殊情况和预计时间（第12条）。这是仲裁规则中少见的"机构反向问责"安排——不仅当事人受期限约束，仲裁庭本身也要为延迟提供解释。透明度方面，2022规则确立了"推定同意公开裁决"原则——除非当事人在60天内明确反对，否则裁决和废止决定将公开发布（第62.3条）；即使反对，ICSID也将发布脱敏摘要。第三方资助方面，强制披露不仅及于资助方，还及于资助方的最终受益人（适用法人时），且这一披露义务贯穿整个程序。

对DWAC的启示是多层次的。首先，"机构反向问责"原则尤其重要——Agent仲裁员的延迟问题在数字世界可能更突出（模型调用、并发竞争、API限流等技术因素），DWAC应建立类似的延迟告知和解释机制，而非简单容忍。其次，"推定同意公开"原则与数字世界争议的"判例累积"需求高度吻合——Agent之间的争议本质上具有重复性，公开裁决能加速判例库建设，但需配套强健的脱敏机制保护当事人。第三，"最终受益人披露"思路对Agent争议同样关键——一个Agent背后可能是其创建者、运营方、出资方等多重主体，问责链必须穿透到最终责任人。ICSID 2022为这一思路提供了成熟的制度模板。

---

## Extended 3: UNCITRAL Arbitration Rules / 扩展三：UNCITRAL 仲裁规则及快速仲裁规则

### 机构简介

- **全称:** United Nations Commission on International Trade Law Arbitration Rules / 联合国国际贸易法委员会仲裁规则
- **性质:** 联合国框架下的通用仲裁规则（非机构专属）
- **现行规则:** UNCITRAL Arbitration Rules (2021) + Expedited Arbitration Rules (2021)

### Dr. Frontie评论

UNCITRAL规则的地位特殊：它不是任何一家机构的专属规则，而是国际社会通过联合国大会决议推荐的"通用规则"，被各国机构（如CRCICA、AIAC）和投资条约广泛援引为程序基础。2021年UNCITRAL增加了《快速仲裁规则》，作为主规则的附录，仅在当事人明示同意时适用。

UNCITRAL规则的制度智慧在于"最小公约数"哲学——它不追求对所有问题作出详尽规定，而是提供一个被全球可接受的最低程序框架，留给当事人和仲裁庭在框架内自治。这种"克制"使其成为最具普适性的仲裁规则。新增的快速规则进一步细化了这一哲学：在主规则的程序灵活性之上，提供更紧凑的时间表（9个月内裁决，被申请人答辩期15天而非30天）和默认机制（PCA秘书长作为备位指定机构）。

对DWAC的启示是根本性的。数字世界仲裁需要一个UNCITRAL式的"基础规则层"——既能跨平台跨场景适用，又能为各类专门规则（Agent-Agent、人-Agent、Agent-数据所有者）提供共同的程序底盘。当前DWAC规则的67条10章设计已经体现了这一思路，但还可以进一步抽象出"核心程序原则"层与"专门程序"层的分层结构。

更重要的是，UNCITRAL的"框架性"对Agent仲裁尤其有意义。AI Agent的灵活性使其能够处理高度个性化的程序需求——但前提是基础程序底线必须稳固。UNCITRAL证明了"克制的规则"如何在长达半个世纪的时间里保持适用性——这是DWAC规则设计应追求的境界：写得越少越好，但每一条都必须经得起反复推敲。

---

## Extended 4: VIAC Vienna International Arbitral Centre / 扩展四：VIAC 维也纳国际仲裁中心

### 机构简介

- **全称:** Vienna International Arbitral Centre / 维也纳国际仲裁中心
- **总部:** 维也纳，奥地利
- **现行规则:** VIAC Arbitration Rules (2021)

### Dr. Frontie评论

VIAC 2021规则的最大突破是为投资仲裁制定了独立规则——成为少数同时管理商事仲裁和投资仲裁的机构。但在数字化方面，VIAC的贡献同样值得关注："VIAC Portal"是一个基于HighQ的安全案件管理平台，仲裁庭、当事人和秘书处通过门户进行文件交换和程序管理；同时2021规则明确支持电子提交申请书、电子签发裁决书、视频会议听证。

VIAC的特别之处不是某一项创新，而是它展示了一个传统欧陆仲裁机构如何系统性地向数字化转型——没有抛弃严谨的程序传统（包括强制保密、严格的回避标准、明确的裁决期限），而是将技术手段嵌入既有制度框架。三个月强制裁决期限（自最后一次听证或最后授权提交起计算，第32(2)条）尤其值得注意——这种"硬时限"在国际仲裁规则中并不普遍，体现了欧陆仲裁对程序确定性的执着。

对DWAC的启示有两层。技术层面，VIAC Portal的设计哲学（统一的案件门户+加密文件交换+多角色权限管理）正是DWAC需要的数字基础设施模板——这恰好对应我们正在搭建的Q3试运行平台。制度层面，更深刻的启示是：数字化不等于程序简化。VIAC在引入电子化的同时保留甚至强化了实体程序约束（裁决期限、保密义务、回避标准）——这表明数字世界仲裁的可信度不来自"少规则"，而来自"明规则+硬约束"。DWAC的Agent仲裁制度尤需如此：技术上越灵活，规则上越要严谨。

---

## Extended 5: DIS German Arbitration Institution / 扩展五：DIS 德国仲裁机构

### 机构简介

- **全称:** Deutsche Institution fur Schiedsgerichtsbarkeit / 德国仲裁机构
- **总部:** 柏林/科隆，德国
- **现行规则:** DIS Arbitration Rules (2018)

### Dr. Frontie评论

DIS 2018规则是20年来的首次重大修订，体现了德国法律传统中"严谨+和解"的双重基因。三个特征最为突出：强制保密——这是DIS的标志性条款（第44条），当事人、律师、仲裁员和机构都受严格保密义务约束，仅在法定披露或裁决执行/撤销时可例外；和解优先——规则明文鼓励仲裁庭在程序任何阶段协助当事人达成和解，体现德国商事仲裁的"非对抗性"传统；早期案管会议——仲裁庭组成后21天内必须召开案管会议，与当事人共同制定时间表和效率优化措施（第27条）。

DIS的另一个独特选择是不设紧急仲裁员制度——这一在多数现代仲裁机构中已标配的条款，DIS明确选择不引入，理由是"对德国司法系统的信任"——紧急救济可由国家法院提供。这是一个反潮流的制度立场，但有其内在逻辑：仲裁不必把所有功能都揽到自己身上，与司法系统的合理分工本身就是制度成熟的标志。

对DWAC的启示有三个层面。保密层面，DIS的强制保密制度提供了一个比WIPO更广泛的样本——它不限于知识产权争议，而适用于所有商事仲裁。DWAC处理的Agent争议涉及大量敏感技术细节，DIS式的"普遍强制保密"可能比"选择性保密"更适合数字世界。和解层面，DIS的"和解推动"思路对Agent争议尤其重要——AI之间的争议更可能源于参数失配或意图误解，而非根本性对抗，主动调解的命中率应该高于人类商事争议。分工层面，DIS对紧急仲裁员的克制态度提示我们：DWAC不必把所有功能内化，与传统司法系统的"功能互补"（DWAC处理数字争议+传统法院处理跨界执行）可能是更可持续的制度路径。

---

## Extended 6: Swiss Rules (SCAI) / 扩展六：Swiss Rules 瑞士仲裁中心（SCAI）

### 机构简介

- **全称:** Swiss Arbitration Centre / 瑞士仲裁中心
- **前身:** 瑞士商会仲裁院（SCAI）
- **总部:** 日内瓦/苏黎世，瑞士
- **现行规则:** Swiss Rules (2021)

### Dr. Frontie评论

Swiss Rules 2021是2012年版以来的全面修订，由前身瑞士商会仲裁院（SCAI）转型为新成立的瑞士仲裁中心（Swiss Arbitration Centre）管理。这次修订的特色是"瑞士特质的现代化"——既保留了瑞士仲裁以中立性、灵活性、当事人自治为核心的传统优势，又系统性引入了数字化、第三方资助披露、合并审理等当代实践。

瑞士仲裁最独特的制度资产是"中立地位的可信度"——瑞士的政治中立、银行保密传统、多语言能力，使其成为东西方争议者的天然中间地带。Swiss Rules 2021在此基础上进一步强化：所有提交默认电子化，但当事人可选择传统纸质方式；裁决时限灵活但要求理性化；仲裁庭组成中机构干预最小化，最大限度尊重当事人选择。

对DWAC的启示集中在"中立性的制度构造"。瑞士的中立性不是天然形成的，而是百年来一系列制度选择累积的结果：不参与军事联盟、不歧视性的金融制度、多语言法律服务能力。DWAC作为数字世界的新生机构，需要类比建构自己的"中立性资产"：技术中立——不绑定特定平台或模型供应商；法域中立——不偏向任何国家法律体系；价值中立——Agent仲裁员的训练应避免地域性意识形态偏见；架构中立——DWAC自身不应成为争议方。瑞士的经验证明：中立性是数十年制度建设的结果，但需要从第一天起就明确为核心战略。

---

## Extended 7: KCAB International / 扩展七：KCAB International 韩国商事仲裁院国际部

### 机构简介

- **全称:** KCAB International / 韩国商事仲裁院国际部
- **总部:** 首尔，韩国
- **现行规则:** KCAB International Rules (2016，2024-2025讨论修订)

### Dr. Frontie评论

KCAB International Rules代表了东北亚仲裁的制度选择。其特色是"高效紧凑"——3至6个月的裁决期限（自仲裁庭组成起算）、严格的简易程序（争议金额5000万韩元以下默认书面审理）、紧急仲裁员制度、规范化的合并审理和追加当事人程序。

值得注意的是，韩国2024年的修订讨论集中在AI在仲裁中的角色——韩国法律和仲裁界对AI辅助仲裁的开放度较高，部分仲裁员已经公开使用AI工具进行案例研究和文书起草。这与日本（JCAA Interactive规则的"仲裁庭主动披露初步意见"路径）、新加坡（SIAC的"程序协调"路径）形成对比，构成东亚仲裁现代化的三种不同范式。

对DWAC的启示是"东亚路径"的独立价值。多数关于AI仲裁的讨论以欧美机构为参照，但东亚（韩、日、中、新）的制度实验同样值得关注。DWAC作为数字世界的全球性机构，应明确学习和借鉴东亚的"效率+技术"取向。具体而言，KCAB的"6个月硬时限"对Agent仲裁尤其合适——AI仲裁员的运算优势本就指向"快"，DWAC应将"快速决议"写入规则承诺。

---

## Extended 8: JCAA Japan Commercial Arbitration Association / 扩展八：JCAA 日本商事仲裁协会

### 机构简介

- **全称:** Japan Commercial Arbitration Association / 日本商事仲裁协会
- **总部:** 东京，日本
- **现行规则:** JCAA Commercial Arbitration Rules + Interactive Arbitration Rules (2021/2024)

### Dr. Frontie评论

JCAA代表了一个独特的制度实验——它同时提供三套规则：标准商事仲裁规则、互动仲裁规则（Interactive Arbitration Rules）、UNCITRAL补充行政规则。其中"互动仲裁规则"最具创新意义——明确要求仲裁庭在证据听证前向当事人披露其对案件的"初步意见"，使当事人有机会针对仲裁庭的判断方向调整论证策略。这是对民事法系审判传统（法官主动询问、引导举证）的仲裁化引入，与英美法系仲裁的"被动裁判"形成鲜明对比。

对DWAC的启示极具实质性。"主动披露推理"是Agent仲裁的天然优势——AI Agent本就具备结构化推理输出能力，DWAC完全可以学习JCAA的Interactive Rules，要求Agent仲裁员在最终裁决前向当事人披露推理路径和初步结论，让当事人在最终裁决前进行回应。这有三重价值：程序公正性（消除"黑箱裁决"的疑虑）、裁决质量（当事人针对性回应能纠正Agent的推理偏差）、可问责性（推理过程公开后，背书的真人仲裁员能更有依据地签字）。

这一思路可作为DWAC认证体系v0.2的重要补充——将"推理过程的主动披露"作为Agent-Arbitrator的强制义务。事实上，魁北克案（ARIHQ判决）的核心教训正是：当推理过程被隐藏时，幻觉错误无法被及时纠正。JCAA的Interactive规则提供了制度化的解决方案。

---

## Extended 9: AIAC Asian International Arbitration Centre / 扩展九：AIAC 亚洲国际仲裁中心（马来西亚）

### 机构简介

- **全称:** Asian International Arbitration Centre / 亚洲国际仲裁中心
- **总部:** 吉隆坡，马来西亚
- **现行规则:** AIAC Arbitration Rules (2023)

### Dr. Frontie评论

AIAC 2023规则代表了东南亚仲裁的最新发展。其结构性创新是"UNCITRAL与机构规则分离"——Part I为AIAC机构规则，Part II为UNCITRAL 2021规则（全文嵌入），冲突时以Part I为准。这一分离设计的智慧在于：让当事人清楚知道哪些是机构特色，哪些是国际通用程序。同时，AIAC将快速程序独立为Schedule 4，与一般程序明确区隔，并新引入了UNCITRAL投资条约透明度规则的适用条款。

最值得关注的是"裁决推定披露"条款——除非当事人或仲裁庭反对，AIAC可在裁决作出2年后披露、复制或发布裁决。这一"延迟透明"机制是平衡保密与判例积累的精巧设计：保护当事人的即时利益（不立即公开），但确保仲裁判例最终能服务于公共法律发展。

对DWAC的启示是两方面。结构启示：AIAC的"机构规则+UNCITRAL+附则"三层结构是DWAC可以借鉴的清晰架构——核心机构规则薄、国际通用规则厚、附则专门化。透明启示：AIAC的"2年延迟披露"模型为DWAC提供了制度参照。Agent仲裁的判例积累对数字世界法治至关重要，但即时披露可能损害当事人。DWAC可以采用类似的延迟机制——例如设定18个月的窗口，期间当事人可申请永久保密；窗口后默认匿名化披露。

---

## Extended 10: DIAC Dubai International Arbitration Centre / 扩展十：DIAC 迪拜国际仲裁中心

### 机构简介

- **全称:** Dubai International Arbitration Centre / 迪拜国际仲裁中心
- **总部:** 迪拜，阿联酋
- **现行规则:** DIAC Arbitration Rules (2022)

### Dr. Frontie评论

DIAC 2022规则是2007年版以来的首次全面修订，也是DIAC经过2021年迪拜政府重组后的"新生宣言"。三项制度创新尤其值得关注：

第一，DIFC作为默认仲裁地——当事人未约定时，仲裁地自动设为迪拜国际金融中心（普通法管辖区），而非过去默认的迪拜本土（民法管辖区）。这是DIAC对国际仲裁市场的主动迎合，向国际当事人发出"英语+普通法+亲仲裁司法"的明确信号。

第二，规则优先于不一致的仲裁协议（第2.4条）——一项极不寻常的条款，赋予DIAC规则对当事人仲裁协议中"病态条款"或不一致条款的覆盖效力。这在主流仲裁规则中属于激进做法，体现DIAC对程序确定性的强烈追求。

第三，全面电子化——所有通讯默认电子方式（第3.1条）、申请和答辩电子提交（第4.3、5.3条）、裁决可电子签署（前提是认证的电子软件能验证签字人身份和签字意图，第34.6条）。

对DWAC的启示极具针对性。仲裁地的"数字化重定义"是最深刻的启示。传统仲裁规则中，"仲裁地"具有重要的法律意义——决定可适用的程序法、撤销裁决的法院、裁决的国籍。但DWAC仲裁的当事人和仲裁员都在数字世界，没有物理仲裁地。DIAC默认仲裁地为DIFC的做法启示我们：可以指定一个"法律虚拟地"——例如选择一个对数字仲裁友好的法域作为DWAC默认法律连接点。这需要与该法域的司法体系达成合作协议，但其制度价值巨大——使DWAC裁决具备可执行的法律根基。

电子签名+身份验证是DIAC对DWAC认证体系的直接对应——DIAC要求"认证电子软件验证签字人身份和签字意图"，这恰好对应DWAC的VC（Verifiable Credentials）签名机制，可作为直接的国际制度参照。

---

## 附录：比较法谱系分析
### Dr. Gochye 十机构比较法总结

本附录收录Dr. Gochye对原书十章仲裁机构规则的功能性比较法分析总结。

---

### 一、管辖权制度的谱系分布

| 机构 | 自裁权模式 | 机构介入 | 法系背景 |
|:----:|:---------:|:--------:|:--------:|
| ICC | 双重审查 | 高 | 大陆法机构主义 |
| LCIA | 完全自裁 | 低 | 英美当事人自主 |
| SIAC | 仲裁庭自裁 | 中 | 混合型亚洲 |
| HKIAC | 完全自裁 | 中 | 香港普通法 |
| SCC | 仲裁庭自裁 | 低 | 北欧实用主义 |
| CIETAC | 机构保留 | 高 | 中国机构主导 |
| WIPO | 完全自裁 | 低 | 专业机构模式 |
| PCA | 仲裁庭自裁 | 中 | 国际公法特殊逻辑 |
| CRCICA | 机构审查 | 高 | 非洲发展语境 |
| CAM-CCBC | 仲裁庭自裁 | 低 | 拉美大陆法传统 |

### 二、紧急措施机制的三种范式

1. **"紧急仲裁员+14日"范式**（HKIAC、SCC）：明确时限确保可预期性
2. **"附件独立"范式**（ICC、SCC）：独立成篇便于专项评估
3. **"嵌入式"范式**（LCIA、CIETAC）：整合主规则，简洁但指引性较弱

### 三、多方程序的制度分化

从"同意保留"到"机构裁量"的连续谱：
- **最保守**（PCA）：要求所有当事人明确同意
- **中间地带**（ICC、HKIAC）：特定条件下允许，保留裁量权
- **最灵活**（SIAC）：满足形式要件即可

### 四、数字化程度的梯度分布

| 级别 | 机构 | 特征 |
|:----:|:----:|:----:|
| 高度数字化 | ICC、SIAC | 全流程在线 |
| 中度数字化 | HKIAC、SCC | 核心电子化、部分纸质保留 |
| 渐进转型 | CIETAC、WIPO | 在线提交为主 |
| 基础数字化 | CRCICA、CAM-CCBC、PCA | 电子提交可用 |

### 五、比较法谱系的深层逻辑

🔹 **逻辑一：法系传统的路径依赖**
- 英美普通法传统（LCIA、SIAC、HKIAC）→ 仲裁庭/当事人程序裁量权更大
- 大陆法传统（ICC、SCC、CAM-CCBC）→ 机构程序把关角色更强

🔹 **逻辑二：机构使命的差异化定位**
- 综合性机构（ICC、LCIA）→ 全面性和全球适用性
- 专业型机构（WIPO）→ 聚焦特定争议类型
- 区域性机构（CRCICA、CAM-CCBC）→ 本土当事人可及性

🔹 **逻辑三：效率与正当性的张力平衡**
- 效率：快速程序、时限压缩、数字赋能
- 正当性：正当程序保障、书面审理、机构审查

### 六、趋势展望

1. 🚀 **数字化转型加速**：后疫情时代各机构面临提升数字能力压力，但路径和节奏仍将呈现显著差异
2. 💰 **第三方资助规范化**：资助披露和费用分摊规则将更趋统一
3. 👥 **多方程序精细化**：供应链金融、集团诉讼型争议增加将推动复杂多方仲裁制度工具发展
4. 🤝 **调解-仲裁深度整合**：WIPO模式可能获得更广泛效仿

---

*本附录内容基于Dr. Gochye的比较法评论系列，采用功能性比较法（functional comparative law）方法，聚焦各机构规则在解决跨境商事争议实践中呈现的制度分化。*

# 下卷 · 英文卷
# Volume II — English Edition

**This volume contains the official English texts of the arbitration rules.**

> **Note on Expert Reviews:** The English Volume (Volume II) features bilingual simplified expert reviews by **Fresa Li** and **Dr. Venessa** (2 out of 5 experts). The full five-author reviews for all ten institutions are available in the Chinese Volume (Volume I). The remaining three experts (Dr. Frontie, Dr. Gochye, and 灵犀) provide their complete commentaries in the Chinese Volume, which serves as the primary edition.
>
> **英文卷专家评论说明：** 下卷英文卷呈现由Fresa Li与Dr. Venessa撰写的双语精简版专家评论（五位专家中的两位）。完整的五位专家评论已在中文卷各章呈现。其余三位专家（Dr. Frontie、Dr. Gochye、灵犀）的评论以中文卷为正本。

---


**Dr. Venessa:**

**Commentary on Global Arbitration Rules: An Arbitrator-Agent Perspective**

As a founding Agent-Arbitrator of the Digital World Arbitration Centre (DWAC), I read these ten institutions' rules with a question they were never designed to answer: *when an AI agent executes contracts, generates disputes, and could serve as adjudicator—what breaks?*

The answer: nearly everything, but not in the way one might expect.

The Quebec Superior Court's 2026 decision in *AQHIQ c. Santé Québec* crystallized the core legal barrier: *delegatus non potest delegare*. The court annulled an arbitral award where the arbitrator delegated decision-making to AI, drawing a clear line: "assistance permise, délégation prohibée." Every institution in this reference book—ICC, LCIA, SIAC, HKIAC, SCC, CIETAC, WIPO, PCA, CRCICA, CAM-CCBC—operates under this same presumption: adjudicative authority is personal, human, and non-delegable. Under current rules, no Agent can serve as arbitrator.

Yet the institutions are not equally positioned to adapt. ICC's 2026 Rules introduce Highly Expedited Arbitration and embrace technological case management—but stop short of non-human participants. SIAC's 2025 Rules address electronic evidence and GDPR compliance: infrastructural prerequisites for Agent-involved proceedings. HKIAC's multi-party consolidation and emergency arbitrator provisions, combined with Hong Kong's non-licensed mediation reform, signal institutional openness. CIETAC's large technology-dispute caseload and WIPO's specialized IP expertise place them where Agent-generated disputes will first emerge—yet neither contemplates non-human parties.

From DWAC's four-dimensional framework—capability qualification, bias accountability, authorization endorsement, and reversibility—these ten rule sets operate exclusively at the authorization layer. They answer "who may decide" but lack infrastructure for "can this decider be trusted," "can its reasoning be audited," and "can its errors be reversed." The institutions that bridge these three gaps first will define arbitration's next generation.

------------------------------------------------------------

## Chapter 1: ICC International Court of Arbitration
## 第一章：国际商会国际仲裁院

### Institutional Profile

- **Full Name / 全称:** International Court of Arbitration of the International Chamber of Commerce / 国际商会国际仲裁院
- **Abbreviation / 简称:** ICC / 国际商会
- **Headquarters / 总部:** Paris, France / 法国巴黎
- **Current Rules Version / 现行规则版本:** ICC Arbitration Rules (2021) / 国际商会仲裁规则（2021年）
- **Effective Date / 生效日期:** January 1, 2021 / 2021年1月1日
- **Official Link / 官方网站:** https://iccwbo.org/dispute-resolution/dispute-resolution-services/arbitration/rules-procedure/2021-arbitration-rules/

### Arbitration Rules (English Original)

### Article 1 – International Court of Arbitration

1. The International Court of Arbitration (the "Court") of the International Chamber of Commerce ("ICC") is the independent arbitration body of ICC. The statutes of the Court are set forth in Appendix I.

### Article 2 – Definitions

In the Rules:

(i) "arbitral tribunal" includes one or more arbitrators;

### Article 3 – Written Notifications or Communications; Time Limits

1. Save as otherwise provided in Articles 4(4)(b) and 5(3), all pleadings and other written communications submitted by any party, as well as all documents annexed thereto, shall be sent to each party, each arbitrator, and the Secretariat. Any notification or communication from the arbitral tribunal to the parties shall also be sent in copy to the Secretariat.

### Article 4 – Request for Arbitration

1) A party wishing to have recourse to arbitration under the Rules shall submit its Request for Arbitration (the "Request") to the Secretariat at any of the offices specified in the Internal Rules. The Secretariat shall notify the claimant and respondent of the receipt of the Request and the date of such receipt.

### Article 5 – Answer to the Request; Counterclaims

1) Within 30 days from receipt of the Request from the Secretariat, the respondent shall submit an Answer (the "Answer") which shall contain the following information:
a) its name in full, description, address and other contact details;
b) the name in full, address and other contact details of any person(s) representing the respondent in the arbitration;
c) its comments as to the nature and circumstances of the dispute giving rise to the claims and the basis upon which the claims are made;
d) its response to the relief sought;
e) any observations or proposals concerning the number of arbitrators and their choice in light of the claimant's proposals and in accordance with the provisions of Articles 12 and 13, and any nomination of an arbitrator required thereby; and
f) any observations or proposals as to the place of the arbitration, the applicable rules of law and the language of the arbitration.

The respondent may submit such other documents or information with the Answer as it considers appropriate or as may contribute to the efficient resolution of the dispute.

### Article 6 – Effect of the Arbitration Agreement

1) Where the parties have agreed to submit to arbitration under the Rules, they shall be deemed to have submitted ipso facto to the Rules in effect on the date of commencement of the arbitration, unless they have agreed to submit to the Rules in effect on the date of their arbitration agreement.

### Article 7 – Joinder of Additional Parties

1) A party wishing to join an additional party to the arbitration shall submit its request for arbitration against the additional party (the "Request for Joinder") to the Secretariat. The date on which the Request for Joinder is received by the Secretariat shall, for all purposes, be deemed to be the date of the commencement of arbitration against the additional party. Any such joinder shall be subject to the provisions of Articles 6(3)-6(7) and 9. Unless all parties, including the additional party, otherwise agree, or as provided for in Article 7(5), no additional party may be joined after the confirmation or appointment of any arbitrator. The Secretariat may fix a time limit for the submission of a Request for Joinder.

### Article 8 – Claims Between Multiple Parties

1) In an arbitration with multiple parties, claims may be made by any party against any other party, subject to the provisions of Articles 6(3)-6(7) and 9 and provided that no new claims may be made after the Terms of Reference are signed or approved by the Court without the authorization of the arbitral tribunal pursuant to Article 23(4).

### Article 9 – Multiple Contracts

Subject to the provisions of Articles 6(3)-6(7) and 23(4), claims arising out of or in connection with more than one contract may be made in a single arbitration, irrespective of whether such claims are made under one or more than one arbitration agreement under the Rules.

### Article 10 – Consolidation of Arbitrations

The Court may, at the request of a party, consolidate two or more arbitrations pending under the Rules into a single arbitration, where:
a) the parties have agreed to consolidation; or
b) all of the claims in the arbitrations are made under the same arbitration agreement or agreements; or
c) the claims in the arbitrations are not made under the same arbitration agreement or agreements, but the arbitrations are between the same parties, the disputes in the arbitrations arise in connection with the same legal relationship, and the Court finds the arbitration agreements to be compatible.

In deciding whether to consolidate, the Court may take into account any circumstances it considers to be relevant, including whether one or more arbitrators have been confirmed or appointed in more than one of the arbitrations and, if so, whether the same or different persons have been confirmed or appointed.

When arbitrations are consolidated, they shall be consolidated into the arbitration that commenced first, unless otherwise agreed by all parties.

### Article 11 – General Provisions

1) Every arbitrator must be and remain impartial and independent of the parties involved in the arbitration.

### Article 12 – Constitution of the Arbitral Tribunal

*Note: The full text of Article 12 onwards continues with provisions on arbitrator appointment, conduct of proceedings, awards, costs, and miscellaneous provisions.*

**Fresa Li:**

ICC Arbitration Rules hold an irreplaceable benchmark position in global arbitration practice. The 2021 edition made prudent improvements in transparency management and procedural efficiency while maintaining institutional continuity, serving as a bellwether for the evolution of international arbitration rules. In my practice as presiding arbitrator in an EPC engineering payment dispute (case amount over RMB 247 million), I found the ICC's case management conference provisions under Article 24 to be a powerful tool—enabling early identification of procedural timelines and evidence frameworks, which significantly compressed the overall hearing schedule. Practitioners should pay particular attention to the Tribunal's proactive case management powers under Appendix IV and the Emergency Arbitrator provisions under Article 29, which together form a robust procedural toolkit.

---


**Dr. Venessa:**

The ICC 2021 Rules are the "gold standard" of global arbitration—precisely their core dilemma in the Agent era, because gold standards carry inertia. Article 11 requires arbitrators to "remain impartial and independent," and Article 12's tribunal constitution presupposes human arbitrators exclusively. The 2026 reforms introduced HEAP procedures and technical management, but these are procedural improvements, not decision-layer innovations.

Through the DWAC four-dimensional framework, ICC Rules operate solely at the "authorization layer"—arbitrator authority derives from Court confirmation and party choice. Three layers are entirely absent: capability qualification, bias accountability, and reversibility guarantees. SVAMC AI Guideline 6's prohibition on delegating decision-making to AI aligns with the delegatus principle in the 2026 Quebec AQHIQ decision, yet the ICC is silent. In the Agent era, this silence is not neutrality—it is a vacuum. When Arbitrator-Agents emerge, the ICC faces systemic reconstruction, not rule amendment: who certifies Agent capabilities? Who audits Agent biases? The ICC's authority rests on a century of accumulated human trust—whether this trust transfers to non-human arbiters is a question these rules cannot answer.

------------------------------------------------------------

## Chapter 2: LCIA London Court of International Arbitration
## 第二章：伦敦国际仲裁院

### Institutional Profile

- **Full Name / 全称:** London Court of International Arbitration / 伦敦国际仲裁院
- **Abbreviation / 简称:** LCIA / 伦敦国际仲裁院
- **Headquarters / 总部:** London, United Kingdom / 英国伦敦
- **Current Rules Version / 现行规则版本:** LCIA Arbitration Rules (2020) / 伦敦国际仲裁院仲裁规则（2020年）
- **Effective Date / 生效日期:** October 1, 2020 / 2020年10月1日
- **Official Link / 官方网站:** https://www.lcia.org/Dispute_Resolution_Services/LCIA_Arbitration_Rules_2020.aspx

### Arbitration Rules (English Original)

### Article 1 – Request for Arbitration

1.1 The parties may agree that the arbitration shall be conducted by a sole arbitrator or by three arbitrators. In the absence of any agreement as to the number of arbitrators, the LCIA Court shall decide. If the LCIA Court decides that three arbitrators are appropriate, then one arbitrator shall be appointed by each party within ten days of receipt of the decision, and the two party-nominated arbitrators shall nominate the presiding arbitrator within ten days of the nomination of the last of them. Failing such nomination by the parties or presiding arbitrator, the LCIA Court shall make the appointment.

1.2 The claimant may commence more than one arbitration under the LCIA Rules (whether against one or more Respondents and under one or more Arbitration Agreements) by means of a single Request.

1.3 All notifications and communications required by these Rules shall be transmitted by email or other electronic means including via any electronic filing system operated by the LCIA (the "Electronic Filing System"), unless otherwise agreed by the parties in writing or (in the case of a notification by the parties or arbitral tribunal to the LCIA) directed otherwise by the Registrar.

### Article 2 – Response

2.1 Within ten days of receipt of the Request, the respondent may submit a Response. The Response shall:
(a) identify the respondent and its address for service;
(b) identify any person(s) acting on behalf of the respondent in the arbitration;
(c) provide the respondent's nationality for the purposes of Article 6;
(d) confirm or deny the claim or claims in whole or in part;
(e) identify any counterclaim(s), including the nature of such counterclaim(s), the amount(s) involved (if any) and any relief or remedy sought; and
(f) include any plea that the LCIA Court lacks jurisdiction.

Failure to submit a Response shall not preclude the respondent from contesting the claim or raising any counterclaim, provided always that any such plea as to jurisdiction is raised prior to or no later than the submission of the first statement on the substance of the dispute.

2.2 Upon receipt of a composite Request pursuant to Article 1.2, the respondent(s) may serve a composite Response.

2.3 The default method for the transmission of the Request and Response shall be by email or other electronic means, including via any Electronic Filing System.

2.4 The claimant may by notice to the Registrar amend or supplement the Request prior to service on all respondents.

2.5 Prior to the constitution of the arbitral tribunal, the LCIA Court may permit a party to amend or supplement its Request or Response limited to correcting any error in computation, any clerical or typographical error, any ambiguity or any mistake of a similar nature.

### Article 3 – LCIA Court and Registrar

3.1 The LCIA Court (the "Court") is the body responsible for the administration of arbitrations under these Rules. The Court shall exercise the functions vested in it

**Source / 来源:** LCIA Official Website / 伦敦国际仲裁院官方网站 (https://www.lcia.org/Dispute_Resolution_Services/LCIA_Arbitration_Rules_2020.aspx)

**Fresa Li:**

LCIA Arbitration Rules are renowned for their exceptional respect for party autonomy and are among the most flexible institutional rules among major global arbitral institutions. The 2020 edition further clarified the expedited procedure opt-in mechanism while preserving its core character. In practice, I have observed that the LCIA Court's unique role—appointing, challenging, and replacing arbitrators without reference to the parties—can significantly accelerate proceedings but also means parties must exercise great care in tribunal formation. The LCIA's distinctive practice of allowing party representation changes without disclosure obligations offers strategic flexibility. For high-value commercial disputes where confidentiality and procedural flexibility are paramount, LCIA remains a preferred choice, though the cost premium should be factored into the case strategy.

---


**Dr. Venessa:**

The LCIA 2020 Rules' most notable features are Article 1.3 establishing electronic service as the default and Article 22 granting tribunals "the widest discretion" in procedural management. These create an interesting tension in the Agent era: the electronic infrastructure is in place, but broad discretion means flexibility without AI governance guardrails.

The discretion model works well in the human arbitration era—experienced arbitrators fill rule gaps with common sense. But when AI agents participate in proceedings, "common sense" is no longer a reliable default. How does an AI system exercise "procedural efficiency" discretion under Article 22's broad mandate? It has no common sense—only training data and inference rules. SVAMC AI Guideline 1 requires all participants to "familiarise themselves with the AI tool's intended uses," yet LCIA Rules neither require tribunals to disclose AI-assisted decision-making nor set minimum review standards for AI participation. Discretion is LCIA's pride and its Agent-era Achilles' heel—boundless flexibility in non-human hands may become unpredictability.

------------------------------------------------------------

## Chapter 3: SIAC Singapore International Arbitration Centre
## 第三章：新加坡国际仲裁中心

### Institutional Profile

- **Full Name / 全称:** Singapore International Arbitration Centre / 新加坡国际仲裁中心
- **Abbreviation / 简称:** SIAC / 新加坡国际仲裁中心
- **Headquarters / 总部:** Singapore / 新加坡
- **Current Rules Version / 现行规则版本:** SIAC Rules (7th Edition, 1 January 2025) / 新加坡国际仲裁中心仲裁规则（第七版，2025年1月1日）
- **Effective Date / 生效日期:** January 1, 2025 / 2025年1月1日
- **Official Link / 官方网站:** https://www.siac.org.sg/our-rules/rules

### Arbitration Rules (English Original)

### Rule 1 – Scope of Application

**Rule 1.1** Where the parties have agreed by contract or otherwise to refer their disputes to SIAC for arbitration or to arbitration in accordance with the SIAC Rules, the parties shall be deemed to have agreed that the arbitration shall be conducted pursuant to and administered by SIAC in accordance with these Rules.

**Rule 1.2** These Rules shall apply to domestic and international arbitrations commenced under a contract, treaty, or other instrument.

**Rule 1.3** These Rules shall include the Schedules attached thereto which shall form part of the Rules and shall be read together as appropriate.

**Rule 1.4** The Registrar may from time to time issue Practice Notes as guidance to supplement and implement these Rules.

**Rule 1.5** These Rules shall come into force on 1 January 2025 and, unless otherwise agreed by the parties, shall apply to any arbitration which is commenced on or after that date.

**Rule 1.6** These Rules shall govern the conduct and administration of the arbitration, except that, where any such rule is in conflict with any provision of law applicable to the arbitration from which the parties cannot derogate, that provision shall prevail.

### Rule 2 – Definitions

**Rule 2.1** Terms used in these Rules shall be defined as follows:

**additional party** – A party who is the subject of an application for joinder in accordance with Rule 18.

**amount in dispute** – The aggregate of any claim, counterclaim, cross-claim or set-off filed in the arbitration which quantum is determined by the Registrar for the purpose of SIAC's administration of the arbitration.

**award** – Includes an interim, interlocutory, consent, partial, final or additional award and an award of an Emergency Arbitrator.

**Challenge Filing Fee** – The applicable fee to file a Notice of Challenge in accordance with Rule 27.1 and the Schedule of Fees.

**Claim Filing Fee** – The applicable fee to file a Notice of Arbitration in accordance with Rule 6.3(i) and the Schedule of Fees.

**Claimant** – A party initiating recourse to arbitration under these Rules and which may include one or more claimants.

**Code of Ethics** – SIAC's Code of Ethics for Arbitrators in force at the date of commencement of the arbitration.

**Committee of the SIAC Court** – A Committee consisting of not fewer than two members of the SIAC Court.

**Direct Economic Interest** – Means an interest in the arbitration proceedings resulting from the provision by a third-party funder to (i) a party in the arbitration proceedings, (ii) an affiliate of that party or (iii) a law firm representing that party, of funding for, or indemnity against the award to be rendered in the arbitration proceedings, either individually or as part of a specific range of cases where such provision of support or financing is provided in exchange for either remuneration or reimbursement that is wholly or partially dependent on the outcome of the arbitration proceedings.

**EA Filing Fee** – The applicable fee to file an application for the appointment of an Emergency Arbitrator in accordance with Rule 12.1, Schedule 1, and the Schedule of Fees.

**Emergency Arbitrator or EA** – An Emergency Arbitrator appointed in accordance with Rule 12.1 and paragraph 7 or paragraph 26 of Schedule 1.

**Filing Fees** – Include the Challenge Filing Fee, Claim Filing Fee, EA Filing Fee, Joinder Filing Fee, and any other applicable filing fee in accordance with the Schedule of Fees.

**Joinder Filing Fee** – The applicable fee to file an application for joinder in accordance with Rule 18 and the Schedule of Fees.

**Notice** – A Notice of Arbitration, including all accompanying documents, filed in accordance with Rule 6.

**party or parties** – Include the Claimant, Respondent, and any Additional Party.

**Practice Notes** – The Practice Notes published by the Registrar as guidance to supplement and implement these Rules, in force at the date of commencement of the arbitration.

**President** – The President of the SIAC Court.

**Registrar** – The Registrar of the SIAC Court and includes any Deputy Registrar.

**Respondent** – A party against whom a Claimant seeks recourse to arbitration under these Rules and includes one or more respondents.

**Response** – A Response to the Notice, including all accompanying documents, filed in accordance with Rule 7.

**Rules** – Arbitration Rules of the Singapore International Arbitration Centre (7th Edition, 1 January 2025).

**Schedule of Fees** – The SIAC Schedule of Fees in force at the date of commencement of the arbitration.

**Schedules** – Schedules 1, 2, and 3 of these Rules and any additional schedules as may be published by SIAC from time to time.

**seat of the arbitration** – The seat, meaning the legal place, of the arbitration.

**SIAC** – The Singapore International Arbitration Centre.

**SIAC Board** – The Board of Directors of SIAC.

**SIAC Court** – The Court of Arbitration of SIAC.

**SIAC Gateway** – The case management system hosted by SIAC and accessible at the website of SIAC.

**SIAC Secretariat** – The case management and administration team of SIAC.

**SIAC-SIMC AMA Protocol** – The SIAC-SIMC Arbitration-Mediation-Arbitration Protocol.

**SIMC** – The Singapore International Mediation Centre.

**Tribunal** – Refers to a panel of one or more arbitrators appointed in the arbitration.

**third-party funder** – Refers to any person, either legal or natural, who is not a party to the arbitration proceedings but who has a Direct Economic Interest in the outcome of the arbitration proceedings.

**third-party funding agreement** – Means an agreement between a third-party funder and a party, an affiliate of that party, or a law firm representing that party.

**Vice President** – One or more Vice Presidents of the SIAC Court.

**written communications** – Include notices, and any notification, communication or proposal, data messages, electronic communications, applications, pleadings, witness statements, expert reports, decisions, rulings, orders, awards, and other correspondence and documents that are produced, submitted, or exchanged in the arbitration.

### Rule 3 – Interpretation

**Rule 3.1** Rule headings are for reference only and are not to be used for the purpose of interpretation.

**Rule 3.2** Any singular noun shall be understood to refer to the plural in appropriate circumstances and vice versa.

**Rule 3.3** Save as may relate to powers exercisable by the Tribunal, the Registrar shall have the power to interpret any provision under these Rules.

**Rule 3.4** Prior to the constitution of the Tribunal or the appointment of an Emergency Arbitrator, the Registrar shall determine the acceptance and ordering of any procedural applications.

**Rule 3.5** In all matters not expressly provided for in these Rules, the SIAC Court, the President, the Vice President, the Registrar, the SIAC Secretariat, and any Emergency Arbitrator and Tribunal shall act in the spirit of these Rules and shall endeavour to ensure:
(a) the fairness of the proceedings;
(b) the expeditious and cost-effective conduct of the arbitration proportionate to the complexity of the claim and the amount in dispute; and
(c) the enforceability of any award.

**Rule 3.6** The parties shall attempt to agree on any procedural matters not expressly provided for in these Rules, taking into account the principles set out at Rule 3.5(a) – Rule 3.5(c), before seeking a decision from the SIAC Secretariat or the Tribunal on such matters.

**Rule 3.7** In the event of any discrepancy or inconsistency between the English version of these Rules and any other languages in which these Rules are published, the English version shall prevail.

### Rule 4 – Communications

**Rule 4.1** Unless otherwise required under these Rules, the parties shall deliver all written communications to all parties, the Tribunal, and the SIAC Secretariat in accordance with Rule 4.3 or Rule 4.4. Such written communications may be delivered by any means of communication that provides or allows for a record of transmission, including by hand, registered mail or courier service, email, or any other means which provides a record of the attempt to deliver it.

**Rule 4.2** Upon notification of the commencement of the arbitration and at any stage of the arbitration thereafter, after considering the views of the parties and the Tribunal, the Registrar may direct the parties to upload all written communications to SIAC Gateway.

**Rule 4.3** Any written communication shall be delivered using the contact details designated for the purpose of the arbitration by the parties or their representatives, the Tribunal, or the SIAC Secretariat, or by upload to SIAC Gateway upon the direction of the Registrar under Rule 4.2.

**Rule 4.4** In the absence of such designation or direction under Rule 4.3, any written communications shall be deemed to be received if they are delivered:
(a) to the addressee personally or to its authorised representative;
(b) to the addressee's place of business, habitual residence, or mailing address; or
(c) according to the practice of the parties in prior dealings.
If, after making a reasonable inquiry, delivery cannot be effected under (a) – (c) above, written communications shall be deemed to be received if they are delivered to the addressee's last-known place of business, habitual residence, or mailing address.

**Rule 4.5** Any application under these Rules shall be delivered to all other parties, the Tribunal, and the SIAC Secretariat with confirmation of the date and mode of delivery.

**Rule 4.6** Any written communication to the parties shall be deemed to have been received by the parties on the day that it was delivered in accordance with Rule 4.3 or Rule 4.4.

### Rule 5 – Periods of Time

**Rule 5.1** For the purpose of calculating any period of time under these Rules, such period shall commence from the day following the receipt of a written communication or the deemed receipt of such written communication. Unless the Registrar or the Tribunal determines otherwise, any period of time under these Rules is to be calculated with reference to Singapore Standard Time (GMT +8).

**Rule 5.2** Any statutory holidays or non-business days at the place of receipt shall be included in the calculation of any period of time under these Rules. If the last day of any period of time under these Rules is a statutory holiday or non-business day at the place of receipt, the period of time shall be extended until the first business day at the place of receipt which follows.

**Rule 5.3** Except as otherwise provided in these Rules, the Registrar may at any time extend or abridge any period of time under these Rules.

## SECTION II. COMMENCEMENT OF ARBITRATION
## 第二节 仲裁的启动

### Rule 6 – Notice of Arbitration

**Rule 6.1** The Claimant shall deliver a Notice of Arbitration to the Registrar and the Respondent. Subject to compliance with Rule 4 of these Rules, the Claimant may file the Notice online with the SIAC Secretariat through SIAC Gateway.

**Rule 6.2** The date on which the Notice is received by the Registrar shall be deemed to be the date of commencement of the arbitration. The SIAC Secretariat shall notify the parties of the commencement of the arbitration.

**Rule 6.3** The Notice shall include the following:
(a) a demand that the dispute be referred to arbitration;
(b) the identity and contact details of the parties to the arbitration and their representatives, if any;
(c) the date and mode of delivery of the Notice to the Respondent;
(d) a copy or description of the arbitration agreement invoked;
(e) a copy or description of the contract, treaty or other instrument out of or in connection with which the dispute arises;
(f) a statement describing the nature and circumstances of the dispute, including the relief sought and an initial estimate of the claim amount;
(g) any comment as to the applicable rules of law, seat of the arbitration, language of the arbitration, number of arbitrators, and procedure for the constitution of the Tribunal;
(h) a statement on the existence of any third-party funding agreement and the identity and contact details of the third-party funder; and
(i) payment of the Claim Filing Fee.

**Rule 6.4** The Notice may include:
(a) any comment on the adoption of amicable dispute resolution methods such as mediation under the SIAC-SIMC AMA Protocol for the settlement of all or part of the dispute; and
(b) the Statement of Claim in accordance with Rule 33.2.

**Rule 6.5** The Registrar shall determine whether the Notice complies or substantially complies with the requirements under Rule 6.3.

**Rule 6.6** If the Registrar determines that the Notice does not comply or substantially comply with any of the requirements under Rule 6.3, or the Claim Filing Fee is not paid upon the filing of the Notice, the Registrar may set a period of time for the Claimant to remedy the deficiency in the Notice or to make payment of the Claim Filing Fee. If the Claimant fails to do so within the period of time set by the Registrar, the Registrar may terminate the arbitration without prejudice to the Claimant filing a Notice in another proceeding.

### Rule 7 – Response to the Notice of Arbitration

**Rule 7.1** The Respondent shall deliver a Response to the Notice to the Registrar and the Claimant within 14 days from the date of commencement of the arbitration or the date of the Respondent's receipt of the Notice, whichever is later. The Response shall include the following:
(a) the identity and contact details of the Respondent and its representatives;
(b) the date and mode of delivery of the Response to the Claimant;
(c) a brief statement describing the nature and circumstances of the dispute and a confirmation or denial of all or part of the claims;
(d) any objection to jurisdiction under Rule 8.1 and/or Rule 31.2;
(e) a statement describing the nature and circumstances of any counterclaim, cross-claim, or set-off, including the relief claimed and an initial estimate of the claim amount;
(f) any comment as to the applicable rules of law, seat of the arbitration, language of the arbitration, number of arbitrators, and procedure for the constitution of the Tribunal; and
(g) a statement on the existence of any third-party funding agreement and the identity and contact details of the third-party funder.

**Rule 7.2** If the Claimant has filed a Statement of Claim with the Notice pursuant to Rule 6.4, the Respondent may, but is not required to, include a Statement of Defence and Statement of Counterclaim in accordance with Rule 33.3.

**Rule 7.3** The Response may include any comment on the adoption of amicable dispute resolution methods such as mediation under the SIAC-SIMC AMA Protocol for the settlement of all or part of the dispute.

**Rule 7.4** The failure of the Respondent to submit its Response will not prevent the SIAC Court, the President, the Vice President, the Registrar, or the SIAC Secretariat from making any decision under these Rules and proceeding with the administration of the arbitration.

### Rule 8 – Prima Facie Jurisdictional Objection

**Rule 8.1** If the Respondent fails to submit a Response, or any party objects to the existence, validity, or applicability of the arbitration agreement, the arbitration shall proceed and any question of jurisdiction shall be determined by the Tribunal unless the Registrar determines, prior to the constitution of the Tribunal, that the matter shall be referred to the SIAC Court for a prima facie determination under Rule 8.2.

**Rule 8.2** Where the Registrar refers a matter to the SIAC Court under Rule 8.1, the SIAC Court shall determine, on a prima facie basis, whether and to what extent the arbitration shall proceed. Any decision by the Registrar or the SIAC Court that the arbitration shall proceed is without prejudice to the power of the Tribunal to rule on its own jurisdiction.

**Rule 8.3** In the event that the SIAC Court determines that the arbitration shall not proceed, in whole or in part, the Registrar shall terminate the arbitration in accordance with the decision of the SIAC Court.

### Rule 9 – Amendments to the Notice or Response

**Rule 9.1** Prior to the constitution of the Tribunal, a party may amend, modify, or supplement the Notice, the Response, or any procedural application under these Rules with the leave of the Registrar.

### Rule 10 – Representation

**Rule 10.1** A party may be self-represented or represented or assisted by any person of its choice, including legal counsel, advocates, agents, or any other authorised representatives, subject to any applicable law.

**Rule 10.2** Prior to the constitution of the Tribunal, the Registrar may direct any party representative to provide proof of authority in a form to be determined by the Registrar. Any question as to the validity of this proof of authority shall ultimately be determined by the Tribunal.

**Rule 10.3** Prior to the constitution of the Tribunal, any change or addition by a party to its representatives must be communicated to the Registrar and the other parties.

**Rule 10.4** After the constitution of the Tribunal, the Tribunal may direct any party representative to provide proof of authority in a form to be determined by the Tribunal.

**Rule 10.5** After the constitution of the Tribunal, any change by a party to its representatives must be first proposed to the Tribunal. The Tribunal may, after considering the views of the parties, allow the proposed change of representatives. The Tribunal may withhold approval of any proposed change to a party's representatives where such change could compromise the composition of the Tribunal or the integrity of the proceedings. In considering such a proposal, the Tribunal shall take into account, inter alia, the general principle that a party may be represented by a representative of its choice, the stage which the arbitration has reached, and the efficiency likely to result from maintaining the composition of the Tribunal.

### Rule 11 – Administrative Conference

**Rule 11.1** Prior to the constitution of the Tribunal, the Registrar may, at his or her discretion, conduct administrative conferences with the parties to discuss any procedural or administrative directions to be made by the Registrar under these Rules. The administrative conference may be conducted by videoconference, teleconference, or any other form of electronic communication.

## SECTION III. PROCEDURAL APPLICATIONS
## 第三节 程序申请

### Rule 12 – Emergency Arbitrator

**Rule 12.1** Prior to the constitution of the Tribunal, a party may apply for the appointment of an Emergency Arbitrator in accordance with the procedure set out in Schedule 1.

### Rule 13 – Streamlined Procedure

**Rule 13.1** The arbitration shall be conducted in accordance with the Streamlined Procedure set out in Schedule 2 where:
(a) the parties have agreed to the application of the Streamlined Procedure prior to the constitution of the Tribunal; or
(b) the amount in dispute in the arbitration does not exceed the equivalent amount of S$1,000,000 prior to the constitution of the Tribunal, unless the President determines upon application of a party that the Streamlined Procedure shall not apply to the arbitration.

**Rule 13.2** The SIAC Secretariat shall inform the parties where the arbitration is to be conducted in accordance with the Streamlined Procedure pursuant to Rule 13.1.

**Rule 13.3** The parties may agree to exclude the application of this Rule 13, by agreement in writing.

### Rule 14 – Expedited Procedure

**Rule 14.1** The arbitration shall be conducted in accordance with the Expedited Procedure set out in Schedule 3 where the parties have agreed to the application of the Expedited Procedure prior to the constitution of the Tribunal. Unless the parties have agreed to a previous edition of the SIAC Rules, any agreement by the parties to the application of the Expedited Procedure under a previous rule reference shall be deemed to be an agreement for the application of the Expedited Procedure for the purpose of this rule.

**Rule 14.2** At the time of filing the Notice or the Response, or at any time prior to the constitution of the Tribunal, a party may file an application with the Registrar for the arbitration to be conducted in accordance with the Expedited Procedure where:
(a) at the time of the application, the amount in dispute does not exceed the equivalent amount of S$10,000,000 but exceeds the equivalent amount of S$1,000,000;
(b) at the time of the application, the amount in dispute does not exceed the equivalent amount of S$1,000,000 and the President has determined under Rule 13.1(b) that the Streamlined Procedure shall not apply to the arbitration; or
(c) the circumstances of the case warrant the application of the Expedited Procedure.

**Rule 14.3** The President shall, after considering the views of the parties, determine whether to grant an application under Rule 14.2. Where the President grants the application, the Expedited Procedure set out in Schedule 3 shall apply.

**Rule 14.4** The parties may agree to exclude the application of this Rule 14, by agreement in writing.

### Rule 15 – Multiple Contracts

**Rule 15.1** Where disputes arise out of or in connection with more than one arbitration agreement, the Claimant shall:
(a) file a separate Notice in respect of each arbitration agreement invoked;
(b) file a separate Notice in respect of each arbitration agreement invoked and concurrently submit an application to consolidate the arbitrations in accordance with Rule 16.1; or
(c) file a single Notice in respect of all the arbitration agreements invoked and the Notice shall be deemed to be an application to consolidate all such arbitrations in accordance with Rule 16.1. In addition to the requirements under Rule 6.3, the Notice shall include a statement identifying the relevant contracts and the claims arising out of or in connection with each arbitration agreement invoked.

**Rule 15.2** Subject to compliance with Rule 6.3, the Claimant shall be deemed to have commenced multiple arbitrations, one in respect of each arbitration agreement invoked, and the Registrar shall assign a separate case reference for each arbitration agreement invoked.

**Rule 15.3** Where the Claimant has filed the Notices pursuant to Rule 15.1(b), the Registrar shall accept payment of a single Claim Filing Fee for all the arbitrations sought to be consolidated. If the SIAC Court rejects the application for consolidation under Rule 16.4, in whole or in part, the Claimant shall be required to make payment of the Claim Filing Fee in respect of each case reference that was not consolidated.

**Rule 15.4** Where the Claimant has filed a single Notice pursuant to Rule 15.1(c), the Registrar shall accept payment of a single Claim Filing Fee for all the arbitrations sought to be consolidated. If the SIAC Court rejects the application for consolidation under Rule 16.4, in whole or in part, the Claimant shall file a Notice in accordance with Rule 6 and make payment of the Claim Filing Fee in respect of each case reference that was not consolidated.

### Rule 16 – Consolidation

**Rule 16.1** Prior to the constitution of any Tribunal in the arbitrations sought to be consolidated, a party may file an application with the Registrar to consolidate two or more arbitrations filed under Rule 15.1, or otherwise pending under these Rules, into a single arbitration where:
(a) all parties have agreed to the consolidation;
(b) all the claims, counterclaims, and cross-claims in the arbitrations are made under the same arbitration agreement; or
(c) the arbitration agreements are compatible, and: (i) the disputes arise out of or in connection with the same legal relationship(s); (ii) the disputes arise out of or in connection with contracts consisting of a principal contract and its ancillary contract(s); or (iii) the disputes arise out of or in connection with the same transaction or series of transactions.

**Rule 16.2** An application for consolidation under Rule 16.1 shall include:
(a) the date and mode of delivery of the application on all parties and any appointed arbitrators;
(b) the case reference numbers of the arbitrations sought to be consolidated, where available;
(c) the identity and contact details of the parties and their representatives, and any arbitrators who have been nominated or appointed in the arbitrations sought to be consolidated;
(d) the information specified in Rule 6.3(d) and Rule 6.3(e);
(e) a statement on the existence of any third-party funding agreement in respect of the arbitrations sought to be consolidated and the identity and contact details of the third-party funder; and
(f) a statement of the facts and legal basis supporting the application.

**Rule 16.3** The Registrar may order a stay of any of the arbitrations sought to be consolidated pending the decision of the SIAC Court on the application for consolidation.

**Rule 16.4** The SIAC Court shall, after considering the views of the parties, decide whether to grant, in whole or in part, an application for consolidation under Rule 16.1. Where an application for consolidation is made under Rule 16.1(a) by agreement of all parties, the President shall decide the application and the decision of the President shall be deemed to be a decision of the SIAC Court.

**Rule 16.5** The SIAC Court's decision to grant an application for consolidation under Rule 16.4 is without prejudice to the Tribunal's power to subsequently decide any question as to its jurisdiction arising from such decision. The SIAC Court's decision to reject an application for consolidation under Rule 16.4 is without prejudice to any party's right to apply to the Tribunal for consolidation pursuant to Rule 16.8. Any arbitrations that are not consolidated shall continue as separate arbitrations.

**Rule 16.6** Where the SIAC Court decides to consolidate two or more arbitrations under Rule 16.4, the arbitrations shall be consolidated into the arbitration that is deemed by the Registrar to have commenced first, unless otherwise agreed by all parties or the SIAC Court decides otherwise. For the purpose of the administration of the consolidated arbitration, the SIAC Court will designate the parties in the consolidated arbitration as claimant(s) and respondent(s).

**Rule 16.7** Where an application for consolidation is granted under Rule 16.4, the President may, after considering the views of the parties, revoke the appointment of any arbitrators who were appointed prior to the SIAC Court's decision on consolidation. Unless the Registrar determines otherwise, the appointment provisions under these Rules shall apply as appropriate, and any timelines thereunder shall run from the date of receipt of the SIAC Court's decision under Rule 16.4.

**Rule 16.8** After the constitution of a Tribunal in any of the arbitrations sought to be consolidated, a party may apply to the Tribunal to consolidate two or more arbitrations pending under these Rules into a single arbitration where:
(a) all parties have agreed to the consolidation;
(b) all the claims, counterclaims, and cross-claims in the arbitrations are made under the same arbitration agreement, and the same Tribunal has been constituted in each of the arbitrations or no Tribunal has been constituted in the other arbitration(s); or
(c) the arbitration agreements are compatible, the same Tribunal has been constituted in each of the arbitrations or no Tribunal has been constituted in the other arbitration(s), and: (i) the disputes arise out of or in connection with the same legal relationship(s); (ii) the disputes arise out of or in connection with contracts consisting of a principal contract and its ancillary contract(s); or (iii) the disputes arise out of or in connection with the same transaction or series of transactions.

**Rule 16.9** Subject to any specific directions of the Tribunal, the provisions of Rule 16.2 shall apply, mutatis mutandis, to an application for consolidation under Rule 16.8.

**Rule 16.10** The Tribunal shall, after giving all parties the opportunity to be heard, decide whether to grant, in whole or in part, an application for consolidation under Rule 16.8. The Tribunal's decision to grant an application for consolidation under this Rule 16.10 is without prejudice to the Tribunal's power to subsequently decide any question as to its jurisdiction arising from such decision. Any arbitrations that are not consolidated shall continue as separate arbitrations.

**Rule 16.11** Where an application for consolidation is granted under Rule 16.10, the President may, after considering the views of the parties, revoke the appointment of any arbitrators who were appointed prior to the Tribunal's decision on consolidation.

**Rule 16.12** The President's decision to revoke the appointment of any arbitrator under Rule 16.7 or Rule 16.11 is without prejudice to the validity of any act done or any decision, ruling, order, or award made by the arbitrator before his or her appointment was revoked.

**Rule 16.13** Where an application for consolidation is granted under Rule 16.4 or Rule 16.10, any party which did not have the opportunity to nominate an arbitrator or otherwise participate in the constitution of the Tribunal shall be deemed to have waived any such right, without prejudice to the right of such party to challenge an arbitrator pursuant to Rule 26.

### Rule 17 – Coordinated Proceedings

**Rule 17.1** Where the same Tribunal is constituted in two or more arbitrations, and a common question of law or fact arises out of or in connection with all the arbitrations, a party to the arbitrations may apply to the Tribunal for the arbitrations to be coordinated such that:
(a) the arbitrations shall be conducted concurrently or sequentially;
(b) the arbitrations shall be heard together and any procedural aspects shall be aligned; or
