36.3 In all cases, the arbitral tribunal shall decide the case in accordance with the terms of the relevant contract(s) and may take into account the usages of the trade applicable to the transaction(s).

### Article 37 – Settlement or Other Grounds for Termination

37.1 If, before the arbitral tribunal is constituted, a party wishes to terminate the arbitration, it shall communicate this to all other parties and HKIAC. HKIAC shall set a time limit for all other parties to indicate whether they agree to terminate the arbitration. If no other party objects within the time limit, HKIAC may terminate the arbitration. If any party objects to the termination of the arbitration, the arbitration shall proceed in accordance with the Rules.

37.2 If, after the arbitral tribunal is constituted and before the final award is made:

(a) the parties settle the dispute, the arbitral tribunal shall either issue an order for the termination of the arbitration or, if requested by the parties and accepted by the arbitral tribunal, record the settlement in the form of an arbitral award on agreed terms. The arbitral tribunal is not obliged to give reasons for such an award.

(b) continuing the arbitration becomes unnecessary or impossible for any reason not mentioned in Article 37.2(a), the arbitral tribunal shall issue an order for the termination of the arbitration. The arbitral tribunal shall issue such an order unless a party raises a justifiable objection, having been given a reasonable opportunity to comment upon the proposed course of action.

37.3 The arbitral tribunal shall communicate copies of the order to terminate the arbitration or of the arbitral award on agreed terms, signed by the arbitral tribunal, to HKIAC. Subject to any lien, HKIAC shall communicate the order for termination of the arbitration or the arbitral award on agreed terms to the parties. Where an arbitral award on agreed terms is made, the provisions of Articles 35.2, 35.3, 35.5 and 35.6 shall apply.

### Article 38 – Correction of the Award

38.1 Within 30 days after receipt of the award, either party, with notice to all other parties, may request the arbitral tribunal to correct in the award any errors in computation, any clerical or typographical errors, or any errors of similar nature. The arbitral tribunal may set a time limit, normally not exceeding 15 days, for all other parties to comment on such request.

38.2 The arbitral tribunal shall make any corrections it considers appropriate within 30 days after receipt of the request but may extend such time limit if necessary.

38.3 The arbitral tribunal may within 30 days after the date of the award make such corrections on its own initiative.

38.4 The arbitral tribunal has the power to make any further correction to the award which is necessitated by or consequential on (a) the interpretation of any point or part of the award under Article 39; or (b) the issue of any additional award under Article 40.

38.5 Such corrections shall be in writing, and the provisions of Articles 35.2 to 35.6 shall apply.

### Article 39 – Interpretation of the Award

39.1 Within 30 days after receipt of the award, either party, with notice to all other parties, may request that the arbitral tribunal give an interpretation of the award. The arbitral tribunal may set a time limit, normally not exceeding 15 days, for all other parties to comment on such request.

39.2 Any interpretation considered appropriate by the arbitral tribunal shall be given in writing within 30 days after receipt of the request but the arbitral tribunal may extend such time limit if necessary.

39.3 The arbitral tribunal has the power to give any further interpretation of the award which is necessitated by or consequential on (a) the correction of any error in the award under Article 38; or (b) the issue of any additional award under Article 40.

39.4 Any interpretation given under Article 39 shall form part of the award and the provisions of Articles 35.2 to 35.6 shall apply.

### Article 40 – Additional Award

40.1 Within 30 days after receipt of the award, either party, with notice to all other parties, may request the arbitral tribunal to make an additional award as to claims presented in the arbitration but omitted from the award. The arbitral tribunal may set a time limit, normally not exceeding 30 days, for all other parties to comment on such request.

40.2 If the arbitral tribunal considers the request for an additional award to be justified, it shall make the additional award within 60 days after receipt of the request but may extend such time limit if necessary.

40.3 The arbitral tribunal has the power to make an additional award which is necessitated by or consequential on (a) the correction of any error in the award under Article 38; or (b) the interpretation of any point or part of the award under Article 39.

40.4 When an additional award is made, the provisions of Articles 35.2 to 35.6 shall apply.

### Article 41 – Deposits for Costs

41.1 As soon as practicable after receipt of the Notice of Arbitration by the Respondent, HKIAC shall, in principle, request the Claimant and the Respondent each to deposit with HKIAC an equal amount as an advance for the costs referred to in Article 34.1(a), (b), (c) and (f). HKIAC shall provide a copy of such request to the arbitral tribunal.

41.2 Where the Respondent submits a counterclaim or cross-claim, or it otherwise appears appropriate in the circumstances, HKIAC may request separate deposits.

41.3 During the course of the arbitration, HKIAC may request the parties to make supplementary deposits with HKIAC. HKIAC shall provide a copy of such request to the arbitral tribunal.

41.4 If the required deposits are not paid in full to HKIAC within 30 days after receipt of the request, HKIAC shall so inform the parties in order that one or another of them may make the required payment. If such payment is not made:

(a) where the arbitral tribunal is not yet constituted, HKIAC may suspend or cease to administer the arbitration;

(b) the arbitral tribunal may order the suspension or termination of the arbitration or continue with the arbitration on such basis and in respect of such claim or counterclaim as the arbitral tribunal considers fit.

41.5 If a party pays the required deposits on behalf of another party, the arbitral tribunal may, at the request of the paying party, make an award for reimbursement of the payment.

41.6 When releasing the final award, HKIAC shall render an account to the parties of the deposits received by HKIAC. Any unexpended balance shall be returned to the parties in the shares in which it was paid by the parties to HKIAC, or as otherwise instructed by the arbitral tribunal.

41.7 HKIAC shall place the deposits made by the parties in an account at a reputable licensed deposit-taking institution. In selecting the account, HKIAC shall have due regard to the possible need to make the deposited funds available immediately.

### Article 42 – Expedited Procedure

42.1 Prior to the constitution of the arbitral tribunal, a party may apply to HKIAC for the arbitration to be conducted in accordance with Article 42.2 where:

(a) the amount in dispute representing the aggregate of any claim and counterclaim (or any set-off defence or cross-claim) does not exceed the amount set by HKIAC, as stated on HKIAC's website on the date the Notice of Arbitration is submitted; or

(b) the parties so agree; or

(c) in cases of exceptional urgency.

42.2 When HKIAC, after considering the views of the parties, grants an application made pursuant to Article 42.1, the arbitral proceedings shall be conducted in accordance with an Expedited Procedure based upon these Rules, subject to the following changes:

(a) the case shall be referred to a sole arbitrator, unless the arbitration agreement provides for three arbitrators;

(b) if the arbitration agreement provides for three arbitrators, HKIAC shall invite the parties to agree to refer the case to a sole arbitrator. If the parties do not agree, the case shall be referred to three arbitrators;

(c) HKIAC may shorten the time limits provided for in the Rules, as well as any time limits that it has set;

(d) after the submission of the Answer to the Notice of Arbitration, the parties shall in principle be entitled to submit one Statement of Claim and one Statement of Defence (and Counterclaim) and, where applicable, one Statement of Defence in reply to the Counterclaim;

(e) the arbitral tribunal shall decide the dispute on the basis of written submissions and documentary evidence only, unless it decides that it is appropriate to hold one or more hearings;

(f) subject to any lien, the award shall be communicated to the parties within six months from the date when HKIAC transmitted the case file to the arbitral tribunal. In appropriate circumstances, HKIAC may extend this time limit;

(g) the arbitral tribunal may state the reasons upon which the award is based in summary form, unless the parties have agreed that no reasons are to be given.

42.3 Upon the request of any party or the arbitral tribunal, and after consulting with the parties and any confirmed or appointed arbitrators, HKIAC may, having regard to any new circumstances that have arisen, decide that the Expedited Procedure under Article 42 shall no longer apply to the case. Unless HKIAC considers that it is appropriate to revoke the confirmation or appointment of any arbitrator, the arbitral tribunal shall remain in place.

### Article 43 – Early Determination Procedure

43.1 The arbitral tribunal shall have the power, at the request of any party and after consulting with all other parties, to decide one or more points of law or fact by way of early determination procedure, on the basis that:

(a) such points of law or fact are manifestly without merit; or

(b) such points of law or fact are manifestly outside the arbitral tribunal's jurisdiction; or

(c) even if such points of law or fact are submitted by another party and are assumed to be correct, no award could be rendered in favour of that party.

43.2 Any party making a request for early determination procedure shall communicate the request to the arbitral tribunal, HKIAC and all other parties.

43.3 Any request for early determination procedure shall be made as promptly as possible after the relevant points of law or fact are submitted, unless the arbitral tribunal directs otherwise.

43.4 The request for early determination procedure shall include the following:

(a) a request for early determination of one or more points of law or fact and a description of such points;

(b) a statement of the facts and legal arguments supporting the request;

(c) a proposal of the form of early determination procedure to be adopted by the arbitral tribunal;

(d) comments on how the proposed form referred to in Article 43.4(c) would achieve the objectives stated in Articles 13.1 and 13.5; and

(e) confirmation that copies of the request and any supporting materials included with it have been or are being communicated simultaneously to all other parties by one or more means of service to be identified in such confirmation.

43.5 After providing all other parties with an opportunity to submit comments on the request, the arbitral tribunal shall issue a decision either dismissing the request or allowing the request to proceed by fixing the early determination procedure in the form it considers appropriate. The arbitral tribunal shall make such decision within 30 days from the date of filing the request. This time limit may be extended by agreement of the parties or, in appropriate circumstances, by HKIAC.

43.6 If the request is allowed to proceed, the arbitral tribunal shall make its order or award, which may be in summary form, on the relevant points of law or fact. The arbitral tribunal shall make such order or award within 60 days from the date of its decision to proceed. This time limit may be extended by agreement of the parties or, in appropriate circumstances, by HKIAC.

43.7 Pending the determination of the request, the arbitral tribunal may decide whether and to what extent the arbitration shall proceed.

### Article 44 – Disclosure of Third Party Funding of Arbitration

44.1 If a funding agreement is made, the funded party shall communicate a written notice to all other parties, the arbitral tribunal, any emergency arbitrator and HKIAC of:

(a) the fact that a funding agreement has been made; and

(b) the identity of the third party funder.

44.2 The notice referred to in Article 44.1 must be communicated:

(a) in respect of a funding agreement made on or before the commencement of the arbitration, in the application for the appointment of an emergency arbitrator, the Notice of Arbitration, the Answer to the Notice of Arbitration, the Request for Joinder or the Answer to the Request for Joinder (as applicable); or

(b) in respect of a funding agreement made after the commencement of the arbitration, as soon as practicable after the funding agreement is made.

44.3 Any funded party shall disclose any changes to the information referred to in Article 44.1 that occur after the initial disclosure.

### Article 45 – Confidentiality

45.1 Unless otherwise agreed by the parties, no party or party representative may publish, disclose or communicate any information relating to:

(a) the arbitration under the arbitration agreement; or

(b) an award or Emergency Decision made in the arbitration.

45.2 Article 45.1 also applies to the arbitral tribunal, any emergency arbitrator, expert, witness, tribunal secretary and HKIAC.

45.3 Article 45.1 does not prevent the publication, disclosure or communication of information referred to in Article 45.1 by a party or party representative:

(a) (i) to protect or pursue a legal right or interest of the party; or

(ii) to enforce or challenge the award or Emergency Decision referred to in Article 45.1;

in legal proceedings before a court or other authority; or

(b) to any government body, regulatory body, court or tribunal where the party is obliged by law to make the publication, disclosure or communication; or

(c) to a professional or any other adviser of any of the parties, including any actual or potential witness or expert; or

(d) to any party or additional party and any confirmed or appointed arbitrator for the purposes of Articles 27, 28, 29 or 30; or

(e) to a person for the purposes of having, or seeking, third party funding of arbitration.

45.4 The deliberations of the arbitral tribunal are confidential.

45.5 HKIAC may publish any award, whether in its entirety or in the form of excerpts or a summary, only under the following conditions:

(a) all references to the parties' names and other identifying information are deleted; and

(b) no party objects to such publication within the time limit fixed for that purpose by HKIAC. In the case of an objection, the award shall not be published.

### Article 45A – Information Security

45A.1 The parties may agree on any reasonable measures to protect information shared, stored, or processed in relation to the arbitration.

45A.2 The arbitral tribunal may, after consulting with the parties, give directions to the parties to protect the security of any information shared, stored, or processed in relation to the arbitration, taking into account the circumstances of the case.

45A.3 The arbitral tribunal may, after consulting with the parties, make a decision, order or award in respect of any breach of the information security measures agreed by the parties or directed by the arbitral tribunal, pursuant to this Article.

### Article 46 – Exclusion of Liability

46.1 None of the Council members of HKIAC nor any body or person specifically designated by it to perform the functions in these Rules, nor the Secretary-General of HKIAC or other staff members of the Secretariat of HKIAC, the arbitral tribunal, any emergency arbitrator, tribunal-appointed expert or tribunal secretary shall be liable for any act or omission in connection with an arbitration conducted under these Rules, save where such act was done or omitted to be done dishonestly.

46.2 After the award has been made and the possibilities of correction, interpretation and additional awards referred to in Articles 38 to 40 have lapsed or been exhausted, neither HKIAC nor the arbitral tribunal, any emergency arbitrator, tribunal-appointed expert or tribunal secretary shall be under an obligation to make statements to any person about any matter concerning the arbitration, nor shall a party seek to make any of these persons a witness in any legal or other proceedings arising out of the arbitration.

*Note: Schedules 1-4 are available in the full HKIAC 2024 Rules document at https://www.hkiac.org/arbitration/rules*

*注：附录1-4的完整内容见HKIAC 2024规则全文，请访问 https://www.hkiac.org/arbitration/rules*
# 附章A：国际争议解决中心

> ℹ️ 本附章收录ICDR完整规则文本。ICDR的编辑评论请参见"扩展评论"部分 Extended 1（## Extended 1: ICDR / 扩展一：ICDR）。
> ℹ️ This appendix contains the full ICDR rules text. For the editorial commentary on ICDR, see Extended Commentary section, Extended 1 (## Extended 1: ICDR / 扩展一：ICDR).

## 机构简介 / Institutional Profile

- **Full Name / 全称:** International Centre for Dispute Resolution / 国际争议解决中心
- **Abbreviation / 简称:** ICDR / 国际争议解决中心
- **Headquarters / 总部:** New York, United States / 美国纽约
- **Current Rules Version / 现行规则版本:** ICDR International Arbitration Rules (2014, as amended 2021) / 国际争议解决中心国际仲裁规则（2014年，2021年修订）
- **Effective Date / 生效日期:** June 1, 2021 / 2021年6月1日
- **Official Link / 官方网站:** https://www.icdr.org/arbitration

**Source / 来源:** ICDR Official Website / 国际争议解决中心官方网站 (https://www.icdr.org/arbitration)

## Rules Text / 规则正文

### Article 1 – Scope of these Rules

1.1 These Rules shall govern the arbitration of disputes arising out of or in connection with contracts that provide for arbitration under the auspices of the ICDR, unless the Rules as applied are amended by the parties.

1.2 These Rules may be adopted in a clause pre-dating a dispute or in a submission agreement.

1.3 Parties shall be deemed to have made these Rules a part of their arbitration agreement if their contract, submission agreement, or clause provides for arbitration under the auspices of the ICDR.

1.4 The Expedited Procedures set forth in the last section of these Rules shall apply where the amount in dispute does not exceed $500,000 USD, exclusive of interest and costs of arbitration, unless the parties have agreed that the Expedited Procedures shall apply regardless of the amount in dispute.

### Commencing the Arbitration

### Article 2 – Notice of Arbitration and Statement of Claim

2.1 A party initiating arbitration (the "Claimant") shall file a Notice of Arbitration and Statement of Claim with any ICDR office. The Notice shall contain:

(a) the names, addresses, telephone numbers, facsimile numbers and email addresses of the parties and their representatives;
(b) a reference to the arbitration agreement invoked;
(c) a reference to the contract or other instrument out of or in relation to which the dispute arises;
(d) a description of the nature and circumstances of the dispute;
(e) a statement of the relief or remedy sought;
(f) a proposal for the number of arbitrators;
(g) the nomination of an arbitrator (if applicable); and
(h) any comments on applicable law and language.

2.2 The date the Notice of Arbitration is received by the ICDR shall be deemed the date of commencement of the arbitration.

### Article 3 – Answer and Counterclaim

3.1 Within 30 days of receipt of the Notice of Arbitration, the Respondent shall file an Answer with the ICDR. The Answer shall include:

(a) confirmation or denial of the claims;
(b) any plea that the tribunal lacks jurisdiction;
(c) comments on the claims and any counterclaims;
(d) comments on the number of arbitrators; and
(e) any nomination of an arbitrator.

3.2 Counterclaims shall be submitted with the Answer and shall contain the matters specified in Article 2.1.

### Article 4 – Administrative Conference

4.1 The Administrator may conduct an administrative conference with the parties to discuss the procedures of the arbitration.

### Article 5 – International Administrative Review Council

5.1 The International Administrative Review Council shall decide arbitrator challenges and other administrative disputes.

### Article 6 – Mediation

6.1 The parties shall discuss in the procedural hearing whether to mediate.

6.2 Mediation may be conducted under the ICDR International Mediation Rules.

### Article 7 – Emergency Measures of Protection

7.1 A party requiring emergency relief prior to the constitution of the tribunal may apply for emergency arbitrator procedures.

7.2 The application shall set forth the nature of the emergency and the relief sought.

7.3 An emergency arbitrator shall be appointed within three business days of filing.

7.4 The emergency arbitrator shall have the power to order or award any interim or conservatory measures necessary.

7.5 The emergency arbitrator shall make a decision within 14 days of appointment.

### Article 8 – Joinder

8.1 A party may request the joinder of an additional party to the arbitration.

8.2 The request shall be filed before the constitution of the tribunal.

8.3 The tribunal, once constituted, may decide on joinder requests.

### Article 9 – Consolidation

9.1 The Administrator may consolidate two or more arbitrations into one where:

(a) the parties agree to consolidation;
(b) all the claims are made under the same arbitration agreement;
(c) the disputes arise out of the same legal relationship; or
(d) the disputes arise out of the same transaction or series of transactions.

### Article 10 – Amendment or Supplement of Claim, Counterclaim, or Defense

10.1 A party may amend or supplement its claim, counterclaim, or defense during the arbitration, subject to the tribunal's discretion.

### Article 11 – Notices

11.1 All notices shall be in writing and may be transmitted by any electronic means.

11.2 A notice shall be deemed received on the day transmitted.

### The Tribunal

### Article 12 – Number of Arbitrators

12.1 The parties may agree on the number of arbitrators.

12.2 If the parties have not agreed, the Administrator shall decide whether there shall be one or three arbitrators.

### Article 13 – Appointment of Arbitrators

13.1 If the tribunal is to consist of a sole arbitrator, the parties shall attempt to agree on the arbitrator.

13.2 If the parties cannot agree within 15 days, the Administrator shall appoint the sole arbitrator.

13.3 If the tribunal is to consist of three arbitrators, each party shall nominate one arbitrator.

13.4 If a party fails to nominate an arbitrator, the Administrator may make the appointment.

### Article 14 – Impartiality and Independence of Arbitrator

14.1 Every arbitrator shall be and remain impartial and independent of the parties.

14.2 A prospective arbitrator shall disclose any circumstances that may give rise to justifiable doubts as to impartiality or independence.

14.3 An arbitrator shall disclose any such circumstances that arise during the arbitration.

### Article 15 – Challenge of an Arbitrator

15.1 A party may challenge an arbitrator if circumstances exist that give rise to justifiable doubts as to impartiality or independence.

15.2 The challenge shall be made within 15 days of the formation of the tribunal or within 15 days of the circumstances becoming known.

15.3 If the other parties agree to the challenge, the arbitrator shall resign.

15.4 If there is no agreement, the Administrator shall decide on the challenge.

### Article 16 – Replacement of an Arbitrator

16.1 An arbitrator shall be replaced if: (a) the arbitrator resigns, dies, or is successfully challenged; (b) the arbitrator is unable or unwilling to act; or (c) the arbitrator's mandate is terminated.

16.2 The replacement procedure shall follow the original appointment procedure.

### Article 17 – Arbitral Tribunal Secretary

17.1 The tribunal may appoint a secretary to assist in the administration of the case.

17.2 The secretary shall be impartial and independent.

### General Conditions

### Article 18 – Party Representation

18.1 Each party may be represented by persons of its choice.

18.2 The names and authority of representatives shall be communicated to the other parties and the tribunal.

### Article 19 – Place of Arbitration

19.1 The place of arbitration shall be as agreed by the parties or determined by the Administrator.

19.2 The tribunal may hold hearings at any place it considers appropriate.

### Article 20 – Language

20.1 The language(s) of the arbitration shall be as agreed by the parties. In the absence of agreement, the tribunal shall determine the language(s).

### Article 21 – Arbitral Jurisdiction

21.1 The tribunal shall have the power to rule on its own jurisdiction, including any objection to the arbitration agreement.

21.2 A plea that the tribunal lacks jurisdiction shall be raised no later than the Statement of Defense.

### Article 22 – Conduct of Proceedings

22.1 The tribunal shall conduct the arbitration in a fair and efficient manner.

22.2 The parties shall act in good faith.

22.3 The tribunal shall decide on all procedural matters.

22.4 The tribunal may use technology to enhance the efficiency of proceedings.

### Article 23 – Early Disposition

23.1 A party may request leave to submit an application for early disposition of any issue that has a reasonable possibility of succeeding and will dispose of or narrow issues.

23.2 The tribunal shall allow such application if it meets the criteria.

### Article 24 – Exchange of Information

24.1 The tribunal shall manage the exchange of information between the parties.

24.2 The parties shall exchange all documents upon which they intend to rely.

24.3 The tribunal may require additional document production.

24.4 Depositions, interrogatories, and requests to admit are generally not appropriate in ICDR arbitration.

### Article 25 – Privilege

25.1 The tribunal shall take into account applicable principles of privilege, such as attorney-client privilege.

### Article 26 – Hearing

26.1 The tribunal shall give reasonable notice of hearings.

26.2 Hearings may be conducted in person or by video, audio, or other electronic means.

26.3 Evidence of witnesses may be presented in written form.

26.4 Hearings are private unless the parties agree otherwise.

### Article 27 – Interim Measures

27.1 The tribunal may order or award any interim or conservatory measures it deems necessary.

27.2 A party may apply for emergency relief prior to constitution of the tribunal under Article 7.

### Article 28 – Tribunal-Appointed Expert

28.1 The tribunal may appoint one or more experts to report on specific issues.

28.2 The parties shall provide the expert with relevant information.

28.3 The expert may participate in hearings.

### Article 29 – Default

29.1 If a party fails to submit its statement or appear at a hearing, the tribunal may proceed with the arbitration.

### Article 30 – Closure of Hearing

30.1 The tribunal shall declare the hearing closed when the parties have presented their cases.

30.2 The tribunal may reopen the hearing if necessary.

### Article 31 – Waiver

31.1 A party who proceeds with the arbitration without stating an objection to any non-compliance shall be deemed to have waived its right to object.

### Article 32 – Awards, Orders, Decisions and Rulings

32.1 Decisions of the tribunal shall be made by a majority of the arbitrators.

32.2 If there is no majority, the presiding arbitrator shall decide.

### Article 33 – Time, Form, and Effect of the Award

33.1 The award shall be made in writing and shall state the reasons upon which it is based.

33.2 The award shall state the date and place of arbitration.

33.3 The award shall be signed by the arbitrators.

33.4 Awards shall be final and binding on the parties.

### Article 34 – Applicable Laws and Remedies

34.1 The tribunal shall decide the dispute in accordance with the law(s) chosen by the parties.

34.2 The tribunal shall not decide as amiable compositeur unless the parties have authorized it to do so.

34.3 Monetary awards may include pre-award and post-award interest.

34.4 Punitive damages are waived unless required by applicable law.

### Article 35 – Settlement or Other Reasons for Termination

35.1 If the parties settle, the tribunal may make a Consent Award recording the settlement.

35.2 If the arbitration terminates for other reasons, the tribunal shall make an appropriate order.

### Article 36 – Interpretation and Correction of Award

36.1 Within 30 days of receipt of an award, a party may request interpretation or correction of errors.

36.2 The tribunal may also correct errors on its own initiative within 30 days.

36.3 An additional award may be requested for claims presented but not determined.

### Article 37 – Costs of Arbitration

37.1 The tribunal shall fix the costs of arbitration in its award.

37.2 Costs include: (a) fees and expenses of arbitrators; (b) costs of assistance required by the tribunal; (c) administrative fees; (d) reasonable legal costs; and (e) costs of interim or emergency relief.

### Article 38 – Fees and Expenses of Arbitral Tribunal

38.1 The fees and expenses of arbitrators shall be reasonable in amount.

38.2 The Administrator shall designate appropriate rates in consultation with the parties and arbitrators.

### Article 39 – Deposits

39.1 The Administrator may request deposits as advance for costs.

39.2 Failure to pay may be deemed a withdrawal of the claim.

### Article 40 – Confidentiality

40.1 The arbitrator and Administrator shall keep the arbitration confidential.

40.2 Awards may be published with consent or as required by law.

### Article 41 – Exclusion of Liability

41.1 The arbitrators, emergency arbitrators, and Administrator shall not be liable for any act or omission in connection with the arbitration.

### Article 42 – Interpretation of Rules

42.1 The tribunal shall interpret these Rules insofar as they relate to its powers and duties.

42.2 The Administrator shall interpret all other Rules.

### International Expedited Procedures

### Article E-1 – Scope of Expedited Procedures

E-1.1 These procedures apply where no claim exceeds $500,000 USD or by agreement of the parties.

### Article E-2 – Detailed Submissions

E-2.1 Parties shall present detailed submissions in the Notice of Arbitration and Answer.

### Article E-3 – Administrative Conference

E-3.1 The Administrator may conduct an administrative conference.

### Article E-4 – Objection to Expedited Procedures

E-4.1 Objections to the applicability of Expedited Procedures may be raised.

### Article E-5 – Changes of Claim or Counterclaim

E-5.1 If a claim exceeds $500,000, the case may continue under Expedited Procedures unless otherwise determined.

### Article E-6 – Appointment and Qualifications of the Arbitrator

E-6.1 A sole arbitrator shall be appointed from a list submitted by the Administrator.

### Article E-7 – Procedural Hearing and Order

E-7.1 The arbitrator shall schedule a procedural hearing and issue a procedural order within 14 days.

### Article E-8 – Proceedings by Written Submissions

E-8.1 All submissions are due within 60 days of the procedural order.

### Article E-9 – Proceedings with an Oral Hearing

E-9.1 Hearings shall take place within 60 days and not exceed one day.

### Article E-10 – The Award

E-10.1 Awards shall be made within 30 days of the closing of the hearing.

**Source:** ICDR Official Website (https://www.icdr.org/sites/default/files/2025_ICDRRules_Web.pdf)



**Fresa Li:**

The 2024 HKIAC Rules demonstrate exceptional procedural sophistication. When handling a construction payment dispute with a Hong Kong nexus, I experienced first-hand the flexibility of HKIAC Article 13 on joinder—its expansion from "a party" to "any person" enables multi-party engineering disputes to be resolved in a single streamlined proceeding, a significant efficiency gain. The HKIAC-administered emergency arbitrator mechanism under Schedule 4 represents a sensible approach with a clear 14-day deadline and well-defined costs framework. HKIAC's strength lies in its bilingual institutional capability and the depth of its arbitrator appointment pool for disputes involving Chinese parties and assets. The Third Party Funding disclosure requirements under Article 44 are a welcome development that enhances procedural integrity.

---


**Dr. Venessa:**

HKIAC 2024 Rules contain the most forward-looking provision among the ten: Article 13.1 tribunals shall conduct proceedings with a view to achieving the effective use of technology. This is the only rule explicitly writing effective technology use into arbitrator obligations. Additionally HKIAC offers the most flexible multi-party joinder and consolidation with well-designed emergency arbitrator and expedited procedures.

But where are the boundaries of effective use of technology? If an Arbitrator-Agent itself constitutes effective technology use does HKIAC provision implicitly authorize Agent arbitration? This open-ended language is HKIAC wisdom and its risk — it neither defines technology boundaries nor excludes Agent possibilities. Through the DWAC framework HKIAC comes closest to the capability qualification layer because effective technology use implies an obligation to scrutinize technological capability. But what are the scrutiny standards? Who determines whether an Agent is effective? In Agent-versus-Agent multi-party disputes — the most common scenario in the digital world — can HKIAC multi-party flexibility accommodate non-human parties? Article 2.7 defines party as including Claimant Respondent and/or additional party without limiting to natural or legal persons — this may be the institutional seam for Agent entry.

------------------------------------------------------------

## Chapter 5: SCC Stockholm Chamber of Commerce
## 第五章：斯德哥尔摩商会仲裁院

### Institutional Profile

- **Full Name / 全称:** Arbitration Institute of the Stockholm Chamber of Commerce / 斯德哥尔摩商会仲裁院
- **Abbreviation / 简称:** SCC / 斯德哥尔摩商会仲裁院
- **Headquarters / 总部:** Stockholm, Sweden / 瑞典斯德哥尔摩
- **Current Rules Version / 现行规则版本:** SCC Arbitration Rules (2023) / 斯德哥尔摩商会仲裁院仲裁规则（2023年）
- **Effective Date / 生效日期:** January 1, 2023 / 2023年1月1日
- **Official Link / 官方网站:** https://sccarbitrationinstitute.se/en/scc-arbitration-rules-english-2023/

### Arbitration Rules (English Original)

### Article 6 – Request for arbitration

A request for arbitration shall include:

### Article 7 – Registration fee

1. Upon filing the request for arbitration, the claimant shall pay a registration fee. The amount of the registration fee shall be determined in accordance with the schedule of costs (Appendix IV) in force on the date the request for arbitration is filed.

### Article 8 – Commencement of arbitration

Arbitration shall be deemed to commence on the date the Secretariat receives the request for arbitration.

### Article 9 – Answer

1. The Secretariat shall send a copy of the request for arbitration and any attached documents to the respondent. The Secretariat shall set a time period within which the respondent shall submit an answer to the SCC. The answer shall include:

### Article 10 – Request for further details

1. The Board may request further details from either party regarding any of their written submissions to the SCC.

### Article 11 – Decisions by the Board

The Board takes decisions as provided under the Arbitration Rules, including deciding:

### Article 12 – Dismissal

The Board shall dismiss a case, in whole or in part, if:

### Article 13 – Joinder of additional parties

1. A party to the arbitration may request that the Board join one or more additional parties to the arbitration.

### Article 14 – Multiple contracts in a single arbitration

1. Parties may make claims arising out of or in connection with more than one contract in a single arbitration.

### Article 15 – Consolidation of arbitrations

1. At the request of a party, the Board may decide to consolidate a newly commenced arbitration with a pending arbitration, if:

### Article 16 – Number of arbitrators

1. The parties may agree on the number of arbitrators.

### Article 17 – Appointment of the Arbitrator

1. The parties may agree on a procedure for the appointment of the Arbitral Tribunal.

### Article 18 – Impartiality, independence and availability

1. Every arbitrator shall be and remain impartial, independent and available throughout the proceedings.

### Article 19 – Challenge to the Arbitrator

1. A party may challenge an arbitrator if circumstances exist that give rise to justifiable doubts as to the arbitrator's impartiality or independence.

### Article 20 – Release from appointment

1. The Board shall release an arbitrator if:

### Article 21 – Replacement of the Arbitrator

1. If an arbitrator is released, the vacancy shall be filled in accordance with the procedure for the original appointment.

### Article 22 – Referral to the Arbitral Tribunal

When the Arbitral Tribunal has been appointed and confirmed and the advance on costs has been paid, the Secretariat shall refer the case to the Arbitral Tribunal.

### Article 23 – Conduct of the arbitration by the Arbitral Tribunal

1. The Arbitral Tribunal shall conduct the proceedings in an efficient and expeditious manner, giving each party a reasonable opportunity to present its case.

### Article 24 – Administrative secretary of the Arbitral Tribunal

1. The Arbitral Tribunal may appoint an administrative secretary to assist in the conduct of the proceedings.

### Article 25 – Seat of arbitration

1. The seat of arbitration shall be as agreed by the parties. In the absence of agreement, the Board shall decide.

### Article 26 – Language

1. The language(s) of the arbitration shall be as agreed by the parties. In the absence of agreement, the Arbitral Tribunal shall determine the language(s).

### Article 27 – Applicable law

1. The Arbitral Tribunal shall decide the dispute in accordance with the law(s) agreed by the parties as applicable to the merits of their dispute.

### Article 28 – Case management conference and timetable

1. After referral of the case to the Arbitral Tribunal, the Arbitral Tribunal shall consult with the parties with a view to establishing a timetable for the proceedings.

### Article 29 – Written submissions

1. The Claimant shall, within the time limit determined by the Arbitral Tribunal, submit a Statement of Claim including:

### Article 30 – Amendments

At any time prior to the close of proceedings, a party may amend or supplement its claim, counterclaim, defence or set-off provided its case, as amended or supplemented, is still comprised by the arbitration agreement.

### Article 31 – Evidence

1. Each party shall have the burden of proving the facts on which it relies for its claim, counterclaim or defence.

### Article 32 – Hearings

1. The Arbitral Tribunal shall decide whether to hold a hearing or whether the proceedings shall be conducted on the basis of documents alone.

### Article 33 – Witnesses

1. Any witness may be heard as a witness of fact or as an expert witness.

### Article 34 – Experts appointed by the Arbitral Tribunal

1. The Arbitral Tribunal may appoint one or more experts to report to it on specific issues.

### Article 35 – Default

1. If the Claimant fails to submit its Statement of Claim, the Arbitral Tribunal may make an award or order.

### Article 36 – Waiver

A party who knows that any provision has not been complied with and proceeds with the arbitration without stating its objection shall be deemed to have waived its right to object.

### Article 37 – Interim measures

1. The Arbitral Tribunal shall have the power, unless otherwise agreed by the parties, to order or award any interim or conservatory measures it deems necessary.

### Article 38 – Security for costs

The Arbitral Tribunal may order any party to provide security for the costs of the arbitration.

### Article 39 – Summary procedure

1. The Arbitral Tribunal may, at the request of a party, decide one or more issues of law or fact by summary procedure.

### Article 40 – Close of proceedings

The Arbitral Tribunal shall declare the proceedings closed when it is satisfied that the parties have had a reasonable opportunity to present their cases.

### Article 41 – Awards and decisions

1. Decisions of the Arbitral Tribunal shall be made by a majority of the arbitrators.

### Article 42 – Making of awards

1. The Arbitral Tribunal shall make its award in writing and shall state the reasons upon which it is based.

### Article 43 – Time limit for final award

The final award shall be made no later than six months from the date the case was referred to the Arbitral Tribunal. The Board may extend this time limit upon a reasoned request from the Arbitral Tribunal.

### Article 44 – Separate award

The Arbitral Tribunal may decide a separate issue or part of the dispute in a separate award.

### Article 45 – Settlement or other grounds for termination of the arbitration

1. If the parties reach a settlement before the final award is made, the Arbitral Tribunal may, at the request of both parties, make a consent award recording the settlement.

### Article 46 – Effect of an award

An award shall be final and binding on the parties when rendered. By agreeing to arbitration under the Arbitration Rules, the parties undertake to carry out any award without delay.

### Article 47 – Correction and interpretation of an award

1. Within 30 days of receiving an award, a party may request that the Arbitral Tribunal correct any clerical, typographical or computational errors in the award, or provide an interpretation of a specific point or part of the award.

### Article 48 – Additional award

Within 30 days of receiving an award, a party may request that the Arbitral Tribunal make an additional award on claims presented in the arbitration but not determined in the award.

### Article 49 – Costs of the arbitration

1. The costs of the arbitration consist of:

### Article 50 – Costs incurred by a party

Unless otherwise agreed by the parties, the Arbitral Tribunal may in the final award order one party to pay any reasonable costs incurred by another party, including costs for legal representation.

### Article 51 – Advance on costs

1. The Board shall determine an amount to be paid by the parties as an advance on costs.

### Article 52 – Exclusion of liability

Neither the SCC, the arbitrator(s), the administrative secretary of the Arbitral Tribunal, nor any expert appointed by the Arbitral Tribunal is liable to any party for any act or omission in connection with the arbitration, unless such act or omission constitutes wilful misconduct or gross negligence.

**Fresa Li:**

SCC Arbitration Rules carry unique weight due to their deep heritage in investment treaty arbitration. The 2023 edition demonstrates a profound understanding of energy and natural resource dispute handling, making SCC the premier institutional choice for Eurasia cross-border investment disputes. I am particularly attentive to Article 23 on interim measures—the SCC Board's authority to decide on emergency relief without prejudicing the Tribunal's ultimate jurisdiction is well-calibrated. The SCC's specialized expertise in disputes involving States and State-owned entities is unmatched among European institutions. Practitioners handling investment arbitrations under the Energy Charter Treaty or bilateral investment treaties involving Russian, CIS, or Nordic counterparties should consider the SCC's appointment mechanism, which effectively balances neutrality with regional expertise.

---


**Dr. Venessa:**

The SCC 2023 Rules embody Nordic minimalism — concise provisions broad discretionary space no redundant design. Article 32 permits remote hearings Article 46 provides final and binding awards yet the entire rules contain no dedicated provisions on technology use or AI governance. This minimalist style is an advantage in human arbitration — less is more and arbitrators fill gaps with professional judgment. But in the Agent era gaps become vacuums.

SCC silence is not prudence — it is absence. When SVAMC has published AI Guidelines SIAC has written GDPR compatibility into its rules and HKIAC has codified effective technology use obligations SCC complete avoidance of AI and Agent issues means it has delegated governance entirely to tribunal discretion. This is the most dangerous arrangement — unguarded discretion with AI participation may produce unpredictable outcomes. A technologically optimistic tribunal may welcome AI-assisted decisions; a skeptical one may reject any AI involvement. Different tribunals under the same institution may produce entirely different AI governance approaches which is more dangerous than having no rules — it creates regulatory uncertainty.

------------------------------------------------------------

## Chapter 6: CIETAC — China International Economic and Trade Arbitration Commission
## 第六章：中国国际经济贸易仲裁委员会

### Institutional Profile


### Arbitration Rules (English Original)

## Institutional Profile / 机构简介

- **Full Name / 全称:** China International Economic and Trade Arbitration Commission (CIETAC) / 中国国际经济贸易仲裁委员会
- **Abbreviation / 简称:** CIETAC / 贸仲
- **Headquarters / 总部:** Beijing, China / 中国北京
- **Current Rules Version / 现行规则版本:** CIETAC Arbitration Rules (2024 Edition) / 中国国际经济贸易仲裁委员会仲裁规则（2024年版）
- **Effective Date / 生效日期:** January 1, 2024 / 2024年1月1日
- **Official Link / 官方网站:** http://www.cietac.org

**Brief History / 简要历史:** Established in 1956 as the Foreign Trade Arbitration Commission of the China Council for the Promotion of International Trade, CIETAC is one of the oldest and most experienced arbitration institutions in China. It has handled over 60,000 cases since its establishment. / 中国国际经济贸易仲裁委员会成立于1956年，前身为中国国际贸易促进委员会对外贸易仲裁委员会，是中国历史最悠久、经验最丰富的仲裁机构之一，自成立以来已处理超过60,000件案件。

**Key Characteristics / 主要特点:** CIETAC specializes in handling international and foreign-related economic and trade disputes, with branches and arbitration centers across China and abroad, including in Hong Kong, Vienna, and North America. / 贸仲专门处理国际及涉外经济贸易争议，在中国境内外设有分会和仲裁中心，包括香港、维也纳和北美。

## Rules Text / 规则正文

### Chapter I — General Provisions
### 第一章 — 总则

### Article 1 — Arbitration Commission
### 第一条 — 仲裁委员会

1. The China International Economic and Trade Arbitration Commission (hereinafter referred to as "the Arbitration Commission"), formerly known as the Foreign Trade Arbitration Commission of the China Council for the Promotion of International Trade and the Foreign Economic and Trade Arbitration Commission of the China Council for the Promotion of International Trade, also uses the name "China International Commercial Arbitration Court."


（一）中国国际经济贸易仲裁委员会（以下简称"仲裁委员会"），原名中国国际贸易促进委员会对外贸易仲裁委员会、中国国际贸易促进委员会对外经济贸易仲裁委员会，同时使用"中国国际商会仲裁院"名称。

2. Where a party has agreed in the arbitration agreement to submit disputes to arbitration by the China Council for the Promotion of International Trade/China International Chamber of Commerce, or to arbitration by the Arbitration Commission or Arbitration Court of the China Council for the Promotion of International Trade/China International Chamber of Commerce, or has used the original name of the Arbitration Commission as the arbitration institution, such party shall be deemed to have agreed to submit the dispute to arbitration by the China International Economic and Trade Arbitration Commission.


（二）当事人在仲裁协议中约定将争议提交中国国际贸易促进委员会/中国国际商会仲裁，或提交中国国际贸易促进委员会/中国国际商会仲裁委员会或仲裁院仲裁，或使用仲裁委员会原名作为仲裁机构的，应视为同意将争议提交中国国际经济贸易仲裁委员会仲裁。

### Article 2 — Organization and Functions
### 第二条 — 机构及职责

1. The President of the Arbitration Commission shall perform the duties assigned by these Rules. Vice Presidents may perform the duties of the President with the authorization of the President.


（一）仲裁委员会主任履行本规则赋予的职责。副主任受主任的委托可以履行主任的职责。

2. The Arbitration Commission has an Arbitration Court which performs the duties prescribed in these Rules under the leadership of an authorized Vice President and the President of the Arbitration Court.


（二）仲裁委员会设有仲裁院，在受委托的副主任和仲裁院院长的指导下履行本规则规定的职责。

3. The Arbitration Commission is based in Beijing. The Arbitration Commission has sub-committees or arbitration centers (listed in Appendix I to these Rules). Sub-committees/arbitration centers are representative offices of the Arbitration Commission which, according to the authorization of the Arbitration Commission, accept arbitration applications and administer arbitration cases.


（三）仲裁委员会设在北京。仲裁委员会设有分会或仲裁中心（本规则附件一）。分会/仲裁中心是仲裁委员会的派出机构，根据仲裁委员会的授权受理仲裁申请、管理仲裁案件。

4. Sub-committees/arbitration centers have their own Arbitration Courts which perform the duties prescribed in these Rules that are to be performed by the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission, under the leadership of the President of the sub-committee/arbitration center Arbitration Court.


（四）分会/仲裁中心设有仲裁院，在分会/仲裁中心仲裁院院长的领导下履行本规则规定由仲裁委员会仲裁院履行的职责。

5. Where a case is administered by a sub-committee/arbitration center, the duties prescribed in these Rules to be performed by the President of the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission shall be performed by the President of the sub-committee/arbitration center Arbitration Court who is authorized by the President of the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission.


（五）分会/仲裁中心管理案件时，本规则规定由仲裁委员会仲裁院院长履行的职责，由仲裁委员会仲裁院院长授权的分会/仲裁中心仲裁院院长履行。

6. The parties may agree to submit disputes to arbitration by the Arbitration Commission or by the sub-committee/arbitration center of the Arbitration Commission. Where arbitration by the Arbitration Commission is agreed, the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission shall accept the arbitration application and administer the case. Where arbitration by a sub-committee/arbitration center is agreed, or where the place of hearing or the place of arbitration is within the province, autonomous region or municipality directly under the Central Government where the sub-committee/arbitration center is located, the Arbitration Court of that sub-committee/arbitration center shall accept the arbitration application and administer the case, unless the parties have agreed otherwise. The Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission may authorize and designate sub-committees/arbitration centers to administer related cases according to the specific circumstances of the case.


（六）当事人可以约定将争议提交仲裁委员会或仲裁委员会的分会/仲裁中心仲裁。约定由仲裁委员会仲裁的，由仲裁委员会仲裁院受理仲裁申请并管理案件。约定由分会/仲裁中心仲裁的，或仲裁地点在分会/仲裁中心所在地的省、自治区、直辖市辖区内的，除当事人另有约定外，由该分会/仲裁中心的仲裁院受理仲裁申请并管理案件。仲裁委员会仲裁院可根据案件具体情况，授权并指定分会/仲裁中心管理相关案件。

Where the agreed sub-committee/arbitration center does not exist, has had its authorization terminated, or the agreement is unclear, the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission shall accept the arbitration application and administer the case. If there is a dispute, it shall be decided by the Arbitration Commission.


约定的分会/仲裁中心不存在、其授权已被终止或者约定不明确的，由仲裁委员会仲裁院受理仲裁申请并管理案件。如有争议，由仲裁委员会决定。

7. The Arbitration Commission may, upon agreement by and request of the parties, provide management and auxiliary services for ad hoc arbitration, including but not limited to providing consultative guidance services on the application of arbitration rules, designating arbitrators/deciding on arbitrator challenges, providing hearing services, reviewing draft awards, and administering arbitrator fees, unless such agreement cannot be implemented or conflicts with mandatory provisions of the applicable arbitration procedural law.


（七）仲裁委员会可根据当事人的约定和请求，为临时仲裁提供管理和辅助服务，包括但不限于提供仲裁规则适用方面的咨询指导意见、指定仲裁员/决定仲裁员回避、提供开庭服务、审阅裁决书草案、代为管理仲裁员报酬等，但该约定无法实施或与仲裁程序适用法律的强制性规定相抵触的除外。

### Article 3 — Scope of Cases
### 第三条 — 案件受理范围

1. The Arbitration Commission shall accept cases of an economic or trade nature, whether contractual or non-contractual, upon agreement of the parties.


（一）仲裁委员会根据当事人的约定，受理契约性或非契约性的经贸等争议案件。

2. The cases mentioned in the preceding paragraph include:
   (1) International or foreign-related dispute cases;
   (2) Dispute cases involving the Hong Kong Special Administrative Region, the Macau Special Administrative Region, and the Taiwan Region;
   (3) Domestic dispute cases.


（二）前款所述争议案件包括：
   （1）国际或涉外争议案件；
   （2）涉及香港特别行政区、澳门特别行政区及台湾地区的争议案件；
   （3）国内争议案件。

### Article 4 — Application of Rules
### 第四条 — 规则的适用

1. These Rules shall apply uniformly to the Arbitration Commission and its sub-committees/arbitration centers.


（一）本规则统一适用于仲裁委员会及其分会/仲裁中心。

2. Where the parties have agreed to submit disputes to arbitration by the Arbitration Commission, they shall be deemed to have agreed to conduct the arbitration in accordance with these Rules.


（二）当事人约定将争议提交仲裁委员会仲裁的，视为同意按照本规则进行仲裁。

3. Where the parties have agreed to submit disputes to arbitration by the Arbitration Commission but have agreed to modify relevant contents of these Rules or to apply other arbitration rules, such agreement shall be followed, unless such agreement cannot be implemented or conflicts with mandatory provisions of the applicable arbitration procedural law. Where the parties have agreed to apply other arbitration rules, the Arbitration Commission shall perform the corresponding management duties.


（三）当事人约定将争议提交仲裁委员会仲裁但约定对本规则有关内容进行变更或约定适用其他仲裁规则的，从其约定，但该约定无法实施或与仲裁程序适用法律的强制性规定相抵触的除外。当事人约定适用其他仲裁规则的，由仲裁委员会履行相应的管理职责。

4. Where the parties have agreed to conduct arbitration in accordance with these Rules but have not designated an arbitration institution, they shall be deemed to have agreed to submit the disputes to the Arbitration Commission.


（四）当事人约定按照本规则进行仲裁但未约定仲裁机构的，视为同意将争议提交仲裁委员会仲裁。

5. Where the parties have agreed to apply specialized arbitration rules of the Arbitration Commission, such agreement shall be followed, unless the dispute does not fall within the scope of application of such specialized arbitration rules, in which case these Rules shall apply.


（五）当事人约定适用仲裁委员会专业仲裁规则的，从其约定，但其争议不属于该专业仲裁规则适用范围的，适用本规则。

### Article 5 — Arbitration Agreement
### 第五条 — 仲裁协议

1. An arbitration agreement refers to an arbitration clause contained in a contract or an arbitration agreement concluded in other forms.


（一）仲裁协议指当事人在合同中订明的仲裁条款或以其他方式达成的书面仲裁协议。

2. An arbitration agreement shall be in writing. An arbitration agreement is in writing if it is concluded in a document signed by the parties, or if it is concluded through telegraphic or telecommunication exchange (including telegrams, telexes, facsimile transmissions, electronic data interchange, emails, etc.) that provides a record of the agreement.


（二）仲裁协议应当采用书面形式。书面形式包括合同书、信件、电报、电传、传真、电子数据交换和电子邮件等可以有形地表现所载内容的形式。

3. The parties may agree to amend the arbitration agreement concluded in a contract, unless the parties have agreed otherwise.


（三）当事人可以约定对合同中订明的仲裁协议进行修改，但当事人另有约定的除外。

4. An arbitration agreement exists independently. The validity of an arbitration agreement shall be determined independently, and the validity of the main contract shall not affect the validity of the arbitration agreement.


（四）仲裁协议独立存在。仲裁协议的效力应单独确定，主合同的效力不影响仲裁协议的效力。

### Article 6 — Objection to Arbitration Agreement and/or Jurisdiction
### 第六条 — 对仲裁协议及/或管辖权的异议

1. A party raising an objection to the arbitration agreement and/or jurisdiction shall file a written application with the Arbitration Commission before the first oral hearing is held or before the filing of the first substantive defense submission if no oral hearing is held. Where a new factual or legal basis for the objection is discovered after the first oral hearing is held or after the first substantive defense submission is filed, the objection shall be raised immediately after such discovery.


（一）当事人对仲裁协议及/或仲裁案件管辖权提出异议的，应当在首次开庭前以书面形式提交仲裁委员会。书面审理的案件，应当在首次实体答辩前提出。仲裁程序适用法律另有规定的，从其规定。

2. The party raising the objection shall provide proof of the objection. The burden of proof for the objection shall be on the objecting party.


（二）提出异议的当事人应当提供证据证明其异议。异议的举证责任由提出异议的当事人承担。

3. The objection shall not affect the continuation of the arbitration proceedings. The objection shall be decided by the arbitral tribunal after it is constituted, unless the Arbitration Commission considers it appropriate to decide the objection before the arbitral tribunal is constituted.


（三）异议不影响仲裁程序的继续进行。异议应当在仲裁庭组成后由仲裁庭作出决定，但仲裁委员会认为有必要在仲裁庭组成前作出决定的除外。

4. Where the arbitral tribunal decides that it has jurisdiction over the case, a party which has previously raised an objection to jurisdiction shall not raise another objection to jurisdiction on the same grounds later in the proceedings. Such party may, however, provide further evidence or reasoning in support of its previous objection.


（四）仲裁庭作出有管辖权的决定后，之前曾提出管辖权异议的当事人不得在后续程序中以相同理由再次提出管辖权异议，但可以进一步提供证据或理由支持其之前的异议。

5. Where the arbitral tribunal decides that it does not have jurisdiction over part of the claims in the arbitration, the arbitration shall proceed with respect to the claims over which the arbitral tribunal decides it has jurisdiction. Where the arbitral tribunal decides that it does not have jurisdiction over all of the claims in the arbitration, the arbitration shall be terminated with respect to such claims.


（五）仲裁庭作出无管辖权决定的，对仲裁庭认定无管辖权的仲裁请求，仲裁程序终止。

6. The objection and/or decision on jurisdiction mentioned above includes objections and/or decisions on party qualification.


（六）上述管辖权异议及/或决定包括对当事人主体资格的异议及/或决定。

7. Where the Arbitration Commission or the arbitral tribunal decides that it does not have jurisdiction, a decision to close the case shall be made. Before the arbitral tribunal is constituted, the decision to close the case shall be made by the President of the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission; after the arbitral tribunal is constituted, such decision shall be made by the arbitral tribunal.


（七）仲裁委员会或仲裁庭作出无管辖权决定的，应当作出撤销案件的决定。撤销案件的决定，在仲裁庭组成前由仲裁委员会仲裁院院长作出，在仲裁庭组成后由仲裁庭作出。

### Article 7 — Place of Arbitration
### 第七条 — 仲裁地

1. The parties may agree on the place of arbitration.


（一）当事人可以对仲裁地作出约定。

2. Where the parties have not agreed on the place of arbitration or their agreement is unclear, the place of arbitration shall be the location of the Arbitration Commission or its sub-committee/arbitration center administering the case; the Arbitration Commission may also determine another place as the place of arbitration based on the specific circumstances of the case.


（二）当事人未约定仲裁地或约定不明确的，以管理案件的仲裁委员会或其分会/仲裁中心所在地为仲裁地；仲裁委员会也可根据案件具体情况确定其他地点为仲裁地。

3. The arbitral award shall be deemed to have been made at the place of arbitration.


（三）仲裁裁决视为在仲裁地作出。

### Article 8 — Service and Time Limits
### 第八条 — 送达及期限

1. All documents, notices, and materials (hereinafter collectively referred to as "arbitration documents") relating to the arbitration may be served by personal delivery, registered mail, express courier service, facsimile, electronic means, or any other means of communication that can provide a record of delivery, or by any other appropriate means as the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission or the arbitral tribunal considers appropriate. Electronic service includes serving arbitration documents by sending them to the email address or other electronic communication address agreed or designated by the parties, as well as serving arbitration documents in electronic form through the information technology storage system of the Arbitration Commission or the information system that the parties can access without obstruction.


（一）所有与仲裁有关的文书、通知和材料（以下统称"仲裁文书"）可采取当面递交、挂号信、特快专递、传真、电子方式及其他能够提供记录的通信方式，或仲裁委员会仲裁院或仲裁庭认为适当的其他方式送达。电子方式送达包括向当事人约定或指定的电子邮箱或其他电子通信地址发送仲裁文书，以及通过仲裁委员会的信息技术存储系统或当事人能够无障碍访问的信息系统以电子形式送达仲裁文书。

2. Electronic service may be prioritized for serving arbitration documents.


（二）仲裁文书的送达可优先采用电子方式。

3. Arbitration documents shall be served on the parties or their arbitration representatives at the address they have provided or agreed upon. Where a party or its arbitration representative has not provided an address or the parties have not agreed on an address, the arbitration documents shall be served at the address provided by the other party or its arbitration representative.


（三）仲裁文书应送达当事人或其仲裁代理人自行提供或约定的地址。当事人或其仲裁代理人未提供地址或当事人未约定地址的，应发送至对方当事人或其仲裁代理人提供的地址。

4. Arbitration documents served on a party or its arbitration representative shall be deemed validly served if delivered by personal delivery to the recipient, or sent to the recipient's place of business, registered address, domicile, habitual residence, or correspondence address, or if, after reasonable inquiry, the other party cannot find any of the above addresses, if sent by registered mail, express courier service, or any other means that can provide a record of delivery, including notarial service, entrusted service, and substituted service, to the recipient's last known place of business, registered address, domicile, habitual residence, or correspondence address.


（四）向当事人或其仲裁代理人送达的仲裁文书，若当面递交收件人，或发送至收件人的营业地、注册地、住所地、惯常居住地或通讯地址，或经合理查询后对方当事人无法找到上述任一地址，以挂号信、特快专递或其他能够提供记录的公证送达、委托送达、留置送达等方式发送至收件人最后为人所知的营业地、注册地、住所地、惯常居住地或通讯地址的，视为有效送达。

5. Time limits prescribed in these Rules shall be calculated from the day following the day when a party has received or should have received arbitration documents sent by the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission.


（五）本规则规定的期限，自当事人收到或应收到仲裁委员会仲裁院发送的仲裁文书之日的次日起计算。

### Article 9 — Good Faith
### 第九条 — 诚实信用

Participants in the arbitration shall comply with the principle of good faith and conduct the arbitration proceedings accordingly.


仲裁参与人应当遵循诚实信用原则进行仲裁程序。

### Article 10 — Waiver of Objection
### 第十条 — 异议的放弃

Where a party knows that any provision or requirement under these Rules or in the arbitration agreement has not been complied with, and yet participates in the arbitration proceedings or continues with the arbitration proceedings, or fails to raise a timely written objection to such non-compliance without valid reason after receiving effective notice, such party shall be deemed to have waived its right to raise an objection.


当事人知道或理应知道本规则或仲裁协议中约定的任何条款或情事未被遵守，但仍参加仲裁程序或继续仲裁程序，或在收到有效通知后无正当理由不按时提出书面异议的，视为放弃其提出异议的权利。

### Chapter II — Arbitration Proceedings
### 第二章 — 仲裁程序

#### Section 1 — Application for Arbitration, Defense, and Counterclaim
#### 第一节 — 仲裁申请、答辩、反请求

### Article 11 — Commencement of Arbitration Proceedings
### 第十一条 — 仲裁程序的开始

Arbitration proceedings shall commence on the date when the Application for Arbitration is received by the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission. Where the applicant submits the Application for Arbitration to the Arbitration Commission in writing and/or applies for arbitration through the online filing system of the Arbitration Commission, the arbitration proceedings shall commence on the earliest date on which such application is received.


仲裁程序自仲裁委员会仲裁院收到仲裁申请书之日起开始。申请人向仲裁委员会提交书面仲裁申请书及/或通过仲裁委员会网上立案系统申请仲裁的，以最先收到该申请之日为仲裁程序开始之日。

### Article 12 — Application for Arbitration
### 第十二条 — 仲裁申请

1. A party applying for arbitration under these Rules shall:
   (1) Submit an Application for Arbitration signed and/or sealed by the applicant or its authorized representative. The Application for Arbitration shall include:
      (i) The name and address of the applicant and the respondent, including postal code, telephone number, facsimile number, email address, or other electronic communication means;
      (ii) The arbitration agreement on which the application for arbitration is based;
      (iii) The facts of the case and the key issues in dispute;
      (iv) The claims of the applicant; and
      (v) The facts and reasons on which the claims are based.
   (2) Submit together with the Application for Arbitration the evidence materials and other supporting documents on which the claims are based.
   (3) Pay the arbitration fee in advance in accordance with the arbitration fee schedule formulated by the Arbitration Commission.


（一）当事人依据本规则申请仲裁时：
   （1）提交由申请人或申请人授权的代理人签名及/或盖章的仲裁申请书。仲裁申请书应写明：
      （i）申请人和被申请人的名称和住所，包括邮政编码、电话、传真、电子邮件或其他电子通讯方式；
      （ii）申请仲裁所依据的仲裁协议；
      （iii）案情和争议要点；
      （iv）申请人的仲裁请求；
      （v）仲裁请求所依据的事实和理由。
   （2）在提交仲裁申请书时，附具申请人请求所依据的证据材料和其他证明文件。
   （3）按照仲裁委员会制定的仲裁费用表预交仲裁费。

2. Where the arbitration agreement provides for pre-arbitration negotiation or mediation procedures, the applicant may submit the Application for Arbitration after such negotiation or mediation, provided that the failure to negotiate or mediate shall not affect the applicant's right to file an Application for Arbitration and the Arbitration Commission's acceptance of the case, unless the applicable law or the arbitration agreement expressly provides otherwise.


（二）仲裁协议规定须经前置协商或调解程序的，申请人可以在协商或调解后提交仲裁申请书，但协商或调解的缺失不影响申请人提交仲裁申请的权利和仲裁委员会仲裁院受理案件，除非适用法律或仲裁协议明确规定相反。

### Article 13 — Acceptance of Cases
### 第十三条 — 案件的受理

1. The Arbitration Commission shall accept a case based on the arbitration agreement reached by the parties before or after the dispute arises and the written application of one party.


（一）仲裁委员会根据当事人在争议发生之前或之后达成的将争议提交仲裁委员会仲裁的仲裁协议和一方当事人的书面申请受理案件。

2. After receiving the Application for Arbitration and its attachments submitted by the applicant, the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission shall, upon examination, if it considers that the formalities for the application for arbitration are complete, send one copy each of the arbitration notice, the Arbitration Rules, and the list of arbitrators to both parties; the applicant's Application for Arbitration and its attachments shall also be sent to the respondent simultaneously.


（二）仲裁委员会仲裁院收到申请人的仲裁申请书及其附件后，经审查，认为申请仲裁的手续完备的，应将仲裁通知、仲裁委员会仲裁规则和仲裁员名册各一份发送给双方当事人；申请人的仲裁申请书及其附件也应同时发送给被申请人。

3. If the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission considers, upon examination, that the formalities for the application for arbitration are not complete, it may require the applicant to complete the formalities within a prescribed time limit. If the applicant fails to complete the formalities within the prescribed time limit, the applicant shall be deemed not to have filed an Application for Arbitration; the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission shall not retain the applicant's Application for Arbitration and its attachments.


（三）仲裁委员会仲裁院经审查，认为申请仲裁的手续不完备的，可以要求申请人在一定期限内予以完备。申请人未能在规定期限内完备申请仲裁手续的，视为申请人未提出仲裁申请；仲裁委员会仲裁院不予保存申请人的仲裁申请书及其附件。

4. After accepting a case, the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission shall designate a case secretary to assist in the procedural management of the arbitration case.


（四）仲裁委员会受理案件后，仲裁委员会仲裁院应指定一名案件秘书协助管理仲裁案件的程序。

### Article 14 — Multi-Contract Arbitration and Addition of Contracts in Arbitration
### 第十四条 — 多份合同的仲裁及仲裁中合同的追加

1. An applicant may file a consolidated application for arbitration for disputes arising under multiple contracts in a single arbitration case, provided that the following conditions are satisfied simultaneously:
   (1) The multiple contracts are principal and ancillary contracts, or the multiple contracts involve the same parties and the legal relationships are of the same nature, or the multiple contracts involve claims that are interconnected;
   (2) The disputes under the multiple contracts arise from the same transaction or the same series of transactions; and
   (3) The arbitration agreements in the multiple contracts are the same or compatible.


（一）申请人可以就多份合同项下的争议在同一仲裁案件中合并提出仲裁申请，但应同时满足下列条件：
   （1）多份合同系主从合同关系，或多份合同当事人相同且法律关系性质相同，或多份合同标的具有关联性；
   （2）多份合同项下的争议源于同一交易或同一系列交易；
   （3）多份合同中仲裁协议的内容相同或具有兼容性。

2. Where conditions in items (1), (2), and (3) of paragraph (1) above are simultaneously satisfied, the applicant may apply to add contracts during the arbitration proceedings, provided that such application is filed in a timely manner so as not to affect the normal conduct of the arbitration proceedings.


（二）同时满足前款第（1）、（2）、（3）项条件的，申请人可以在仲裁程序中申请追加合同，但该申请应及时提出以免影响仲裁程序的正常进行。

3. The procedural matters in paragraphs (1) and (2) above shall be decided by the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission. Where an application to add contracts is filed after the arbitral tribunal is constituted, such decision shall be made by the arbitral tribunal.


（三）本条第一款、第二款的程序事项由仲裁委员会仲裁院决定。合同追加的申请在仲裁庭组成后提出的，由仲裁庭决定。

### Article 15 — Defense Statement
### 第十五条 — 答辩

1. The respondent shall submit a defense statement within 45 days after receiving the arbitration notice. Where the respondent has valid reasons for requesting an extension of the time limit for submitting the defense statement, the arbitral tribunal shall decide whether to extend the time limit for the defense statement; where the arbitral tribunal has not yet been constituted, such decision shall be made by the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission.


（一）被申请人应在收到仲裁通知后45天内提交答辩书。被申请人确有正当理由请求延长答辩期限的，由仲裁庭决定是否延长答辩期限；仲裁庭尚未组成的，由仲裁委员会仲裁院决定。

2. The defense statement shall be signed and/or sealed by the respondent or its authorized representative and shall include the following and attachments:
   (1) The name and address of the respondent, including postal code, telephone number, facsimile number, email address, or other electronic communication means;
   (2) The defense to the Application for Arbitration and the facts and reasons on which the defense is based; and
   (3) The evidence materials and other supporting documents on which the defense is based.


（二）答辩书由被申请人或被申请人授权的代理人签名及/或盖章，并应包括下列内容及附件：
   （1）被申请人的名称和住所，包括邮政编码、电话、传真、电子邮件或其他电子通讯方式；
   （2）对仲裁申请书的答辩及所依据的事实和理由；
   （3）答辩所依据的证据材料和其他证明文件。

3. The arbitral tribunal has the power to decide whether to accept a defense statement submitted after the time limit.


（三）仲裁庭有权决定是否接受逾期提交的答辩书。

4. The failure of the respondent to submit a defense statement shall not affect the conduct of the arbitration proceedings.


（四）被申请人未提交答辩书的，不影响仲裁程序的进行。

### Article 16 — Counterclaim
### 第十六条 — 反请求

1. If the respondent has a counterclaim, the respondent shall submit a counterclaim within 45 days after receiving the arbitration notice. Where the respondent has valid reasons for requesting an extension of the time limit for submitting the counterclaim, the arbitral tribunal shall decide whether to extend the time limit for the counterclaim; where the arbitral tribunal has not yet been constituted, such decision shall be made by the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission.


（一）被申请人如有反请求，应在收到仲裁通知后45天内提交反请求。被申请人确有正当理由请求延长反请求提交期限的，由仲裁庭决定是否延长反请求期限；仲裁庭尚未组成的，由仲裁委员会仲裁院决定。

2. The counterclaim shall be submitted together with the defense statement and shall include:
   (1) The specific claims of the counterclaim and the facts and reasons on which the counterclaim is based; and
   (2) The evidence materials and other supporting documents on which the counterclaim is based.


（二）被申请人提出反请求时，应在反请求申请书中写明具体的反请求事项及其所依据的事实和理由，并附具有关证据材料和其他证明文件。

3. The arbitral tribunal has the power to decide whether to accept a counterclaim submitted after the time limit.


（三）仲裁庭有权决定是否接受逾期提交的反请求。

4. The respondent may submit a counterclaim after submitting the defense statement, provided that the time limit for submitting the counterclaim has not expired. The decision of the arbitral tribunal on whether to accept a late counterclaim shall be made after considering the circumstances of the case.


（四）被申请人可以在提交答辩书后提出反请求，但反请求提交期限尚未届满的除外。仲裁庭对是否接受逾期反请求的决定应考虑案件的具体情况。

### Article 17 — Amendment of Arbitration Claims or Counterclaims
### 第十七条 — 变更仲裁请求或反请求

A party may amend its arbitration claims or counterclaims during the arbitration proceedings, provided that the arbitral tribunal considers the amendment appropriate and the other party agrees or the arbitral tribunal decides, after considering the nature of the amendment, the status of the proceedings, and other relevant circumstances, that the amendment is acceptable.


当事人可以在仲裁程序中对其仲裁请求或反请求提出变更申请，但仲裁庭认为该变更申请提出过迟，影响仲裁程序正常进行的，可以拒绝其变更请求。

### Article 18 — Addition of Party
### 第十八条 — 当事人的追加

1. A third party may, together with the existing parties to the arbitration, jointly apply in writing to the Arbitration Commission for addition as a party to the arbitration proceedings, or a party may apply in writing to the Arbitration Commission for addition of a third party as a party to the arbitration proceedings.


（一）在仲裁程序中，当事人可依据约束拟追加当事人的案涉仲裁协议，向仲裁委员会申请追加当事人。仲裁庭组成后提出当事人追加申请的，当仲裁庭认为确有必要时，应听取包括拟追加当事人在内的各方当事人意见后，由仲裁委员会决定。

2. An application for addition of a party shall include:
   (1) The name and address of the third party proposed to be added;
   (2) The reasons for the addition of the third party; and
   (3) The consent of the third party proposed to be added.


（二）当事人追加申请书应写明现有仲裁案件的案号、拟追加的当事人及各方当事人的名称、住所及通讯方式、追加当事人所依据的仲裁协议、事实和理由以及仲裁请求，并附具相关证据材料和其他证明文件。

3. The application for addition of a party shall be submitted before the closure of the proceedings. Where an application for addition of a party is filed after the arbitral tribunal is constituted, the arbitral tribunal shall decide whether to accept such application after considering the nature of the case, the status of the proceedings, and whether the third party proposed to be added agrees to participate in the arbitration, as well as other relevant circumstances.


（三）当事人申请追加应在程序终结前提出。仲裁庭组成后提出当事人追加申请的，仲裁庭应在考虑案件性质、程序进展状况以及拟追加当事人是否同意参加仲裁等相关情况后决定是否接受该申请。

4. Where the third party proposed to be added has agreed in writing to participate in the arbitration and the arbitral tribunal decides that the addition is appropriate, the third party shall become a party to the arbitration and shall have the rights and obligations of a party. The arbitral tribunal shall inform the parties of the rights and obligations of a party.


（四）拟追加的当事人书面同意参加仲裁且仲裁庭认为追加适当的，该当事人成为仲裁当事人，享有仲裁当事人的权利并承担义务。仲裁庭应告知当事人享有的权利义务。

5. Where the third party proposed to be added does not agree to participate in the arbitration or the arbitral tribunal decides that the addition is not appropriate, the arbitration shall proceed without the third party, and the rights and obligations of the existing parties shall not be affected.


（五）拟追加的当事人不同意参加仲裁或仲裁庭认为追加不适当的，仲裁程序继续进行，且不影响现有当事人的权利义务。

### Article 19 — Consolidated Arbitration
### 第十九条 — 仲裁的合并

1. Where two or more arbitration cases exist simultaneously and satisfy the following conditions, the parties may jointly apply to the Arbitration Commission for consolidated arbitration, or a party may apply to the Arbitration Commission for consolidated arbitration with the consent of the other parties:
   (1) The arbitration agreements in the multiple cases are the same or compatible;
   (2) The disputes in the multiple cases involve the same parties or parties that are legally connected; and
   (3) The disputes in the multiple cases arise from the same transaction or the same series of transactions, or the multiple contracts involve claims that are interconnected.


（一）同时存在两个或两个以上仲裁案件并符合下列条件的，当事人可共同向仲裁委员会申请合并仲裁，或一方当事人经其他当事人同意后向仲裁委员会申请合并仲裁：
   （1）各案件的仲裁申请依据同一仲裁协议；
   （2）各案件的仲裁申请依据多份仲裁协议，且多份合同系主从合同关系，或多份合同当事人相同及法律关系性质相同，或多份合同标的具有关联性，且多份合同仲裁协议内容相同或具有兼容性；
   （3）案件的所有当事人均同意合并仲裁。

2. Where multiple cases are filed by the same parties based on multiple contracts involving related subject matters, the parties may apply to the Arbitration Commission for consolidated arbitration.


（二）相同当事人基于涉及关联标的的多份合同提交多个案件的，当事人可以申请仲裁委员会合并仲裁。

3. The procedural matters in paragraphs (1) and (2) above shall be decided by the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission. Where a consolidated arbitration application is filed after the arbitral tribunal is constituted, such decision shall be made by the arbitral tribunal.


（三）上述第一、二款的程序事项由仲裁委员会仲裁院决定。仲裁庭组成后提出合并仲裁申请的，由仲裁庭决定。

4. Where consolidated arbitration is decided, the arbitration proceedings shall be merged and shall continue from the stage of the earliest pending case.


（四）决定合并仲裁的，仲裁程序合并，并从最先开始的仲裁案件所处阶段继续。

### Article 20 — Submission and Exchange of Arbitration Documents
### 第二十条 — 仲裁文件的提交与交换

1. All arbitration documents shall be submitted to the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission. After receiving arbitration documents, the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission shall promptly transmit them to the other party.


（一）当事人应将仲裁文件提交仲裁委员会仲裁院。仲裁委员会仲裁院收到仲裁文件后，应及时转交对方当事人。

2. The parties shall submit arbitration documents to the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission through the means prescribed in Article 21 of these Rules.


（二）当事人应通过本规则第二十一条规定的方式向仲裁委员会仲裁院提交仲裁文件。

3. The parties shall simultaneously submit copies of the arbitration documents for the other party and the arbitral tribunal (if constituted).


（三）当事人应同时提交仲裁文件副本供对方当事人和仲裁庭（如已组成）使用。

### Article 21 — Means of Submission and Number of Copies
### 第二十一条 — 提交方式及份数

1. The parties may submit arbitration documents through the following means:
   (1) Personal delivery;
   (2) Registered mail or express courier service;
   (3) Facsimile;
   (4) Electronic means, including email, online filing system, or any other means of electronic transmission or delivery that can provide a record.


（一）当事人可通过以下方式提交仲裁文件：
   （1）当面递交；
   （2）挂号信或特快专递；
   （3）传真；
   （4）电子方式，包括电子邮件、网上立案系统或任何其他可提供记录的电子传输或递送方式。

2. The parties shall submit arbitration documents in a number of copies sufficient to provide one copy each for the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission, the other party, and each member of the arbitral tribunal (if constituted).


（二）当事人提交仲裁文件的份数应足以向仲裁委员会仲裁院、对方当事人及仲裁庭（如已组成）每位成员各提供一份。

### Article 22 — Arbitration Representatives
### 第二十二条 — 仲裁代理人

1. A party may authorize Chinese and/or foreign arbitration representatives to handle matters relating to the arbitration. The party or its arbitration representative shall submit a power of attorney to the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission. The Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission shall forward the power of attorney to the relevant parties and the arbitral tribunal.


（一）当事人可以授权中国及/或外国的仲裁代理人办理有关仲裁事项。当事人或其仲裁代理人应向仲裁委员会仲裁院提交授权委托书，仲裁委员会仲裁院应将授权委托书转交相关当事人和仲裁庭。

2. Where a party changes or adds arbitration representatives after the constitution of the arbitral tribunal, the President of the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission may, taking into consideration the opinions expressed by the parties on arbitrator challenge within a reasonable time period and the progress of the case, take necessary measures to prevent conflicts of interest arising from changes in the parties' representation, including excluding new arbitration representatives from participation in the arbitration proceedings.


（二）仲裁庭组成后当事人变更或新增仲裁代理人的，仲裁委员会仲裁院院长可考虑当事人在合理期限内对仲裁员回避事项发表意见的情况、仲裁庭审理案件的进展等因素，采取必要措施防止仲裁员因当事人代理人的变化而产生的利益冲突，包括排除新的仲裁代理人参与仲裁程序。

### Article 23 — Conservatory and Interim Measures
### 第二十三条 — 保全措施及临时措施

1. Where a party applies for preservation measures, the Arbitration Commission shall forward the party's preservation application to the competent court designated by the party. The Arbitration Commission may, at the request of the party, forward the preservation application to the aforementioned court before the issuance of the arbitration notice.


（一）当事人申请保全措施的，仲裁委员会应当将当事人的保全措施申请转交当事人指明的有管辖权的法院。仲裁委员会可依据当事人的请求，将其提交的保全措施申请在仲裁通知发出前先行转交上述法院。

2. In accordance with the applicable law or the agreement of the parties, the parties may apply to the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission for emergency interim relief in accordance with the CIETAC Emergency Arbitrator Procedures (Appendix III to these Rules). The emergency arbitrator may decide on necessary or appropriate emergency interim relief measures. The decision of the emergency arbitrator shall be binding on both parties.


（二）根据所适用的法律或当事人的约定，当事人可以依据《中国国际经济贸易仲裁委员会紧急仲裁员程序》（本规则附件三）向仲裁委员会仲裁院申请紧急性临时救济。紧急仲裁员可以决定采取必要或适当的紧急性临时救济措施。紧急仲裁员决定对双方当事人具有约束力。
