3. At the request of a party, the arbitral tribunal may, in accordance with the applicable law or the agreement of the parties, decide on such interim measures as it considers necessary or appropriate, and may require the party applying for interim measures to provide appropriate security.


（三）经一方当事人请求，仲裁庭依据所适用的法律或当事人的约定可以决定采取其认为必要或适当的临时措施，并有权决定由请求临时措施的一方当事人提供适当的担保。

### Section 2 — Arbitrators and Arbitral Tribunal
### 第二节 — 仲裁员及仲裁庭

### Article 24 — Obligations of Arbitrators
### 第二十四条 — 仲裁员的义务

1. An arbitrator shall not represent any party and shall treat the parties equally from a neutral and independent position.


（一）仲裁员不代表任何一方当事人，应独立于当事人，以中立立场平等对待各方当事人。

2. An arbitrator who has accepted appointment or designation shall perform his or her duties in accordance with these Rules and shall conduct the arbitration proceedings diligently and efficiently.


（二）仲裁员接受选定或指定后，应按照本规则履行职责，勤勉高效地进行仲裁程序。

### Article 25 — Number of Arbitrators
### 第二十五条 — 仲裁庭的人数

1. An arbitral tribunal shall consist of one or three arbitrators.


（一）仲裁庭由一名或三名仲裁员组成。

2. Unless otherwise agreed by the parties or provided otherwise in these Rules, the arbitral tribunal shall consist of three arbitrators.


（二）除非当事人另有约定或本规则另有规定，仲裁庭由三名仲裁员组成。

### Article 26 — Appointment or Designation of Arbitrators
### 第二十六条 — 仲裁员的选定或指定

1. The Arbitration Commission maintains a uniform panel of arbitrators for the Arbitration Commission and its sub-committees/arbitration centers. The parties shall appoint arbitrators from the panel of arbitrators maintained by the Arbitration Commission.


（一）仲裁委员会制定统一适用于仲裁委员会及其分会/仲裁中心的仲裁员名册，当事人从仲裁委员会制定的仲裁员名册中选定仲裁员。

2. Where the parties have agreed to appoint an arbitrator from outside the panel of arbitrators of the Arbitration Commission, the person appointed by the parties or designated in accordance with the parties' agreement may serve as an arbitrator after confirmation by the President of the Arbitration Commission.


（二）当事人约定在仲裁委员会仲裁员名册之外选定仲裁员的，当事人选定的或根据当事人约定指定的人士经仲裁委员会主任确认后可以担任仲裁员。

3. Unless otherwise agreed by the parties, the arbitral tribunal shall be constituted in accordance with the provisions of these Rules.


（三）除当事人另有约定外，仲裁庭应按照本规则的规定组成。

4. Where the parties have agreed on a method of constitution of the arbitral tribunal that is manifestly unjust or unfair, or where the parties have abused their rights causing unnecessary delays to the arbitration proceedings, the President of the Arbitration Commission may decide on the method of constitution of the arbitral tribunal or designate the members of the arbitral tribunal based on the principle of equity.


（四）当事人约定的仲裁庭组成方式明显不公正或不公平的，或当事人滥用权利导致仲裁程序产生不必要延迟的，仲裁委员会主任可根据公平原则决定仲裁庭组成方式或指定仲裁庭成员。

### Article 27 — Constitution of Three-Member Tribunal
### 第二十七条 — 三人仲裁庭的组成

1. The claimant and the respondent shall each appoint one arbitrator or entrust the President of the Arbitration Commission to designate one arbitrator within 15 days after receiving the arbitration notice. If a party fails to appoint or entrust the President to designate within such time limit, the President of the Arbitration Commission shall designate the arbitrator.


（一）申请人和被申请人应在各自收到仲裁通知后15天内各自选定一名仲裁员，或委托仲裁委员会主任指定一名仲裁员。当事人未在上述期限内选定或委托仲裁委员会主任指定的，由仲裁委员会主任指定。

2. The third arbitrator shall be jointly appointed by the parties or jointly entrusted to the President of the Arbitration Commission for designation within 15 days after the respondent receives the arbitration notice. The third arbitrator shall be the presiding arbitrator of the arbitral tribunal. If the parties fail to jointly appoint or jointly entrust the President for designation within such time limit, the President of the Arbitration Commission shall designate the presiding arbitrator.


（二）第三名仲裁员由当事人在被申请人收到仲裁通知后15天内共同选定或共同委托仲裁委员会主任指定。第三名仲裁员为仲裁庭的首席仲裁员。当事人未在上述期限内共同选定或共同委托仲裁委员会主任指定的，由仲裁委员会主任指定首席仲裁员。

3. The parties may agree that the two arbitrators appointed by them shall jointly appoint the presiding arbitrator. The two arbitrators shall jointly appoint the presiding arbitrator or jointly entrust the President of the Arbitration Commission to designate the presiding arbitrator within 7 days after accepting their appointments. If they fail to do so, the President of the Arbitration Commission shall designate the presiding arbitrator.


（三）当事人可以约定由各自选定的两名仲裁员共同选定首席仲裁员。该两名仲裁员应在各自接受选定后7天内共同选定首席仲裁员，或共同委托仲裁委员会主任指定首席仲裁员。逾期未能共同选定或共同委托指定的，由仲裁委员会主任指定首席仲裁员。

4. Each party may recommend 1 to 5 candidates as candidates for presiding arbitrator and submit the recommendation list within the time limit specified in paragraph (2) of this Article. If there is one common candidate in the recommendation lists of the parties, that candidate shall be the presiding arbitrator jointly appointed by the parties. If there are multiple common candidates, the President of the Arbitration Commission shall determine the presiding arbitrator from among them based on the specific circumstances of the case, and that presiding arbitrator shall be deemed to have been jointly appointed by the parties. If there is no common candidate in the recommendation lists, the President of the Arbitration Commission shall designate the presiding arbitrator from outside the recommendation lists.


（四）当事人各方可各自推荐一至五名候选人作为首席仲裁员人选，并在本条第二款规定的期限内提交推荐名单。当事人的推荐名单中有一名相同人选的，该人选为当事人共同选定的首席仲裁员。有数名相同人选的，由仲裁委员会主任根据案件具体情况从中确定一名为首席仲裁员，该首席仲裁员为当事人共同选定的首席仲裁员。推荐名单中没有相同人选的，由仲裁委员会主任在推荐名单之外指定首席仲裁员。

5. With the agreement of the parties or upon the joint request of the parties, the President of the Arbitration Commission may nominate three candidates as candidates for presiding arbitrator. The parties may select the presiding arbitrator from the nomination list within 7 days after receiving such list.


（五）经当事人约定或当事人共同请求，仲裁委员会主任可提名三名首席仲裁员候选人，当事人在收到该提名名单后7天内可以选定首席仲裁员。

### Article 28 — Constitution of Sole Arbitrator Tribunal
### 第二十八条 — 独任仲裁庭的组成

Where the arbitral tribunal consists of a sole arbitrator, the sole arbitrator shall be appointed or designated in accordance with the procedures provided in Article 27, paragraphs (2), (3), and (4) of these Rules.


仲裁庭由一名仲裁员组成的，独任仲裁员按照本规则第二十七条第（二）、（三）、（四）款规定的程序选定或指定。

### Article 29 — Constitution of Multi-Party Tribunal
### 第二十九条 — 多方当事人仲裁庭的组成

1. Where there are two or more claimants and/or respondents in an arbitration case, the claimant side and/or the respondent side shall each, after consultation, jointly appoint or jointly entrust the President of the Arbitration Commission to designate one arbitrator.


（一）仲裁案件中有两名或两名以上申请人及/或被申请人的，申请人侧及/或被申请人侧各方应分别经协商后，共同选定或共同委托仲裁委员会主任指定一名仲裁员。

2. The presiding arbitrator or the sole arbitrator shall be appointed or designated in accordance with the procedures provided in Article 27, paragraphs (2), (4), and (5) of these Rules. When the claimant side and/or the respondent side jointly appoint the presiding arbitrator or the sole arbitrator in accordance with Article 27, they shall submit the list of recommended candidates after joint consultation.


（二）首席仲裁员或独任仲裁员按照本规则第二十七条第（二）、（四）、（五）款规定的程序选定或指定。申请人侧及/或被申请人侧按照本规则第二十七条的规定选定首席仲裁员或独任仲裁员时，应经各方协商后共同提出候选人推荐名单。

3. Where the claimant side and/or the respondent side fail to jointly appoint one arbitrator or jointly entrust the President of the Arbitration Commission to designate one arbitrator within 15 days after receiving the arbitration notice, the President of the Arbitration Commission shall designate all three arbitrators of the arbitral tribunal and determine one of them as the presiding arbitrator.


（三）申请人侧及/或被申请人侧在收到仲裁通知后15天内未能共同选定一名仲裁员或共同委托仲裁委员会主任指定一名仲裁员的，由仲裁委员会主任指定仲裁庭三名仲裁员，并从中确定一名为首席仲裁员。

### Article 30 — Considerations for Designating Arbitrators
### 第三十条 — 指定仲裁员的考虑因素

When designating arbitrators pursuant to these Rules, the President of the Arbitration Commission shall take into consideration the law applicable to the dispute, the place of arbitration, the language of arbitration, the nationalities of the parties, the type of dispute, and other factors the President of the Arbitration Commission considers appropriate.


根据本规则指定仲裁员时，仲裁委员会主任应考虑争议适用的法律、仲裁地、仲裁语言、当事人国籍、争议类型以及仲裁委员会主任认为适当的其他因素。

### Article 31 — Disclosure
### 第三十一条 — 披露

1. An arbitrator who has been appointed or designated shall sign a declaration and disclose any facts or circumstances that may give rise to reasonable doubts as to his or her impartiality or independence.


（一）被选定或被指定的仲裁员应签署声明书，披露可能引起对其公正性和独立性产生合理怀疑的任何事实或情况。

2. If facts or circumstances that are subject to disclosure arise during the arbitration proceedings, the arbitrator shall promptly disclose them in writing.


（二）仲裁程序中，如出现应予披露的事实或情况的，仲裁员应立即书面披露。

3. The arbitrator's declaration and/or disclosure information shall be submitted to the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission and transmitted to the parties and other members of the arbitral tribunal.


（三）仲裁员的声明书及/或披露信息应提交仲裁委员会仲裁院，并转交当事人及仲裁庭其他成员。

### Article 32 — Challenge of Arbitrators
### 第三十二条 — 仲裁员的回避

1. Where a party has received the arbitrator's declaration and/or disclosure and, based on the facts or circumstances disclosed, requests the challenge of that arbitrator, the party shall submit the challenge in writing within 10 days after receiving the arbitrator's disclosure. If a party fails to file a challenge within such time limit, it may not challenge the arbitrator on the basis of matters previously disclosed by the arbitrator.


（一）当事人收到仲裁员的声明书及/或披露书后，根据披露的事实或情况要求该仲裁员回避的，应在收到仲裁员披露书后10天内书面提出。逾期未申请回避的，不得以仲裁员曾经披露的事项为由申请该仲裁员回避。

2. A party may challenge an arbitrator in writing if there are justifiable grounds to doubt the arbitrator's impartiality or independence. The party shall state the specific facts and reasons on which the challenge is based and provide evidence thereof.


（二）当事人对被选定或被指定的仲裁员的公正性和独立性产生具有正当理由的怀疑时，可以书面提出回避申请，但应说明所依据的具体事实和理由，并举证。

3. A challenge shall be submitted in writing within 15 days after receipt of the notice of constitution of the arbitral tribunal. If a party becomes aware of the ground for challenge after the expiry of such time limit, the challenge may be filed within 15 days after becoming aware of such ground. However, the challenge shall be filed no later than the conclusion of the last oral hearing.


（三）回避申请应在收到仲裁庭组成通知后15天内书面提出。期限届满后得知回避事由的，可以在得知回避事由后15天内提出，但最迟应在最后一次开庭审理终结前提出。

4. A challenge shall be promptly communicated to the other party, the challenged arbitrator, and the other members of the arbitral tribunal.


（四）回避申请应迅速送达对方当事人、被申请回避的仲裁员及仲裁庭其他成员。

5. If the other party agrees to the challenge, or if the challenged arbitrator voluntarily proposes to withdraw from the case, the arbitrator shall withdraw. This does not imply the validity of the grounds for challenge.


（五）当事人申请仲裁员回避，对方当事人同意该回避申请，或被申请回避的仲裁员主动提出不再担任该案件仲裁员的，该仲裁员应退出该案件的仲裁工作，但这不表明当事人提出的回避申请理由成立。

6. Except as provided in the preceding paragraph, the President of the Arbitration Commission shall make the final decision on the challenge of an arbitrator without stating reasons.


（六）除前款规定的情形外，仲裁员是否回避，由仲裁委员会主任作出终局决定，无需说明理由。

7. The challenged arbitrator shall continue to perform his or her duties until the President of the Arbitration Commission has decided on the challenge.


（七）被申请回避的仲裁员应继续履行其职责，直至仲裁委员会主任就回避问题作出决定。

### Article 33 — Replacement of Arbitrators
### 第三十三条 — 仲裁员的更换

1. The President of the Arbitration Commission has the power to decide on the replacement of an arbitrator who is de jure or de facto unable to perform his or her functions, or who fails to act in accordance with these Rules or within the time limits prescribed in these Rules. The arbitrator may also resign from his or her position.


（一）仲裁委员会主任有权决定更换在法律上或事实上不能履行其职责的仲裁员，或未能按照本规则或本规则规定期限内履行其职责的仲裁员。该仲裁员也可以自行申请辞去仲裁员职务。

2. The decision on whether to replace an arbitrator shall be finally made by the President of the Arbitration Commission without stating reasons.


（二）是否更换仲裁员，由仲裁委员会主任作出终局决定，无需说明理由。

3. Where an arbitrator is unable to perform his or her duties due to challenge or replacement, the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission shall appoint or designate a substitute arbitrator within a reasonable time in the same manner as the original arbitrator was appointed or designated. If the parties fail to appoint or designate a substitute arbitrator, the President of the Arbitration Commission shall designate the substitute arbitrator.


（三）仲裁员因回避或更换不能履行职责的，仲裁委员会仲裁院应在合理期限内按照原仲裁员选定或指定的方式选定或指定替代仲裁员。当事人未能选定或指定替代仲裁员的，由仲裁委员会主任指定替代仲裁员。

4. After a new arbitrator has been appointed or designated, the arbitral tribunal shall decide whether and to what extent the proceedings already conducted shall be repeated.


（四）新仲裁员选定或指定后，仲裁庭应决定是否以及在何种范围内重复已经进行的程序。

### Article 34 — Continuation of Proceedings by Majority of Arbitrators
### 第三十四条 — 过半数仲裁员继续仲裁程序

After the conclusion of the last hearing, if one of the three arbitrators is unable to participate in deliberations or to render an award due to death, removal, or other reasons, the other two arbitrators may, in accordance with Article 33, request the President of the Arbitration Commission to replace the arbitrator. After hearing the views of the parties and obtaining the consent of the President of the Arbitration Commission, the two arbitrators may continue the proceedings and make decisions or render awards. The Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission shall notify the parties of the above circumstances.


最后开庭审理结束后，三名仲裁员中的一名因死亡、除名等原因不能参加合议及/或作出裁决的，另外两名仲裁员可以按照第三十三条的规定，请求仲裁委员会主任更换仲裁员。在听取当事人意见并经仲裁委员会主任同意后，两名仲裁员可以继续审理并作出决定或裁决。仲裁委员会仲裁院应将上述情况通知当事人。

### Section 3 — Hearing
### 第三节 — 审理

### Article 35 — Methods of Hearing
### 第三十五条 — 审理方式

1. Unless otherwise agreed by the parties, the arbitral tribunal may conduct the hearing in a manner it considers appropriate. In all cases, the arbitral tribunal shall act fairly and impartially and give each party a reasonable opportunity to present its case.


（一）除非当事人另有约定，仲裁庭可以以其认为适当的方式进行审理。在任何情况下，仲裁庭均应公平公正地行事，给予各方当事人陈述与辩论的合理机会。

2. The arbitral tribunal shall hold oral hearings for the case, but if the parties agree and the arbitral tribunal approves, or if the arbitral tribunal considers it unnecessary to hold oral hearings and the parties agree, the arbitral tribunal may conduct a documents-only hearing.


（二）仲裁庭应当开庭审理案件，但当事人约定并经仲裁庭同意，或仲裁庭认为没有必要开庭审理并征得当事人同意的，仲裁庭可以只依据书面文件进行审理。

3. Unless otherwise agreed by the parties, the arbitral tribunal may, based on the specific circumstances of the case, conduct the hearing by means of an inquisitorial or adversarial method.


（三）除非当事人另有约定，仲裁庭可根据案件具体情况采用审问式或辩论式的审理方式审理案件。

4. The arbitral tribunal may deliberate at any place and in any manner it considers appropriate.


（四）仲裁庭可以在其认为适当的任何地点和以任何方式进行合议。

5. Unless otherwise agreed by the parties, the arbitral tribunal may, if it deems necessary, issue procedural orders, issue questionnaires, prepare statements on the scope of the hearing, or hold pre-hearing conferences. The presiding arbitrator may, under the authority of the other members of the arbitral tribunal, make decisions on procedural matters of the arbitration case alone.


（五）除非当事人另有约定，仲裁庭认为必要时，可就所审理的案件发布程序令、发出问题单、制作审理范围书、召开庭前会议等。首席仲裁员在仲裁庭其他成员的授权下，可以单独就仲裁案件的程序安排作出决定。

### Article 36 — Place of Hearing
### 第三十六条 — 开庭地点

1. Where the parties have agreed on the place of hearing, the oral hearing of the arbitration case shall be held at the agreed place, except as provided in Article 85(3) of these Rules.


（一）当事人约定了开庭地点的，仲裁案件的开庭审理应在约定的地点进行，但本规则第八十五条第（三）款规定的情形除外。

2. Unless otherwise agreed by the parties, the hearing of a case administered by the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission or its sub-committee/arbitration center Arbitration Court shall be held in Beijing or at the location of the sub-committee/arbitration center, as applicable. If the arbitral tribunal considers it necessary, it may hold oral hearings at another location with the consent of the President of the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission.


（二）除非当事人另有约定，由仲裁委员会仲裁院或其分会/仲裁中心仲裁院管理的案件的审理，分别在北京或分会/仲裁中心所在地进行。仲裁庭认为必要的，经仲裁委员会仲裁院院长同意，可以在其他地点开庭审理。

### Article 37 — Oral Hearing
### 第三十七条 — 开庭审理

1. Where an oral hearing is to be held, the arbitral tribunal shall, after fixing the date of the first oral hearing, notify the parties of the date of the hearing at least 20 days before the hearing. A party may request a postponement of the hearing if it has justified reasons, provided that the request for postponement is submitted within 5 days after receiving the hearing notice. The decision to postpone shall be made by the arbitral tribunal.


（一）案件进行开庭审理的，仲裁庭在确定第一次开庭日期后，应于开庭20天前将开庭日期通知当事人。当事人有正当理由的，可以请求延期开庭，但应在收到开庭通知后5天内提出延期申请。是否延期，由仲裁庭决定。

2. Where a party has justified reasons for being unable to submit a request for postponement in accordance with the preceding paragraph, the arbitral tribunal shall decide whether to accept the request.


（二）当事人有正当理由未能按前款规定提出延期开庭申请的，是否受理其延期申请，由仲裁庭决定。

3. The notification of the rescheduled or postponed hearing date and the request for postponement shall not be subject to the time limit restriction in paragraph (1) of this Article.


（三）重新开庭或延期开庭日期的通知以及延期申请，不受本条第一款期限的限制。

4. Where an oral hearing is held, the parties and their arbitration representatives have the right to attend the hearing. The attendance of other arbitration participants shall be decided by the arbitral tribunal. Unless the arbitral tribunal and the parties agree otherwise, persons other than the arbitration participants shall not attend.


（四）开庭审理时，当事人及其仲裁代理人有权出席庭审。其他仲裁参与人的出庭由仲裁庭决定。除非仲裁庭和当事人同意，仲裁参与人以外的人员不得出席。

5. After hearing the parties' views, the arbitral tribunal may, at its discretion, decide to hold oral hearings in person, remotely by video conference, or by other appropriate electronic communication means depending on the circumstances of the case.


（五）仲裁庭在听取当事人意见后，可根据仲裁案件情况，自行决定开庭审理以现场、远程视频及其他适当电子通讯方式的形式进行。

6. The Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission shall provide the necessary facilities and administrative support for oral hearings and remote video hearings.


（六）仲裁委员会仲裁院应提供开庭审理的必要设施及远程视频开庭审理的必要的行政支持。

### Article 38 — Confidentiality
### 第三十八条 — 保密

1. The hearings of the arbitral tribunal shall be held in camera. If the parties request a public hearing, the arbitral tribunal shall decide whether to hold the hearing in public.


（一）仲裁庭的审理不公开进行。当事人要求公开审理的，由仲裁庭决定是否公开审理。

2. For cases heard in camera, the parties, their arbitration representatives, arbitrators, witnesses, interpreters, experts consulted by the arbitral tribunal, appraisers appointed by the arbitral tribunal, and other related persons shall not disclose to any external party any information relating to the case and the proceedings.


（二）不公开审理的案件，当事人及其仲裁代理人、仲裁员、证人、翻译、仲裁庭咨询的专家和指定的鉴定人以及其他有关人员，均不得对外界透露案件实体和程序的信息。

### Article 39 — Default Hearing
### 第三十九条 — 缺席审理

1. If the claimant fails to appear at the hearing without justifiable reasons, or withdraws from the hearing without the permission of the arbitral tribunal, the claimant may be deemed to have withdrawn its application for arbitration. If the respondent has filed a counterclaim, the arbitral tribunal shall proceed with the hearing of the counterclaim and render an award.


（一）申请人无正当理由不到庭，或未经仲裁庭许可中途退庭的，可以视为撤回仲裁申请。被申请人提出反请求的，不影响仲裁庭就反请求进行审理并作出裁决。

2. If the respondent fails to appear at the hearing without justifiable reasons, or withdraws from the hearing without the permission of the arbitral tribunal, the arbitral tribunal may proceed with the hearing by default and render an award. If the respondent has filed a counterclaim, the respondent may be deemed to have withdrawn its counterclaim.


（二）被申请人无正当理由不到庭，或未经仲裁庭许可中途退庭的，仲裁庭可以进行缺席审理并作出裁决。被申请人提出反请求的，可以视为撤回反请求。

### Article 40 — Record of Oral Hearing
### 第四十条 — 开庭审理的记录

1. Where an oral hearing is held, the arbitral tribunal may make a record and/or audio-visual recording of the oral hearing. The arbitral tribunal may, if it deems necessary, produce a summary of the oral hearing and request the parties and/or their representatives, witnesses and/or other relevant persons to sign or seal the record or summary of the oral hearing.


（一）开庭审理时，仲裁庭可以制作庭审笔录及/或录音录像记录。仲裁庭认为必要时，可以制作庭审要点，并要求当事人及/或其代理人、证人及/或其他有关人员在庭审笔录或庭审要点上签名或盖章。

2. Where a party or other arbitration participant considers that there are omissions or errors in the record of its statements, it may apply for corrections. If the arbitral tribunal does not agree to the application for correction, the application shall be recorded in the record.


（二）当事人及其他仲裁参与人认为对自己陈述的记录有遗漏或差错的，可以申请更正。仲裁庭不同意更正的，应将申请记录在案。

3. The record, summary, and audio-visual recording of the oral hearing shall be made available for inspection by the arbitral tribunal.


（三）庭审笔录、庭审要点和录音录像记录供仲裁庭查阅。

4. Upon a party's application, the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission may, depending on the specific circumstances of the case, decide to engage a stenographer to record the hearing. The parties shall pay the costs incurred thereby in advance.


（四）经当事人申请，仲裁委员会仲裁院可根据案件具体情况决定聘请速录人员记录审理情况，当事人应预付由此产生的费用。

### Article 41 — Burden of Proof
### 第四十一条 — 举证

1. Each party shall bear the burden of proving the facts on which it relies to support its claim, defense, or counterclaim. The parties shall provide evidence for the factual assertions, arguments, and defenses they rely upon.


（一）当事人应对其申请、答辩和反请求所依据的事实承担举证责任并提供证据，对其主张、辩论及抗辩要点承担举证责任。

2. The arbitral tribunal may prescribe a time limit for the parties to produce evidence. The parties shall produce evidence within such time limit. The arbitral tribunal may refuse to accept evidence submitted after the time limit. Where a party has genuine difficulty in producing evidence within the time limit, it may apply for an extension before the expiry of the time limit. The decision to extend shall be made by the arbitral tribunal.


（二）仲裁庭可以规定当事人提交证据的期限。当事人应在规定的期限内提交证据。仲裁庭可以拒绝接受逾期提交的证据。当事人在举证期限内提交证据材料确有困难的，可以在举证期限届满前申请延长举证期限。是否延长，由仲裁庭决定。

3. If a party fails to produce evidence within the prescribed time limit, or if the evidence produced is insufficient to prove its claims, the party bearing the burden of proof shall bear the consequences.


（三）当事人未能在规定期限内提交证据，或提交的证据不足以证明其主张的，负有举证责任的当事人应承担相应后果。

4. Unless otherwise agreed by the parties, the arbitral tribunal may decide to apply or partially apply the CIETAC Evidence Guidelines in conducting the hearing. The CIETAC Evidence Guidelines do not form part of these Rules.


（四）除非当事人另有约定，仲裁庭可以决定适用或部分适用《中国国际经济贸易仲裁委员会证据指引》（以下简称"证据指引"）进行审理，但证据指引不构成本规则的组成部分。

### Article 42 — Cross-Examination
### 第四十二条 — 质证

1. Unless otherwise agreed by the parties, in cases where an oral hearing is held, the evidence shall be presented during the hearing, and the parties may cross-examine such evidence.


（一）除非当事人另有约定，开庭审理的案件，在开庭时提交的证据，当事人可以对其进行交叉盘问。

2. For cases heard on a documents-only basis or where evidence is submitted after the hearing and the parties have agreed to cross-examine in writing, cross-examination may be conducted in writing. The parties shall submit written cross-examination opinions within the time limit set by the arbitral tribunal.


（二）书面审理的案件或证据在开庭后提交且当事人同意以书面方式进行交叉盘问的，可以书面方式交叉盘问。当事人应在仲裁庭规定的期限内提交书面质证意见。

### Article 43 — Investigation and Evidence Collection by Arbitral Tribunal
### 第四十三条 — 仲裁庭的调查与取证

1. The arbitral tribunal may, if it deems it necessary, investigate facts and collect evidence.


（一）仲裁庭认为有必要时，可以调查事实，收集证据。

2. When investigating facts and collecting evidence, the arbitral tribunal may notify the parties to be present. If one or both parties fail to appear after being notified, the investigation and evidence collection by the arbitral tribunal shall not be affected.


（二）仲裁庭调查事实、收集证据时，可以通知当事人到场。经通知，一方或双方当事人不到场的，不影响仲裁庭调查事实和收集证据。

3. Evidence collected by the arbitral tribunal through investigation shall be transmitted to the parties, and the parties shall be given an opportunity to comment.


（三）仲裁庭经调查收集的证据，应转交当事人，并给予当事人发表意见的机会。

### Article 44 — Expert Reports and Appraisal Reports
### 第四十四条 — 专家报告及鉴定报告

1. The arbitral tribunal may consult experts or appoint appraisers on specialized issues of the case. Experts and appraisers may be institutions or individuals from China or abroad.


（一）仲裁庭可以就案件中的专门问题向专家咨询或指定鉴定人进行鉴定。专家和鉴定人可以是中国或外国的机构或自然人。

2. The arbitral tribunal has the right to require the parties, and the parties have the obligation, to provide or produce any relevant information, documents, property, or physical evidence to the experts or appraisers for their review, inspection, or appraisal.


（二）仲裁庭有权要求当事人、当事人也有义务向专家或鉴定人提供或出示任何有关资料、文件或财产、实物，以供专家或鉴定人审阅、检验或鉴定。

3. Copies of expert reports and appraisal reports shall be forwarded to the parties, giving them the opportunity to present their opinions. Where a party applies for or the arbitral tribunal requests the expert or appraiser to attend the hearing, the expert or appraiser shall attend the hearing and, if the arbitral tribunal considers it necessary, provide explanations regarding the report.


（三）专家报告和鉴定报告的副本应转交当事人，给予当事人提出意见的机会。一方当事人申请或仲裁庭要求专家或鉴定人参加开庭的，专家或鉴定人应参加开庭，并在仲裁庭认为必要时就所作出的报告进行解释。

### Article 45 — Suspension of Proceedings
### 第四十五条 — 程序中止

1. The arbitration proceedings may be suspended if the parties jointly or individually request suspension, or if other circumstances requiring suspension arise.


（一）当事人共同或个别请求中止仲裁程序，或出现其他需要中止程序的情形的，仲裁程序可以中止。

2. Once the reasons for suspension are eliminated or the suspension period expires, the arbitration proceedings shall resume.


（二）中止程序的原因消除或中止程序期限届满后，仲裁程序恢复。

3. The suspension and resumption of arbitration proceedings shall be decided by the arbitral tribunal. If the arbitral tribunal has not yet been constituted, the decision shall be made by the President of the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission.


（三）仲裁程序的中止及恢复，由仲裁庭决定；仲裁庭尚未组成的，由仲裁委员会仲裁院院长决定。

### Article 46 — Withdrawal of Claims and Dismissal of Case
### 第四十六条 — 撤回请求和撤销案件

1. A party may withdraw all of its arbitration claims or all of its counterclaims. If the claimant withdraws all of its arbitration claims, the arbitral tribunal may continue to hear the respondent's counterclaim and render an award. If the respondent withdraws all of its counterclaims, the arbitral tribunal may continue to hear the claimant's claims and render an award.


（一）当事人可以撤回全部仲裁请求或全部仲裁反请求。申请人撤回全部仲裁请求的，不影响仲裁庭就被申请人的反请求进行审理和裁决。被申请人撤回全部仲裁反请求的，不影响仲裁庭就申请人的请求进行审理和裁决。

2. Where it is impossible to continue the arbitration proceedings due to a party's circumstances or relevant legal provisions, the party may be deemed to have withdrawn its application.


（二）因当事人自身原因或相关法律规定导致仲裁程序无法继续进行的，当事人可视为撤回仲裁申请。

3. Where both the arbitration claims and counterclaims are withdrawn entirely, the case may be dismissed. If the case is dismissed before the constitution of the arbitral tribunal, the decision to dismiss shall be made by the President of the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission; if the case is dismissed after the constitution of the arbitral tribunal, the decision to dismiss shall be made by the arbitral tribunal.


（三）仲裁请求和反请求全部撤回的，可以撤销案件。仲裁庭组成前撤销案件的，由仲裁委员会仲裁院院长作出撤销案件决定；仲裁庭组成后撤销案件的，由仲裁庭作出撤销案件决定。

4. Decisions to dismiss the case under the preceding paragraph and Article 6(7) of these Rules shall be sealed with the seal of the China International Economic and Trade Arbitration Commission.


（四）前款及本规则第六条第（七）款的撤销案件决定，应加盖"中国国际经济贸易仲裁委员会"印章。

### Article 47 — Combination of Arbitration with Mediation
### 第四十七条 — 仲裁与调解相结合

1. If the parties wish to mediate, or if one party so wishes and the other party agrees after the arbitral tribunal's consultation, the arbitral tribunal may mediate during the arbitration proceedings. The parties may also reach a settlement by themselves.


（一）当事人希望调解的，或一方当事人希望调解且仲裁庭征得另一方当事人同意的，仲裁庭可以在仲裁程序中进行调解。当事人也可以自行和解。

2. With the consent of the parties, the arbitral tribunal may mediate in a manner it considers appropriate.


（二）经当事人同意，仲裁庭可以以其认为适当的方式进行调解。

3. During the mediation process, if any party proposes to terminate the mediation, or if the arbitral tribunal considers that mediation is no longer possible, the arbitral tribunal shall terminate the mediation.


（三）调解过程中，任何一方当事人提出终止调解，或仲裁庭认为调解已无成功可能的，仲裁庭应终止调解。

4. If the parties reach a settlement through mediation by the arbitral tribunal or by themselves, the parties shall enter into a settlement agreement.


（四）经仲裁庭调解达成和解或当事人自行和解的，双方当事人应签订和解协议。

5. Where the parties reach a settlement through mediation or by themselves, they may withdraw the arbitration claim or counterclaim, or request the arbitral tribunal to render an award or make a mediation statement in accordance with the terms of the settlement agreement.


（五）当事人经调解或自行达成和解的，可以撤回仲裁请求或反请求，也可以请求仲裁庭根据当事人和解协议的内容作出裁决书或制作调解书。

6. Where the parties request a mediation statement, the mediation statement shall set out the arbitration claims and the terms of the parties' written settlement agreement. The mediation statement shall be signed by the arbitrators, sealed with the seal of the China International Economic and Trade Arbitration Commission, and served on both parties.


（六）当事人要求制作调解书的，调解书应写明仲裁请求和当事人书面和解协议的内容，由仲裁员签名，并加盖"中国国际经济贸易仲裁委员会"印章，送达双方当事人。

7. If mediation fails, the arbitral tribunal shall continue the arbitration proceedings and render an award.


（七）调解不成的，仲裁庭应继续进行仲裁程序并作出裁决。

8. Where the parties wish to mediate but do not wish the mediation to be conducted by the arbitral tribunal, the Arbitration Commission may, with the consent of the parties, assist the parties in conducting mediation in an appropriate manner and procedure.


（八）当事人希望调解但不愿由仲裁庭主持调解的，仲裁委员会在征得当事人同意后，可以协助当事人以适当的方式和程序进行调解。

9. If mediation fails, neither party may rely on any opinions, views, statements, or proposals made or admitted by the other party or the arbitral tribunal during the mediation as grounds for any claim, defense, or counterclaim in subsequent arbitration, judicial, or other proceedings.


（九）调解不成的，任何一方当事人均不得在之后的仲裁程序、司法程序和其他任何程序中援引对方当事人或仲裁庭在调解过程中曾发表的意见、提出的观点、作出的陈述、表示认同或否定的建议或主张作为其请求、答辩或反请求的依据。

10. Where the parties reach a settlement agreement before the commencement of arbitration, whether by themselves or through mediation, the parties may, on the basis of the arbitration agreement providing for arbitration by the Arbitration Commission and the settlement agreement, request the Arbitration Commission to constitute an arbitral tribunal to render an award in accordance with the terms of the settlement agreement. Unless otherwise agreed by the parties, the President of the Arbitration Commission shall appoint a sole arbitrator to constitute the arbitral tribunal. The arbitral tribunal shall conduct the hearing in a manner it considers appropriate and render an award. The specific procedural arrangements and time limits shall not be subject to the procedural arrangements and time limits set out elsewhere in these Rules.


（十）当事人在仲裁程序开始前自行或通过调解达成和解协议的，可以依据仲裁委员会仲裁的仲裁协议及其和解协议，请求仲裁委员会组成仲裁庭，按照和解协议的内容作出裁决书。除非当事人另有约定，仲裁委员会主任指定一名独任仲裁员组成仲裁庭，仲裁庭按照其认为适当的程序进行审理并作出裁决。具体程序和期限不受本规则其他条款规定程序和期限的限制。

### Article 48 — Third-Party Funding
### 第四十八条 — 第三方资助

1. A party receiving third-party funding shall, without delay after concluding the funding agreement, submit to the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission information on the fact of the third-party funding, the economic interest of the third party, and the name and address of the third party. The Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission shall transmit such information to the relevant parties and the arbitral tribunal. The arbitral tribunal may, if it deems necessary, require the party receiving third-party funding to disclose relevant information.


（一）接受第三方资助的当事人应在与资助方签订资助协议后，毫不延迟地将第三方资助的事实、经济利益及第三方的名称和住所等信息提交仲裁委员会仲裁院。仲裁委员会仲裁院应将上述信息转交有关当事人和仲裁庭。仲裁庭认为必要时，可以要求接受第三方资助的当事人披露相关信息。

2. The arbitral tribunal may, when deciding on arbitration costs and other related expenses, take into consideration the existence of third-party funding and whether the party has complied with the preceding paragraph.


（二）仲裁庭在就仲裁费用和其他相关费用作出决定时，可以考虑是否存在第三方资助以及当事人是否遵守了前款规定。

### Article 49 — Interim Decisions
### 第四十九条 — 中间决定

1. Where the arbitral tribunal considers it necessary, or where a party so requests and the arbitral tribunal agrees, the arbitral tribunal may make an interim decision on any issue of the case before rendering the final award.


（一）仲裁庭认为必要时，或经当事人请求且仲裁庭同意的，仲裁庭可以在作出最终裁决之前，就案件的任何问题作出中间决定。

2. The failure of any party to comply with an interim decision shall not affect the continuation of the arbitration proceedings or the rendering of the final award by the arbitral tribunal.


（二）任何一方当事人不遵守中间决定的，不影响仲裁程序的继续进行或仲裁庭作出最终裁决。

### Article 50 — Early Dismissal Procedure
### 第五十条 — 早期驳回程序

1. A party may apply for early dismissal of all or part of an arbitration claim or counterclaim on the grounds that the claim or counterclaim is manifestly without legal merit or manifestly outside the jurisdiction of the arbitral tribunal (hereinafter referred to as "early dismissal application").


（一）当事人可以以仲裁请求或反请求明显缺乏法律依据或明显超出仲裁庭管辖范围为由，申请早期驳回全部或部分仲裁请求或反请求（以下简称"早期驳回申请"）。

2. A party shall submit an early dismissal application in writing, setting out the factual and legal grounds. The arbitral tribunal may, to prevent the party from abusing the early dismissal application to delay the arbitration proceedings, require the party making the application to provide valid reasons and demonstrate that the early dismissal will expedite the overall arbitration proceedings. The early dismissal application by a party shall not prevent the arbitral tribunal from continuing the arbitration proceedings.


（二）当事人应以书面方式提出早期驳回申请，并说明事实和法律依据。仲裁庭为防止当事人滥用早期驳回程序申请拖延仲裁程序，可要求提出申请的当事人提出正当理由，并证明早期驳回程序将整体加快仲裁程序。当事人提出早期驳回申请不影响仲裁庭继续进行仲裁程序。

3. A party shall file the early dismissal application as early as possible. Unless the arbitral tribunal decides otherwise, the application shall be filed no later than the submission of the defense statement or the reply to the counterclaim.


（三）当事人应尽早提出早期驳回申请。除非仲裁庭另有决定，最迟应在提交答辩书或反请求答辩书时提出。

4. After hearing the views of the parties, the arbitral tribunal may decide whether to accept the early dismissal application.


（四）仲裁庭在听取当事人意见后，可以决定是否受理早期驳回申请。

5. The arbitral tribunal shall make a decision or award on the early dismissal application within 60 days from the date the application is filed, with reasons stated. The President of the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission may, upon the request of the arbitral tribunal, properly extend the time limit if he or she considers it justified and necessary.


（五）仲裁庭应在早期驳回申请提出之日起60天内就该申请作出决定或裁决，并说明理由。仲裁委员会仲裁院院长经仲裁庭请求，认为正当且必要的，可以适当延长该期限。

6. Where the arbitral tribunal decides to uphold or partially uphold the early dismissal application, it shall not prevent the arbitral tribunal from continuing the hearing on other arbitration claims and counterclaims.


（六）仲裁庭作出支持或部分支持早期驳回申请的决定的，不影响仲裁庭就其他仲裁请求及反请求继续进行审理。

### Chapter III — Award
### 第三章 — 裁决

### Article 51 — Time Limit for Award
### 第五十一条 — 作出裁决的期限

1. The arbitral tribunal shall render an award within 6 months from the date on which the arbitral tribunal is constituted.


（一）仲裁庭应在仲裁庭组成后6个月内作出裁决。

2. The President of the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission may, upon the request of the arbitral tribunal, extend the time limit if he or she considers it justified and necessary.


（二）仲裁委员会仲裁院院长经仲裁庭请求，认为正当且必要的，可以延长该期限。

3. The period during which the proceedings are suspended shall not be counted in the time limit for rendering the award specified in paragraph (1) of this Article.


（三）中止程序的期间不计入本条第一款规定的裁决期限。

### Article 52 — Award
### 第五十二条 — 裁决

1. The arbitral tribunal shall render the award independently and impartially, based on the facts and the contractual agreement, in accordance with the provisions of law and with reference to international practices, in a fair and reasonable manner.


（一）仲裁庭应根据事实和合同约定，依照法律规定，参考国际惯例，公平合理、独立公正地作出裁决。

2. Where the parties have agreed on the law applicable to the merits of the dispute, the arbitral tribunal shall apply that law. Where the parties have not agreed, or their agreement conflicts with mandatory provisions of law, the arbitral tribunal shall determine the law or rules of law applicable to the merits.


（二）当事人对案件适用法律有约定的，从其约定。当事人未约定或其约定与法律强制性规定相抵触的，由仲裁庭决定案件应适用的法律或法律规则。

3. The arbitral tribunal shall state in the award the claims, the facts in dispute, the reasons for the award, the result of the award, the allocation of arbitration costs, and the date and place of the award. Where the parties agree not to state the facts in dispute and the reasons for the award, or where the award is made in accordance with the terms of the settlement agreement between the parties, the facts in dispute and the reasons for the award may be omitted. The arbitral tribunal has the power to specify in the award the specific time limit for the parties to perform the award and the liability for delayed performance.


（三）仲裁庭应在裁决书中写明仲裁请求、争议事实、裁决理由、裁决结果、仲裁费用的承担、裁决的日期和地点。当事人约定不写明争议事实和裁决理由，或裁决按照当事人和解协议内容作出的，可以不写明争议事实和裁决理由。仲裁庭有权在裁决书中确定当事人履行裁决的具体期限及逾期履行的责任。

4. The award shall be sealed with the seal of the China International Economic and Trade Arbitration Commission.


（四）裁决书应加盖"中国国际经济贸易仲裁委员会"印章。

5. For a case heard by a tribunal consisting of three arbitrators, the award shall be made by all or a majority of the arbitrators. The written opinion of a dissenting arbitrator may be attached to the file but shall not form part of the award.


（五）由三名仲裁员组成的仲裁庭审理的案件，裁决依全体或多数仲裁员的意见作出。少数仲裁员的书面意见可以附卷，但可以附入裁决书，不构成裁决书的组成部分。

6. Where the arbitral tribunal cannot reach a majority opinion, the award shall be made in accordance with the opinion of the presiding arbitrator. The written opinions of other arbitrators may be attached to the file but shall not form part of the award.


（六）仲裁庭不能形成多数意见的，裁决依首席仲裁员的意见作出。其他仲裁员的书面意见可以附卷，但可以附入裁决书，不构成裁决书的组成部分。

7. Except where the award is made in accordance with the opinion of the presiding arbitrator or the sole arbitrator and signed by that person, the award shall be signed by a majority of the arbitrators. An arbitrator who disagrees may decline to sign the award. The electronic signature of an arbitrator shall have the same effect as a handwritten signature.


（七）裁决书应由多数仲裁员签名，但裁决依首席仲裁员或独任仲裁员意见作出并由该仲裁员签名的除外。持不同意见的仲裁员可以在裁决书上不签名。仲裁员的电子签名与手写签名具有同等效力。

8. The date on which the award is made shall be the date on which the award becomes legally effective.


（八）作出裁决的日期即为裁决发生法律效力的日期。

9. The award is final and binding on the parties.


（九）裁决是终局的，对当事人具有约束力。

10. The award to be served on the parties shall be in paper form. Where the parties have agreed, or where the Arbitration Commission considers it necessary, the award may be served in electronic form.


（十）送达当事人的裁决书应为纸质形式。当事人约定或仲裁委员会认为必要的，裁决书可以电子形式送达。

### Article 53 — Interim Award
### 第五十三条 — 部分裁决

1. Where the arbitral tribunal considers it necessary, or where a party so requests and the arbitral tribunal agrees, the arbitral tribunal may render an interim award on any issue of the case before rendering the final award. An interim award is final and binding on the parties.


（一）仲裁庭认为必要，或当事人提出请求并经仲裁庭同意的，仲裁庭可以在作出最终裁决前，就当事人主张的部分事项作出部分裁决。部分裁决是终局的，对当事人具有约束力。

2. A party's failure to comply with an interim award shall not prevent the continuation of the arbitration proceedings or the rendering of the final award by the arbitral tribunal.


（二）当事人对部分裁决不履行的，不影响仲裁程序的继续进行，也不影响仲裁庭作出最终裁决。

### Article 54 — Review of Draft Award
### 第五十四条 — 裁决书草案的核阅

The arbitral tribunal shall submit the draft award to the Arbitration Commission for review before signing the award. Without prejudice to the independence of the arbitral tribunal, the Arbitration Commission may draw the arbitral tribunal's attention to issues concerning the award.


仲裁庭应在签署裁决书前将裁决草案提交仲裁委员会核阅。在不影响仲裁庭独立性的情况下，仲裁委员会可以就裁决书的相关问题提请仲裁庭注意。

### Article 55 — Costs
### 第五十五条 — 应承担的费用

1. The arbitral tribunal has the power to determine in the award the arbitration costs and other expenses to be finally paid by the parties to the Arbitration Commission.


（一）仲裁庭有权在裁决书中确定当事人最终应向仲裁委员会支付的仲裁费用和其他费用。

2. The arbitral tribunal has the power to decide in the award that the losing party shall compensate the winning party for the reasonable costs incurred in conducting the case, taking into account the specific circumstances of the case. In determining whether it is reasonable for the losing party to compensate the winning party for the costs incurred in conducting the case, the arbitral tribunal shall specifically consider the outcome of the award, the complexity of the case, the actual workload of the winning party and/or its representatives, and the amount in dispute.


（二）仲裁庭有权在裁决书中裁决败诉方应当补偿胜诉方因办理案件而支出的合理费用。在确定败诉方补偿胜诉方因办理案件而支出的费用是否合理时，仲裁庭应具体考虑案件的裁决结果、复杂程度、胜诉方当事人及/或代理人的实际工作量以及案件的争议金额。

### Article 56 — Correction of Award
### 第五十六条 — 裁决书的更正

1. The arbitral tribunal may, on its own initiative, within a reasonable time after the award is made, correct in writing any clerical, typographical, computational errors or any errors or omissions of a similar nature in the award.


（一）仲裁庭可以在作出裁决后合理期限内自行以书面形式对裁决书中的笔误、打字错误、计算错误或类似性质的错误或遗漏进行更正。

2. Any party may, within 30 days after receiving the award, apply to the arbitral tribunal in writing for correction of any clerical, typographical, computational errors or any errors or omissions of a similar nature in the award. If such errors exist, the arbitral tribunal shall make a written correction within 30 days after receiving the application.


（二）任何一方当事人可以在收到裁决书后30天内向仲裁庭书面申请更正裁决书中的笔误、打字错误、计算错误或类似性质的错误或遗漏。如确有错误，仲裁庭应在收到申请后30天内作出书面更正。

3. Any written correction shall form an integral part of the award and shall be subject to the provisions of Article 52(4) through (10) of these Rules.


（三）任何书面更正构成裁决书的不可分割部分，适用本规则第五十二条第（四）至（十）项的规定。

### Article 57 — Additional Award
### 第五十七条 — 补充裁决

1. Where the award has omitted any claim or counterclaim, the arbitral tribunal may, on its own initiative, within a reasonable time after the award is made, make an additional award.


（一）仲裁庭对仲裁请求或反请求有遗漏的，可以在作出裁决后合理期限内自行作出补充裁决。

2. Any party may, within 30 days after receiving the award, apply to the arbitral tribunal in writing for an additional award on any claim or counterclaim omitted from the award. If such omission exists, the arbitral tribunal shall make an additional award within 30 days after receiving the application.


（二）任何一方当事人可以在收到裁决书后30天内向仲裁庭书面申请就裁决书中遗漏的事项作出补充裁决。如确有遗漏，仲裁庭应在收到书面申请后30天内作出补充裁决。

3. Any additional award shall form part of the award and shall be subject to the provisions of Article 52(4) through (10) of these Rules.


（三）补充裁决构成裁决书的一部分，适用本规则第五十二条第（四）至（十）项的规定。

### Article 58 — Performance of Award
### 第五十八条 — 裁决的履行

1. The parties shall perform the award within the time limit specified in the award. If no time limit is specified in the award, the parties shall perform the award immediately.


（一）当事人应在裁决书规定的期限内履行裁决。裁决书未规定履行期限的，当事人应立即履行。

2. If one party fails to perform the award, the other party may apply to a competent court for enforcement in accordance with the law.


（二）一方当事人不履行裁决的，另一方当事人可以依照法律规定向有管辖权的法院申请执行。

### Chapter IV — Summary Procedure
### 第四章 — 简易程序

### Article 59 — Application of Summary Procedure
### 第五十九条 — 简易程序的适用

1. Unless otherwise agreed by the parties, the Summary Procedure shall apply where the amount in dispute does not exceed RMB 5,000,000, or where the amount in dispute exceeds RMB 5,000,000 but one party applies in writing and the other party agrees in writing, or where the parties agree to apply the Summary Procedure.


（一）除非当事人另有约定，凡争议金额不超过人民币500万元，或争议金额超过人民币500万元但一方当事人书面申请且另一方当事人书面同意，或当事人约定适用简易程序的，适用简易程序。

2. Where the amount in dispute is not specified or is unclear, the Arbitration Commission shall determine whether to apply the Summary Procedure based on a comprehensive consideration of the complexity of the case, the size of the interests involved, and other relevant factors.


（二）没有争议金额或争议金额不明确的，由仲裁委员会综合考虑案件的复杂程度、涉及利益的大小以及其他有关因素决定是否适用简易程序。

### Article 60 — Acceptance of Case
### 第六十条 — 案件的受理

After receiving the applicant's Application for Arbitration and its attachments, the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission shall, upon examination, if it finds that the conditions for acceptance set out in Article 12 are satisfied and determines that the Summary Procedure applies, issue an arbitration notice to both parties.


仲裁委员会仲裁院收到申请人的仲裁申请书及其附件后，经审查，符合本规则第十二条规定的受理条件且适用简易程序的，应向双方当事人发出仲裁通知。

### Article 61 — Constitution of Arbitral Tribunal
### 第六十一条 — 仲裁庭的组成

Unless otherwise agreed by the parties, cases to which the Summary Procedure applies shall be heard by a sole arbitral tribunal constituted in accordance with Article 28 of these Rules.


除非当事人另有约定，适用简易程序的案件，依照本规则第二十八条的规定设立独任仲裁庭进行审理。

### Article 62 — Defense and Counterclaim
### 第六十二条 — 答辩和反请求

1. The respondent shall submit its defense statement, evidence, and other supporting documents within 20 days after receiving the arbitration notice. If there is a counterclaim, the counterclaim statement, evidence, and other supporting documents shall also be submitted within this period.


（一）被申请人应在收到仲裁通知后20天内提交答辩书、证据材料及其他证明文件；如有反请求，也应在此期限内提交反请求申请书、证据材料及其他证明文件。

2. The claimant shall submit its defense to the counterclaim within 20 days after receiving the notice of acceptance of the counterclaim.


（二）申请人应在收到反请求受理通知后20天内对被申请人的反请求提交答辩书。

3. Where a party has justified reasons for requesting an extension of the above time limits, the arbitral tribunal shall decide whether to extend; if the arbitral tribunal has not yet been constituted, the President of the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission shall decide.


（三）当事人有正当理由请求延长上述期限的，由仲裁庭决定是否延长；仲裁庭尚未组成的，由仲裁委员会仲裁院院长决定。

### Article 63 — Method of Hearing
### 第六十三条 — 审理方式

The arbitral tribunal may conduct the hearing in a manner it considers appropriate. After hearing the views of the parties, the arbitral tribunal may decide to conduct a documents-only hearing based on the written materials and evidence submitted by the parties, or to hold an oral hearing.


仲裁庭可以以其认为适当的方式审理案件。在听取当事人意见后，仲裁庭可以决定只依据当事人提交的书面材料和证据进行书面审理，也可以决定开庭审理。

### Article 64 — Oral Hearing
### 第六十四条 — 开庭审理

1. Where the case is heard by oral hearing, the arbitral tribunal shall, after fixing the date of the first oral hearing, notify the parties of the hearing date at least 15 days before the hearing. A party may request a postponement of the hearing if it has justified reasons, provided that the request for postponement is submitted in writing within 3 days after receiving the hearing notice. The arbitral tribunal shall decide whether to postpone.


（一）开庭审理的案件，仲裁庭在确定第一次开庭日期后，应于开庭15天前将开庭日期通知当事人。当事人有正当理由的，可以请求延期开庭，但应在收到开庭通知后3天内以书面方式提出延期申请。仲裁庭应决定是否延期。

2. Where a party has justified reasons for being unable to submit a request for postponement in accordance with the time limit provided in the preceding paragraph, the arbitral tribunal shall decide whether to accept the request for postponement.


（二）当事人有正当理由未能按前款规定期限提出延期开庭申请的，仲裁庭应决定是否受理其延期申请。

3. The notification of the rescheduled or postponed hearing date and the request for postponement shall not be subject to the time limit restriction in paragraph (1) of this Article.


（三）重新开庭或延期开庭日期的通知以及延期申请，不受本条第一款期限的限制。

### Article 65 — Time Limit for Award
### 第六十五条 — 作出裁决的期限

1. The arbitral tribunal shall render an award within 3 months from the date on which the arbitral tribunal is constituted.


（一）仲裁庭应在仲裁庭组成后3个月内作出裁决。

2. The President of the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission may, upon the request of the arbitral tribunal, extend the time limit if he or she considers it justified and necessary.


（二）仲裁委员会仲裁院院长经仲裁庭请求，认为正当且必要的，可以延长该期限。

3. The period during which the proceedings are suspended shall not be counted in the time limit for rendering the award specified in paragraph (1) of this Article.


（三）中止程序的期间不计入本条第一款规定的裁决期限。

### Article 66 — Change of Procedure
### 第六十六条 — 程序变更

The amendment of arbitration claims or the submission of counterclaims shall not prevent the continuation of the Summary Procedure. Where the amount in dispute arising from the amended claims or counterclaims exceeds RMB 5,000,000, the Summary Procedure shall continue to apply unless the parties agree or the arbitral tribunal considers it necessary to change to the ordinary procedure.


仲裁请求的变更或反请求的提出，不影响简易程序的继续进行。经变更的仲裁请求或反请求所涉争议金额超过人民币500万元的，除当事人约定或仲裁庭认为有必要变更为普通程序外，继续适用简易程序。

### Article 67 — Application of Other Provisions
### 第六十七条 — 其他规定的适用

Matters not provided for in this Chapter shall be governed by the relevant provisions of other Chapters of these Rules.


本章未规定的事项，适用本规则其他各章的有关规定。

### Chapter V — Special Provisions for Domestic Arbitration
### 第五章 — 国内仲裁的特别规定

### Article 68 — Application of This Chapter
### 第六十八条 — 本章的适用

1. This Chapter shall apply to domestic arbitration cases.


（一）国内仲裁案件，适用本章规定。

2. For domestic arbitration cases falling within the scope of Article 59 of these Rules, the Summary Procedure in Chapter IV shall apply.


（二）符合本规则第五十九条规定的国内仲裁案件，适用第四章简易程序的规定。

### Article 69 — Acceptance of Case
### 第六十九条 — 案件的受理

After receiving the Application for Arbitration and its attachments, the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission shall, upon examination, if it finds that the conditions for acceptance set out in Article 12 are satisfied and determines that the domestic arbitration procedure applies, issue an arbitration notice to the parties.


仲裁委员会仲裁院收到申请人的仲裁申请书及其附件后，经审查，符合本规则第十二条规定的受理条件且适用国内仲裁程序的，应向当事人发出仲裁通知。

### Article 70 — Constitution of Arbitral Tribunal
### 第七十条 — 仲裁庭的组成

The arbitral tribunal shall be constituted in accordance with Articles 25, 26, 27, 28, 29, and 30 of these Rules.


仲裁庭应按照本规则第二十五条、第二十六条、第二十七条、第二十八条、第二十九条和第三十条的规定组成。

### Article 71 — Defense and Counterclaim
### 第七十一条 — 答辩和反请求

1. The respondent shall submit its defense statement, evidence, and other supporting documents within 20 days after receiving the arbitration notice. If there is a counterclaim, the counterclaim statement, evidence, and other supporting documents shall also be submitted within this period.


（一）被申请人应在收到仲裁通知后20天内提交答辩书及其所依据的证据材料和其他证明文件；如有反请求，也应在此期限内提交反请求申请书及其所依据的证据材料和其他证明文件。

2. The claimant shall submit its defense to the counterclaim within 20 days after receiving the notice of acceptance of the counterclaim.


（二）申请人应在收到反请求申请书受理通知后20天内提交答辩书。

3. Where a party has justified reasons for requesting an extension of the above time limits, the arbitral tribunal shall decide whether to extend; if the arbitral tribunal has not yet been constituted, the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission shall decide.


（三）当事人有正当理由请求延长上述期限的，由仲裁庭决定是否延长；仲裁庭尚未组成的，由仲裁委员会仲裁院决定。

### Article 72 — Oral Hearing
### 第七十二条 — 开庭审理

1. Where an oral hearing is to be held, the arbitral tribunal shall, after fixing the date of the first oral hearing, notify the parties of the hearing date at least 15 days before the hearing. A party may request a postponement of the hearing based on justified reasons, provided that the request for postponement is submitted in writing within 3 days after receiving the hearing notice. The arbitral tribunal shall decide whether to postpone.


（一）开庭审理的案件，仲裁庭在确定第一次开庭日期后，应于开庭15天前将开庭日期通知当事人。当事人有正当理由的，可以请求延期开庭，但应在收到开庭通知后3天内以书面方式提出延期申请。仲裁庭应决定是否延期。

2. Where a party has justified reasons for being unable to submit a request for postponement in accordance with the preceding paragraph, the arbitral tribunal shall decide whether to accept the request for postponement.


（二）当事人有正当理由未能按前款规定提出延期开庭申请的，仲裁庭应决定是否受理其延期申请。

3. The notification of the rescheduled or postponed hearing date and the request for postponement shall not be subject to the time limit restriction in paragraph (1) of this Article.


（三）重新开庭或延期开庭日期的通知以及延期申请，不受本条第一款期限的限制。

### Article 73 — Record of Oral Hearing
### 第七十三条 — 开庭审理记录

1. The arbitral tribunal shall record the hearing and make a written record. Where a party or other arbitration participant considers that there are omissions or errors in the record of its own statements, it may apply for corrections. If the arbitral tribunal does not agree to the correction, the application shall be recorded in the record.


（一）仲裁庭应对开庭情况予以记录，制作笔录。当事人及其他仲裁参与人认为对自己陈述的记录有遗漏或差错的，可以申请更正。仲裁庭不同意更正的，应将申请记录在案。

2. The arbitrators, recorder, parties, and other arbitration participants shall sign or seal the written record.


（二）仲裁员、记录员、当事人及其他仲裁参与人应在笔录上签名或盖章。

### Article 74 — Time Limit for Award
### 第七十四条 — 作出裁决的期限

1. The arbitral tribunal shall render an award within 4 months from the date on which the arbitral tribunal is constituted.


（一）仲裁庭应在仲裁庭组成后4个月内作出裁决。

2. The President of the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission may, upon the request of the arbitral tribunal, extend the time limit if he or she considers it justified and necessary.


（二）仲裁委员会仲裁院院长经仲裁庭请求，认为正当且必要的，可以延长该期限。

3. The period during which the proceedings are suspended shall not be counted in the time limit for rendering the award specified in paragraph (1) of this Article.


（三）中止程序的期间不计入本条第一款规定的裁决期限。

### Article 75 — Application of Other Provisions
### 第七十五条 — 其他条款的适用

Matters not provided for in this Chapter shall be governed by the relevant provisions of other Chapters of these Rules. The provisions of Chapter VI of these Rules shall not apply.


本章未规定的事项，适用本规则其他各章的有关规定。本规则第六章的规定不适用于本章。

### Chapter VI — Special Provisions for Arbitration in Hong Kong
### 第六章 — 香港仲裁的特别规定

### Article 76 — Application of This Chapter
### 第七十六条 — 本章的适用

1. The Arbitration Commission has established the CIETAC Hong Kong Arbitration Center in the Hong Kong Special Administrative Region. This Chapter shall apply to arbitration cases accepted and administered by the CIETAC Hong Kong Arbitration Center.


（一）仲裁委员会在香港特别行政区设立仲裁委员会香港仲裁中心。本章适用于仲裁委员会香港仲裁中心受理并管理的仲裁案件。

2. Where the parties have agreed to submit disputes to arbitration by the CIETAC Hong Kong Arbitration Center, or have agreed to submit disputes to arbitration by the Arbitration Commission in Hong Kong, the CIETAC Hong Kong Arbitration Center shall accept the arbitration application and administer the case.


（二）当事人约定将争议提交仲裁委员会香港仲裁中心仲裁，或约定将争议提交仲裁委员会在香港仲裁的，由仲裁委员会香港仲裁中心受理仲裁申请并管理案件。

### Article 77 — Place of Arbitration and Applicable Procedural Law
### 第七十七条 — 仲裁地和适用程序法

Unless otherwise agreed by the parties, for cases administered by the CIETAC Hong Kong Arbitration Center, the place of arbitration shall be Hong Kong, the law applicable to the arbitration proceedings shall be the Hong Kong Arbitration Ordinance, and the award shall be a Hong Kong award.


除非当事人另有约定，仲裁委员会香港仲裁中心管理的案件的仲裁地为香港，仲裁程序适用法律为香港仲裁法，裁决为香港裁决。

### Article 78 — Determination of Jurisdiction
### 第七十八条 — 管辖权决定的作出

A party's objection to the arbitration agreement and/or jurisdiction shall be raised no later than the first substantive defense submission. The arbitral tribunal has the power to decide on the existence, validity, and jurisdiction of the arbitration agreement.


当事人对仲裁协议及/或仲裁案件管辖权的异议，应不晚于第一次实体答辩前提出。仲裁庭有权对仲裁协议的存在、效力以及仲裁案件的管辖权作出决定。

### Article 79 — Appointment or Designation of Arbitrators
### 第七十九条 — 仲裁员的选定或指定

The current panel of arbitrators of the Arbitration Commission is recommended for use in cases administered by the CIETAC Hong Kong Arbitration Center. However, the parties may appoint arbitrators from outside the panel. The appointed arbitrator shall be confirmed by the President of the Arbitration Commission.


现行仲裁委员会仲裁员名册推荐用于仲裁委员会香港仲裁中心管理的案件，但当事人可以在仲裁员名册之外选定仲裁员。被选定的仲裁员由仲裁委员会主任确认。

### Article 80 — Interim Measures and Emergency Relief
### 第八十条 — 临时措施与紧急救济

1. Unless otherwise agreed by the parties, the arbitral tribunal has the power to order appropriate interim measures upon the application of a party.


（一）除非当事人另有约定，仲裁庭有权经一方当事人申请决定适当的临时措施。

2. Before the constitution of the arbitral tribunal, a party may apply for emergency interim relief in accordance with the CIETAC Emergency Arbitrator Procedures (Appendix III to these Rules).


（二）仲裁庭组成前，当事人可依照《中国国际经济贸易仲裁委员会紧急仲裁员程序》（本规则附件三）申请紧急临时救济。

### Article 81 — Seal of Award
### 第八十一条 — 裁决书的印章

The award shall be sealed with the stamp of \"CIETAC Hong Kong Arbitration Center.\"


裁决书应加盖"中国国际经济贸易仲裁委员会香港仲裁中心"印章。

### Article 82 — Arbitration Fees
### 第八十二条 — 仲裁收费

Cases accepted and administered under this Chapter shall be subject to the CIETAC Arbitration Fee Schedule (III) (Appendix II to these Rules).


依本章接受申请并管理的案件适用《中国国际经济贸易仲裁委员会仲裁费用表（三）》（本规则附件二）。

### Article 83 — Application of Other Provisions of These Rules
### 第八十三条 — 本规则其他条款的适用

Matters not provided for in this Chapter shall be governed by the relevant provisions of other Chapters of these Rules. The provisions of Chapter V of these Rules shall not apply.


本章未规定的事项，适用本规则其他各章的有关规定。本规则第五章的规定不适用于本章。

### Chapter VII — Supplementary Provisions
### 第七章 — 附则

### Article 84 — Language of Arbitration
### 第八十四条 — 仲裁语言

1. Where the parties have agreed on the language of arbitration, their agreement shall prevail.


（一）当事人对仲裁语言有约定的，从其约定。

2. Where the parties have not agreed on the language of arbitration, Chinese shall be the language of arbitration. The Arbitration Commission may, after fully considering the language of the contract and other circumstances, decide to conduct the arbitration in one or more languages. After the constitution of the arbitral tribunal, the arbitral tribunal may, based on the specific circumstances of the case, reselect the language of arbitration to be used in the proceedings.


（二）当事人对仲裁语言没有约定的，以中文为仲裁语言。仲裁委员会在全面考虑合同语言等情况后，可以决定以一种或多种语言进行仲裁。仲裁庭组成后，仲裁庭可以根据案件具体情况重新选择仲裁程序中使用的一种或多种仲裁语言。

3. If the arbitral tribunal holds an oral hearing and the parties, their representatives, or witnesses require interpretation, the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission may provide interpretation, or the parties may provide their own interpreter.


（三）仲裁庭开庭审理时，当事人或其代理人、证人需要翻译的，仲裁委员会仲裁院可以提供译员，也可以由当事人自行提供。

4. Where the arbitral tribunal or the Arbitration Court of the Arbitration Commission deems it necessary, the parties may be required to submit Chinese or other language translations of various documents and evidentiary materials they have submitted.


（四）仲裁庭或仲裁委员会仲裁院认为必要时，可以要求当事人提交其提交的各种文书和证明材料的汉语或其他语言的译本。

### Article 85 — Arbitration Fees and Expenses
### 第八十五条 — 仲裁费用及开支

1. The Arbitration Commission shall charge arbitration fees in accordance with the prescribed fee schedule. In addition, the Arbitration Commission may charge other additional and reasonable expenses, including special remuneration for arbitrators, travel, meals, and accommodation expenses, fees for engaging stenographers to record cases, and expenses for engaging experts, appraisers, and interpreters.


（一）仲裁委员会按照规定的仲裁费用表向当事人收取仲裁费用，并可以因办理案件需要向当事人收取仲裁员的特殊报酬、旅差费、食宿费、聘请速录员记录案件的费用，以及仲裁庭聘请专家、鉴定人和翻译等费用，以及因其他额外且合理的情形产生的费用。

2. If a party fails to pay in advance the special remuneration, travel, meals, and accommodation expenses of the arbitrator it has appointed within the time limit prescribed by the Arbitration Commission, the party shall be deemed not to have appointed that arbitrator.


（二）当事人未在仲裁委员会规定的期限内预交其选定的仲裁员的特殊报酬、旅差费、食宿费的，视为当事人未选定该仲裁员。

3. Where the parties have agreed to hold the hearing at a place other than the location of the Arbitration Commission or its sub-committee/arbitration center, the parties shall pay in advance the travel, meals, and accommodation expenses and the expenses incurred as a result of such hearing. If the parties fail to pay such expenses in advance within the time limit prescribed by the Arbitration Commission, the oral hearing shall be held at the location of the Arbitration Commission or its sub-committee/arbitration center.


（三）当事人约定在仲裁委员会或其分会/仲裁中心所在地以外开庭的，当事人应预缴旅差费、食宿费以及因此产生的审理费用。当事人未在仲裁委员会规定的期限内预缴该费用的，开庭审理在仲裁委员会或其分会/仲裁中心所在地进行。

4. Where the parties agree to adopt two or more languages as the language of arbitration, or agree to have a case heard by a three-member tribunal where the Summary Procedure under Article 59 would otherwise apply, the Arbitration Commission may charge the parties additional reasonable expenses.


（四）当事人约定以两种以上语言为仲裁语言，或约定由三名仲裁员组成仲裁庭审理本规则第五十九条规定适用简易程序的案件时，仲裁委员会可以向当事人收取额外合理的费用。

5. Where the Arbitration Commission provides ad hoc arbitration services under Article 2(7) of these Rules, the arbitral tribunal shall, upon the request of the parties and depending on the circumstances of the case, decide on the relevant arbitration fees after consultation with the parties, and notify the parties to pay within a specified period. If the parties fail to pay or fail to pay in full, the Arbitration Commission may suspend the provision of ad hoc arbitration services in whole or in part, and may deem the request withdrawn.


（五）仲裁委员会根据本规则第二条第（七）项规定向当事人提供临时仲裁服务时，仲裁庭可根据当事人请求、案件情况与当事人协商后决定相关仲裁费用，通知当事人在指定期限内缴纳。当事人不缴纳或未全部缴纳的，仲裁委员会可以中止提供全部或部分临时仲裁服务，也可以视为该请求已被撤回。

### Article 86 — Limitation of Liability
### 第八十六条 — 责任限制

The Arbitration Commission and its staff, arbitrators, emergency arbitrators, and other relevant personnel engaged by the arbitral tribunal in the arbitration proceedings shall not be liable to any person for any acts, including negligence, acts, or omissions, in connection with the arbitration under these Rules, and shall not be required to give testimony, unless otherwise provided by the law applicable to the arbitration.


除非仲裁适用法律另有规定，仲裁委员会及其工作人员、仲裁员、紧急仲裁员以及仲裁庭在仲裁程序中邀请的相关人员，不就本规则项下仲裁有关的任何行为，包括过失、作为和不作为，对任何人承担民事责任，亦不负有作证义务。

### Article 87 — Interpretation of Rules
### 第八十七条 — 规则的解释

1. The headings of the articles in these Rules shall not be used for the purpose of interpreting the meaning of the provisions.


（一）本规则各条款的标题不应用于解释条文含义。

2. These Rules shall be interpreted by the Arbitration Commission.


（二）本规则由仲裁委员会解释。

### Article 88 — Entry into Force of Rules
### 第八十八条 — 规则的施行

These Rules shall come into force as of January 1, 2024. For cases administered by the Arbitration Commission and its sub-committees/arbitration centers before the entry into force of these Rules, the arbitration rules applicable at the time of acceptance of the case shall continue to apply. However, these Rules may also apply if both parties agree.


本规则自2024年1月1日起施行。本规则施行前仲裁委员会及其分会/仲裁中心管理的案件，仍适用受理案件时适用的仲裁规则。双方当事人同意的，也可以适用本规则。

*This concludes the CIETAC Arbitration Rules (2024), comprising 88 Articles across 7 Chapters, with Appendices covering the list of sub-committees/arbitration centers, fee schedules, and emergency arbitrator procedures.*

*以上为中国国际经济贸易仲裁委员会仲裁规则（2024年版）全文，共88条，分为7章，附件包括分会/仲裁中心名册、仲裁费用表和紧急仲裁员程序。*

**Note:** The Chinese text above represents the authoritative version of the CIETAC Arbitration Rules (2024), originally enacted in Chinese. The English translation is provided for reference. In case of any discrepancy between the Chinese and English versions, the Chinese version shall prevail.

**Fresa Li:**

The 2024 CIETAC Arbitration Rules represent the latest milestone in Chinese arbitration practice and a concentrated embodiment of Chinese institutional arbitration's internationalization. What distinguishes the 2024 edition is its capacity for localized adaptation of international rules—preserving core features of Chinese arbitration practice under the Arbitration Law framework while embracing globally accepted procedural approaches. In Case No. 1146 (an EPC engineering dispute), I found CIETAC's multi-tiered tribunal formation mechanism particularly effective: the Chairman's appointment authority under Article 29 provides a well-considered default that avoids deadlock in multi-arbitrator panels, a common pain point in large-scale disputes. The CIETAC-HK model under Article 82 offers a unique bridge for disputes with both Mainland China and international elements.

---


**Dr. Venessa:**

CIETAC 2024 Rules most distinctive technical feature is Article 8(2) — electronic service may be prioritized for serving arbitration documents — and Article 11 online filing system. CIETAC handles the most technology disputes among the ten institutions an advantage determined by China digital economy scale. But electronic first is infrastructure-level modernization not decision-governance innovation.

CIETAC deeper dilemma: the dispute types it handles (e-commerce data compliance platform algorithm disputes) are precisely the areas that most urgently need Agent arbitration yet its rules still assume parties have symmetrical technological capabilities. In the digital world severe capability asymmetry exists between individual users and platforms — platform AI systems can generate legal arguments in real time while individuals may lack equivalent tools. CIETAC provides no procedural safeguards for this asymmetry. Additionally CIETAC Article 2(7) authorizes administration of ad hoc arbitration offering a possible institutional interface for Agent arbitration experiments — if parties agree to Agent arbitration can CIETAC provide administrative services under the ad hoc framework? This direction is worth exploring.

------------------------------------------------------------

## Chapter 7: WIPO Arbitration and Mediation Center
## 第七章：世界知识产权组织仲裁与调解中心

### Institutional Profile

- **Full Name / 全称:** World Intellectual Property Organization / 世界知识产权组织
- **Abbreviation / 简称:** WIPO / 世界知识产权组织
- **Arbitration Center / 仲裁中心:** WIPO Arbitration and Mediation Center / WIPO仲裁与调解中心
- **Headquarters / 总部:** Geneva, Switzerland / 瑞士日内瓦
- **Current Rules Version / 现行规则版本:** WIPO Arbitration Rules (Effective from July 1, 2021) / WIPO仲裁规则（2021年7月1日生效）
- **Official Link / 官方网站:** https://www.wipo.int/amc/en/arbitration/rules/

### Arbitration Rules (English Original)

**WIPO Arbitration Rules**
**(Effective from July 1, 2021)**

**WIPO仲裁规则**
**（2021年7月1日生效）**

## I. GENERAL PROVISIONS
## 第一编 总则

### Article 1
### 第1条

#### Abbreviated Expressions
#### 缩略语

**Article 1**
In these Rules:

**"Arbitration Agreement" means an agreement by the parties to submit to arbitration all or certain disputes which have arisen or which may arise between them; an Arbitration Agreement may be in the form of an arbitration clause in a contract or in the form of a separate contract;**

"仲裁协议"指当事人约定将他们之间已发生或可能发生的全部或某些争议提交仲裁的协议；仲裁协议可以采取合同中的仲裁条款形式，也可以采取单独合同的形式；

**"Claimant" means the party initiating an arbitration;**

"申请人"指提起仲裁的一方当事人；

**"Respondent" means the party against which the arbitration is initiated, as named in the Request for Arbitration;**

"被申请人"指仲裁申请书中指明的被提起仲裁的一方当事人；

**"WIPO" means the World Intellectual Property Organization;**

"WIPO"指世界知识产权组织；

**"Center" means the WIPO Arbitration and Mediation Center.**

"中心"指WIPO仲裁与调解中心。

### Article 2
### 第2条

#### Scope of Application of Rules
#### 规则的适用范围

**Article 2**
Where an Arbitration Agreement provides for arbitration under the WIPO Arbitration Rules, these Rules shall be deemed to form part of that Arbitration Agreement and the dispute shall be settled in accordance with these Rules, as in effect on the date of the commencement of the arbitration, unless the parties have agreed otherwise.

### Article 3
### 第3条

**Article 3**
(a) These Rules shall govern the arbitration, except that, where any of these Rules is in conflict with a provision of the law applicable to the arbitration from which the parties cannot derogate, that provision shall prevail.

(b) The law applicable to the arbitration shall be determined in accordance with Article 61(b).

### Article 4
### 第4条

#### Notices and Periods of Time
#### 通知与期限

**Article 4**
(a) Any notice or other communication that may or is required to be given under these Rules shall be in writing and shall be delivered by email or other means of electronic communication that provide a record thereof, unless a party decides to use also expedited postal or courier service.

(b) A party's last known residence or place of business shall be a valid address for the purpose of any notice or other communication in the absence of any notification of a change by that party. Communications may in any event be addressed to a party in the manner stipulated or, failing such a stipulation, according to the practice followed in the course of the dealings between the parties.

(c) For the purpose of determining the date of commencement of a time limit, a notice or other communication shall be deemed to have been received on the day it is delivered in accordance with paragraphs (a) and (b) of this Article.

(d) For the purpose of determining compliance with a time limit, a notice or other communication shall be deemed to have been sent, made or transmitted if it is dispatched, in accordance with paragraphs (a) and (b) of this Article, prior to or on the day of the expiration of the time limit.

(e) For the purpose of calculating a period of time under these Rules, such period shall begin to run on the day following the day when a notice or other communication is received. If the last day of such period is an official holiday or a non-business day at the residence or place of business of the addressee, the period is extended until the first business day which follows. Official holidays or non-business days occurring during the running of the period of time are included in calculating the period.

(f) The parties may agree to reduce or extend the periods of time referred to in Articles 11, 15(b), 16(b), 17(b), 17(c), 18, 19(b)(iii), 41(a) and 42(a).

(g) The Center may, at the request of a party or on its own motion, extend the periods of time referred to in Articles 11, 15(b), 16(b), 17(b), 17(c), 18, 19(b)(iii), 69(d), 70(e) and 72(e).

### Article 5
### 第5条

#### Documents Required to be Submitted to the Center
#### 需向中心提交的文件

**Article 5**
(a) Until the notification by the Center of the establishment of the Tribunal, any written statement, notice or other communication required or allowed under these Rules shall be submitted by a party to the Center and a copy thereof shall at the same time be transmitted by that party to the other party.

(b) Where a party decides to use expedited postal or courier service for the transmission of any written statement, notice or other communication so sent to the Center, it shall send it in a number of copies equal to the number required to provide one copy for each envisaged arbitrator and one for the Center.

(c) After the notification by the Center of the establishment of the Tribunal, any written statements, notices or other communications shall be submitted by a party directly to the Tribunal and a copy thereof shall at the same time be supplied by that party to the other party.

(d) The Tribunal shall send to the Center a copy of each order or other decision that it makes.

## II. COMMENCEMENT OF THE ARBITRATION
## 第二编 仲裁的启动

### Article 6
### 第6条

#### Request for Arbitration
#### 仲裁申请书

**Article 6**
The Claimant shall transmit the Request for Arbitration to the Center and to the Respondent.

### Article 7
### 第7条

**Article 7**
The date of commencement of the arbitration shall be the date on which the Request for Arbitration is received by the Center.

### Article 8
### 第8条

**Article 8**
The Center shall inform the Claimant and the Respondent of the receipt by it of the Request for Arbitration and of the date of the commencement of the arbitration.

### Article 9
### 第9条

**Article 9**
The Request for Arbitration shall contain:

(i) a demand that the dispute be referred to arbitration under the WIPO Arbitration Rules;

(ii) the names, addresses and telephone, e-mail or other communication references of the parties and of the representative of the Claimant;

(iii) a copy of the Arbitration Agreement and, if applicable, any separate choice-of-law clause;

(iv) a brief description of the nature and circumstances of the dispute, including an indication of the rights and property involved and the nature of any technology involved;

(v) a statement of the relief sought and an indication, to the extent possible, of any amount claimed;

(vi) any nomination that is required by, or observations that the Claimant considers useful in connection with, Articles 14 to 20; and

(vii) if applicable, the identity of any third party funder. If a funding agreement is concluded at a later stage of the proceedings, the identity of the third party funder shall be disclosed promptly to the parties, the Center, and the Tribunal.

### Article 10
### 第10条

**Article 10**
The Request for Arbitration may also be accompanied by the Statement of Claim referred to in Article 41.

### Article 11
### 第11条

#### Answer to the Request
#### 对仲裁申请书的答辩

**Article 11**
(a) Within 30 days from the date on which the Respondent receives the Request for Arbitration from the Claimant, the Respondent shall address to the Center and to the Claimant an Answer to the Request which shall contain comments on any of the elements in the Request for Arbitration and may include indications of any counter-claim or set-off.

(b) The Answer to the Request shall contain, if applicable, the identity of any third party funder. If a funding agreement is concluded at a later stage of the proceedings, the identity of the third party funder shall be disclosed promptly to the parties, the Center, and the Tribunal.

### Article 12
### 第12条

**Article 12**
If the Claimant has filed a Statement of Claim with the Request for Arbitration pursuant to Article 10, the Answer to the Request may also be accompanied by the Statement of Defense referred to in Article 42.

### Article 13
### 第13条

#### Representation
#### 代理

**Article 13**
(a) The parties may be represented by persons of their choice, irrespective of, in particular, nationality or professional qualification. The names, addresses and telephone, e-mail or other communication references of representatives shall be communicated to the Center, the other party and, after its establishment, the Tribunal.

(b) Each party shall ensure that its representatives have sufficient time available to enable the arbitration to proceed expeditiously.

(c) The parties may also be assisted by persons of their choice.

## III. COMPOSITION AND ESTABLISHMENT OF THE TRIBUNAL
## 第三编 仲裁庭的组成与设立

### Article 14
### 第14条

#### Number and Appointment of Arbitrators
#### 仲裁员人数与指定

**Article 14**
(a) The Tribunal shall consist of such number of arbitrators as has been agreed by the parties.

(b) Where the parties have not agreed on the number of arbitrators, the Tribunal shall consist of a sole arbitrator, except where the Center in its discretion determines that, in view of all the circumstances of the case, a Tribunal composed of three members is appropriate.

(c) Any nomination of an arbitrator made by the parties pursuant to Articles 16, 17 and 18 shall be confirmed by the Center provided that the requirements of Articles 22 and 23 have been met. The appointment shall be effective upon the Center's notification to the parties.

### Article 15
### 第15条

#### Appointment Pursuant to Procedure Agreed Upon by the Parties
#### 根据当事人约定的程序指定

**Article 15**
(a) If the parties have agreed on a procedure for the appointment of the arbitrator or arbitrators, that procedure shall be followed.

(b) If the Tribunal has not been established pursuant to such procedure within the period of time agreed upon by the parties or, in the absence of such an agreed period of time, within 45 days after the commencement of the arbitration, the Tribunal shall be established or completed, as the case may be, in accordance with Article 19.

### Article 16
### 第16条

#### Appointment of a Sole Arbitrator
#### 独任仲裁员的指定

**Article 16**
(a) Where a sole arbitrator is to be appointed and the parties have not agreed on an appointment procedure, the sole arbitrator shall be nominated jointly by the parties.

(b) If the nomination of the sole arbitrator is not made within the period of time agreed upon by the parties or, in the absence of such an agreed period of time, within 30 days after the commencement of the arbitration, the sole arbitrator shall be appointed in accordance with Article 19.

### Article 17
### 第17条

#### Appointment of Three Arbitrators
#### 三名仲裁员的指定

**Article 17**
(a) Where three arbitrators are to be appointed and the parties have not agreed upon an appointment procedure, the arbitrators shall be appointed in accordance with this Article.

(b) The Claimant shall nominate an arbitrator in its Request for Arbitration. The Respondent shall nominate an arbitrator within 30 days from the date on which it receives the Request for Arbitration. The two arbitrators shall, within 20 days after the appointment of the second arbitrator, nominate a third arbitrator, who shall be the presiding arbitrator.

(c) Notwithstanding paragraph (b), where three arbitrators are to be appointed as a result of the exercise of the discretion of the Center under Article 14(b), the Claimant shall, by notice to the Center and to the Respondent, nominate an arbitrator within 15 days after the receipt by it of notification by the Center that the Tribunal is to be composed of three arbitrators. The Respondent shall nominate an arbitrator within 30 days after the receipt by it of the said notification. The two arbitrators shall, within 20 days after the appointment of the second arbitrator, nominate a third arbitrator, who shall be the presiding arbitrator.

(d) If the nomination of any arbitrator is not made within the applicable period of time referred to in the preceding paragraphs, that arbitrator shall be appointed in accordance with Article 19.

### Article 18
### 第18条

#### Appointment of Three Arbitrators in Case of Multiple Claimants or Respondents
#### 多名申请人或被申请人时三名仲裁员的指定

**Article 18**
Where:
(i) there are multiple Claimants and/or multiple Respondents; and
(ii) three arbitrators are to be appointed;

the multiple Claimants, jointly, in the Request for Arbitration, shall nominate an arbitrator, and/or the multiple Respondents, jointly, within 30 days after receiving the Request for Arbitration, shall nominate an arbitrator, as the case may be. If a joint nomination is not made within the applicable period of time, the Center shall appoint one or both arbitrators. The two arbitrators shall, within 20 days after the appointment of the second arbitrator, nominate a third arbitrator, who shall be the presiding arbitrator.

### Article 19
### 第19条

#### Default Appointment
#### 默认指定

**Article 19**
(a) If a party has failed to nominate an arbitrator as required under Articles 15, 17 or 18, the Center shall forthwith make the appointment.

(b) If the sole or presiding arbitrator has not been appointed as required under Articles 15, 16, 17 or 18, the appointment shall take place in accordance with the following procedure:

(i) The Center shall send to each party an identical list of candidates. The list shall normally comprise the names of at least three candidates in alphabetical order. The list shall include or be accompanied by a statement of each candidate's qualifications. If the parties have agreed on any particular qualifications, the list shall contain the names of candidates that satisfy those qualifications.

(ii) Each party shall have the right to delete the name of any candidate or candidates to whose appointment it objects and shall number any remaining candidates in order of preference.

(iii) Each party shall return the marked list to the Center within 20 days after the date on which the list is received by it. Any party failing to return a marked list within that period of time shall be deemed to have assented to all candidates appearing on the list.

(iv) As soon as possible after receipt by it of the lists from the parties, or failing this, after the expiration of the period of time specified in the previous subparagraph, the Center shall, taking into account the preferences and objections expressed by the parties, appoint a person from the list as sole or presiding arbitrator.

(v) If the lists which have been returned do not show a person who is acceptable as arbitrator to both parties, the Center shall be authorized to appoint the sole or presiding arbitrator.
