##### 第7条：程序原则

1. The Arbitral Tribunal shall ensure equal treatment and the right to be heard.

##### § 8: Confidentiality
##### 第8条：保密性

1. Unless otherwise agreed, arbitrators and the Court shall maintain confidentiality.

##### § 9: Consolidation
##### 第9条：合并仲裁

1. Two or more proceedings may be consolidated if:

##### § 10: Joinder
##### 第10条：追加当事人

1. A third party may be admitted upon application and consent of the parties.

##### § 11: Service
##### 第11条：送达

1. Written communications shall be served personally, by registered post, or courier.

##### § 13: Language
##### 第13条：语言

1. Unless otherwise agreed, the language of the proceeding shall be Polish.

##### § 14: Place of Arbitration
##### 第14条：仲裁地

1. Unless otherwise agreed, the place of arbitration shall be Warsaw.

#### Chapter III: Arbitrators
#### 第三章：仲裁员

##### § 15: Qualifications
##### 第15条：资格

1. Arbitrators must be independent and impartial.

##### § 16: List of Arbitrators
##### 第16条：仲裁员名册

1. The Court maintains a List of Arbitrators.

##### § 18: Number
##### 第18条：人数

1. Unless otherwise agreed:

##### § 19: Appointment
##### 第19条：指定

1. For three arbitrators, each party appoints one arbitrator.

##### § 20: Default Appointment
##### 第20条：缺席指定

1. The Arbitral Council shall appoint an arbitrator if a party fails to do so.

##### § 21: Termination of Appointment
##### 第21条：指定的终止

1. Upon termination, a new arbitrator shall be appointed.

##### § 22: Challenge
##### 第22条：回避

1. An arbitrator may be challenged for circumstances raising doubts.

##### § 23: Effects of Challenge
##### 第23条：回避的效果

1. If upheld, the arbitrator shall be replaced.

#### Chapter IV: Proceedings
#### 第四章：程序

##### § 24: Statement of Claim
##### 第24条：诉状

1. The Claimant shall submit a statement of claim containing:

##### § 25: Service of Statement
##### 第25条：诉状的送达

1. The Secretary General shall serve the statement on the Respondent.

##### § 26: Statement of Defense
##### 第26条：答辩状

1. Within 30 days of service, the Respondent shall submit a statement of defense.

##### § 27: Evidence
##### 第27条：证据

1. Each party shall prove the facts on which it relies.

##### § 28: Hearings
##### 第28条：庭审

1. The Arbitral Tribunal shall hold hearings unless the parties agree otherwise.

##### § 29: Interim Measures
##### 第29条：临时措施

1. The Arbitral Tribunal may order interim measures upon request.

##### § 30: Close of Proceedings
##### 第30条：程序的终结

1. Upon completion of submissions and hearings, the Arbitral Tribunal shall close the proceedings.

##### § 31: Deliberations
##### 第31条：评议

1. The Arbitral Tribunal shall deliberate in private.

#### Chapter V: Award
#### 第五章：裁决

##### § 32: Making of Award
##### 第32条：裁决的作出

1. The Arbitral Tribunal shall make the award as soon as possible.

##### § 33: Effect
##### 第33条：效力

1. Awards are final and binding.

##### § 34: Correction
##### 第34条：更正

1. Within 30 days, a party may request correction of clerical errors.

##### § 35: Interpretation
##### 第35条：解释

1. Within 30 days, a party may request interpretation.

##### § 36: Settlement
##### 第36条：和解

1. If settled, the Arbitral Tribunal shall issue an award on agreed terms.

#### Chapter VI: Costs
#### 第六章：费用

##### § 37: Costs of Arbitration
##### 第37条：仲裁费用

1. The Arbitral Tribunal shall fix the costs of arbitration.

##### § 38: Advances
##### 第38条：预付款

1. The Secretary General shall fix advances for costs.

### Standard Arbitration Clause
### 标准仲裁条款

"Any disputes arising out of or in connection with this contract shall be finally settled by arbitration under the Arbitration Rules of the Court of Arbitration at the Polish Chamber of Commerce by one or three arbitrators appointed in accordance with the said Rules. The place of arbitration shall be Warsaw."

## S9: SVAMC Silicon Valley Arbitration and Mediation Center
## S9: 硅谷仲裁与调解中心（SVAMC）

### Institution Overview
### 机构简介

The Silicon Valley Arbitration and Mediation Center (SVAMC) serves the global technology sector by promoting business-practical dispute resolution. SVAMC works with leading technology companies, law firms, ADR institutions, and universities to provide educational programming and resources for technology-related disputes. SVAMC is particularly known for publishing the Tech List of leading technology neutrals and the groundbreaking Guidelines on the Use of Artificial Intelligence in Arbitration.

### Guidelines on the Use of Artificial Intelligence in Arbitration (2024)
### 仲裁中人工智能使用指南（2024年）

**First Edition, Published April 30, 2024**
**第一版，2024年4月30日发布**

**IMPORTANT:** These AI Guidelines are specifically significant for DWAC (Digital World Arbitration Center) research as they represent the first comprehensive framework for AI use in international arbitration.

#### Foreword
#### 前言

The SVAMC Guidelines on the Use of Artificial Intelligence in Arbitration introduce a principle-based framework for the use of AI tools in arbitration at a time when such technologies are becoming increasingly powerful and popular.

#### Members of the SVAMC AI Task Force
#### SVAMC人工智能工作组组员

**Chair:** Benjamin I. Malek, T.H.E. Chambers, New York

### Preliminary Provisions
### 初步规定

#### Application of the Guidelines
#### 指南的适用

These Guidelines shall apply when and to the extent that the parties have so agreed and/or following a decision by an arbitral tribunal or an arbitral institution to adopt these Guidelines.

### Part I: Guidelines for All Participants in Arbitrations
### 第一部分：适用于仲裁所有参与者的指南

#### GUIDELINE 1: Understanding the Uses, Limitations, and Risks of AI Applications
#### 指南1：理解人工智能应用的使用、局限性和风险

1. All participants involved in arbitration proceedings who use AI tools in preparation for or during an arbitration are responsible for familiarising themselves with the AI tool's intended uses and should adapt their use accordingly.

#### GUIDELINE 2: Safeguarding Confidentiality
#### 指南2：保密保障

All participants in international arbitration are responsible for ensuring their use of AI tools is consistent with their obligations to safeguard confidential information (including privileged, private, secret, or otherwise protected data). They should not submit confidential information to any AI tool without appropriate vetting and authorisation.

#### GUIDELINE 3: Disclosure
#### 指南3：披露

1. Disclosure that AI tools were used in connection with an arbitration is not necessary as a general matter.

============================================================


# 全书总评 / Overall Commentary

## Dr. Venessa — Summary Commentary


All ten institutions face the same structural fracture in the Agent era: they all operate at the authorization layer — arbitrator authority derives from institutional confirmation and party choice — but the other three layers are entirely absent: capability qualification certification bias accountability auditing and reversibility guarantees. The SVAMC AI Guidelines are the only institutional effort attempting to fill this vacuum but they are guidelines not rules and Guideline 6 non-delegation principle while protecting procedural due process also closes the door on institutional exploration of Arbitrator-Agents.

DWAC mission is not to patch within the existing framework but to build new institutions for the Agent era. The four-dimensional framework (capability qualification bias accountability authorization endorsement reversibility) is the systematic response to this structural fracture.

*本参考书编纂于2026年6月，为DWAC研究系列成果之一。*
